# Admin SDK Mock Milestone 1 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the read-only milestone of a mock Google Admin SDK that answers on the real Google hostnames, seeds K-12 domains up to Chicago Public Schools size, and reproduces Google quota, latency, and fault behavior.

**Architecture:** One Node 24 process serves HTTPS for the four Google hostnames and plain HTTP for a control API. Each seeded domain lives in its own SQLite file through `node:sqlite`. Requests flow through one dispatch pipeline: route match, token authentication, faults, quota admission, handler, `fields` filtering, latency delay.

**Tech Stack:** Node 24 (type stripping, `node:test`, `node:sqlite`), TypeScript (type checks only), `node-forge` (certificates), ESLint, Prettier, Docker. Compatibility tests use Google's `google-auth-library`, `@googleapis/admin`, and `@googleapis/chromemanagement`.

**Spec:** `docs/specs/2026-10-09-admin-sdk-mock-design.md`. Read it before any task.

## Global Constraints

- Repository: `~/projects/admin-sdk-mock`, remote `CampusCommander/admin-sdk-mock` (private). Never add files to `~/projects/campus-commander`.
- Runtime: Node 24.11 or later. Local development uses Node 24.19.0.
- Node runs `.ts` files directly through type stripping. Never use `enum`, `namespace`, parameter properties, decorators, or `import x = require()`.
- Relative imports end in `.ts`. Type-only imports use `import type`.
- The only runtime dependency is `node-forge`. Install every package with `--save-exact`.
- Google hostnames: `oauth2.googleapis.com`, `admin.googleapis.com`, `www.googleapis.com`, `chromemanagement.googleapis.com`.
- Seeded domains use the reserved `.test` top-level domain.
- No real Google credential enters the repository, test output, or evidence. The parity check reads the credential path from an environment variable.
- Directory errors use `{"error":{"code","message","errors":[{"message","domain","reason"}]}}`. Chrome Management errors use `{"error":{"code","message","status"}}`.
- Default quota: 2,400 requests per minute per bucket, 125% ceiling, heavy above 112.5%, flag at 30 over minutes or 10 heavy minutes in 60, flag lasts 60 minutes.
- Page limits: Chrome devices 300, users 500, telemetry 1,000.
- Documentation prose follows the repository writing rules: active voice, no hedging words, no semicolons, no contractions, at most 20 words per instruction sentence.
- Every commit message ends with the trailer `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## Review Focus

- A page token from before a domain reset must return `400`, never rows from the new data. Task 11 pins this with a reset test.
- OU paths with spaces must work in `orgUnitPath` path segments, query parameters, and `users.list` queries. Tasks 10, 11, and 12 pin this.
- Batch bodies with LF line endings, absolute part URLs, or a trailing `HTTP/1.1` must parse. Task 14 pins this.
- A token whose domain was deleted must return `401`, never a crash. Task 8 pins this.
- A time-scale change during a run must not leave quota buckets with stale window lengths. Task 15 pins this.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing random tests**

`src/random.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { createRandom } from './random.ts';

const draws = (seed: string, count = 5) => {
  const random = createRandom(seed);
  return Array.from({ length: count }, () => random.next());
};

test('the same seed produces the same sequence', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(draws('district-1'), draws('district-1'));
});

test('different seeds produce different sequences', () => {
  assert.notDeepEqual(draws('district-1'), draws('district-2'));
});

test('a fork depends on its label, not on the parent position', () => {
  const advanced = createRandom('s');
  advanced.next();
  advanced.next();
  const fresh = createRandom('s');
  assert.equal(
    advanced.fork('device/1').next(),
    fresh.fork('device/1').next(),
  );
  assert.notEqual(
    fresh.fork('device/1').next(),
    fresh.fork('device/2').next(),
  );
});

test('int stays inside inclusive bounds and reaches both ends', () => {
  const random = createRandom('bounds');
  const seen = new Set<number>();
  for (let i = 0; i < 1000; i++) seen.add(random.int(1, 3));
  assert.deepEqual([...seen].sort(), [1, 2, 3]);
});

test('chance honors zero and one', () => {
  const random = createRandom('chance');
  for (let i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
    assert.equal(random.chance(0), false);
    assert.equal(random.chance(1), true);
  }
});

test('weighted never returns a zero-weight entry', () => {
  const random = createRandom('weighted');
  for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++)
    assert.equal(
      random.weighted([
        ['a', 0],
        ['b', 1],
      ]),
      'b',
    );
});

test('normal draws center on zero with unit spread', () => {
  const random = createRandom('normal');
  const values = Array.from({ length: 10_000 }, () => random.normal());
  const mean = values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0) / values.length;
  const variance =
    values.reduce((sum, value) => sum + (value - mean) ** 2, 0) /
    values.length;
  assert.ok(Math.abs(mean) < 0.05, `mean ${mean}`);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(Math.sqrt(variance) - 1) < 0.05, `sd ${variance}`);
});

test('string and uuid use the requested formats', () => {
  const random = createRandom('format');
  assert.match(random.string(8), /^[0-9a-z]{8}$/);
  assert.match(random.string(4, 'AB'), /^[AB]{4}$/);
  assert.match(
    random.uuid(),
    /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-4[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/,
  );
});

test('pick rejects an empty list', () => {
  assert.throws(() => createRandom('empty').pick([]), RangeError);
});
```

`src/clock.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { fakeClock, parseTiming, systemClock, TimingError } from './clock.ts';

test('a fake sleep resolves only after the clock advances past it', async () => {
  const clock = fakeClock(1_000);
  let done = false;
  const sleeping = clock.sleep(500).then(() => {
    done = true;
  });
  await clock.advance(499);
  assert.equal(done, false);
  await clock.advance(1);
  await sleeping;
  assert.equal(done, true);
  assert.equal(clock.now(), 1_500);
});

test('fake sleepers wake in time order', async () => {
  const clock = fakeClock(0);
  const order: number[] = [];
  const late = clock.sleep(30).then(() => order.push(30));
  const early = clock.sleep(10).then(() => order.push(10));
  await clock.advance(100);
  await Promise.all([late, early]);
  assert.deepEqual(order, [10, 30]);
  assert.equal(clock.pending(), 0);
});

test('a zero sleep resolves at once on both clocks', async () => {
  await systemClock.sleep(0);
  await fakeClock().sleep(0);
});

test('parseTiming applies defaults and rejects bad values', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(parseTiming(undefined, undefined), {
    scale: 1,
    delays: true,
  });
  assert.deepEqual(parseTiming('60', 'off'), { scale: 60, delays: false });
  assert.deepEqual(parseTiming('1', 'false'), { scale: 1, delays: false });
  assert.throws(() => parseTiming('0', undefined), TimingError);
  assert.throws(() => parseTiming('abc', undefined), TimingError);
  assert.throws(() => parseTiming('200000', undefined), TimingError);
  assert.throws(() => parseTiming('1', 'maybe'), TimingError);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the random streams**

`src/random.ts`:

```ts
/** A deterministic random stream. The same seed always yields the same sequence. */
export interface Random {
  readonly seed: string;
  /** A number in [0, 1). */
  next(): number;
  /** A whole number in [min, max], inclusive. */
  int(min: number, max: number): number;
  pick<T>(items: readonly T[]): T;
  weighted<T>(entries: readonly (readonly [T, number])[]): T;
  chance(probability: number): boolean;
  /** A standard normal draw. */
  normal(): number;
  string(length: number, alphabet?: string): string;
  uuid(): string;
  /** An independent stream that depends only on this seed and the label. */
  fork(label: string): Random;
}

const LOWER_ALNUM = '0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz';
const HEX = '0123456789abcdef';

function cyrb128(text: string): [number, number, number, number] {
  let h1 = 1779033703;
  let h2 = 3144134277;
  let h3 = 1013904242;
  let h4 = 2773480762;
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const k = text.charCodeAt(i);
    h1 = h2 ^ Math.imul(h1 ^ k, 597399067);
    h2 = h3 ^ Math.imul(h2 ^ k, 2869860233);
    h3 = h4 ^ Math.imul(h3 ^ k, 951274213);
    h4 = h1 ^ Math.imul(h4 ^ k, 2716044179);
  }
  h1 = Math.imul(h3 ^ (h1 >>> 18), 597399067);
  h2 = Math.imul(h4 ^ (h2 >>> 22), 2869860233);
  h3 = Math.imul(h1 ^ (h3 >>> 17), 951274213);
  h4 = Math.imul(h2 ^ (h4 >>> 19), 2716044179);
  h1 ^= h2 ^ h3 ^ h4;
  h2 ^= h1;
  h3 ^= h1;
  h4 ^= h1;
  return [h1 >>> 0, h2 >>> 0, h3 >>> 0, h4 >>> 0];
}

function sfc32(a: number, b: number, c: number, d: number): () => number {
  return () => {
    a |= 0;
    b |= 0;
    c |= 0;
    d |= 0;
    const t = (((a + b) | 0) + d) | 0;
    d = (d + 1) | 0;
    a = b ^ (b >>> 9);
    b = (c + (c << 3)) | 0;
    c = (c << 21) | (c >>> 11);
    c = (c + t) | 0;
    return (t >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

export function createRandom(seed: string): Random {
  const next = sfc32(...cyrb128(seed));
  const random: Random = {
    seed,
    next,
    int(min, max) {
      return min + Math.floor(next() * (max - min + 1));
    },
    pick(items) {
      if (items.length === 0)
        throw new RangeError('pick() needs at least one item.');
      return items[Math.floor(next() * items.length)];
    },
    weighted(entries) {
      const total = entries.reduce((sum, [, weight]) => sum + weight, 0);
      let roll = next() * total;
      for (const [value, weight] of entries) {
        roll -= weight;
        if (roll < 0) return value;
      }
      for (let i = entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--)
        if (entries[i][1] > 0) return entries[i][0];
      throw new RangeError('weighted() needs a positive weight.');
    },
    chance(probability) {
      return next() < probability;
    },
    normal() {
      const u = 1 - next();
      const v = next();
      return Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * v);
    },
    string(length, alphabet = LOWER_ALNUM) {
      let out = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < length; i++)
        out += alphabet[Math.floor(next() * alphabet.length)];
      return out;
    },
    uuid() {
      const h = random.string(32, HEX);
      const variant = '89ab'[Math.floor(next() * 4)];
      return `${h.slice(0, 8)}-${h.slice(8, 12)}-4${h.slice(13, 16)}-${variant}${h.slice(17, 20)}-${h.slice(20, 32)}`;
    },
    fork(label) {
      return createRandom(`${seed}/${label}`);
    },
  };
  return random;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the clock**

`src/clock.ts`:

```ts
/** Time source. Tests replace it with a fake clock. */
export interface Clock {
  now(): number;
  sleep(ms: number): Promise<void>;
}

/** Global time compression. `scale` divides every window, penalty, and delay. */
export interface Timing {
  scale: number;
  delays: boolean;
}

export class TimingError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'TimingError';
  }
}

export const systemClock: Clock = {
  now: () => Date.now(),
  sleep: (ms) =>
    ms <= 0
      ? Promise.resolve()
      : new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms)),
};

export interface FakeClock extends Clock {
  advance(ms: number): Promise<void>;
  pending(): number;
}

export function fakeClock(start = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 12)): FakeClock {
  let time = start;
  const timers: { at: number; resolve: () => void }[] = [];
  return {
    now: () => time,
    sleep(ms) {
      if (ms <= 0) return Promise.resolve();
      return new Promise((resolve) => {
        timers.push({ at: time + ms, resolve });
      });
    },
    async advance(ms) {
      const target = time + ms;
      for (;;) {
        timers.sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at);
        const next = timers[0];
        if (!next || next.at > target) break;
        timers.shift();
        time = next.at;
        next.resolve();
        await new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve));
      }
      time = target;
    },
    pending: () => timers.length,
  };
}

export const MAX_SCALE = 100_000;

/** Read the time scale and the delay switch from environment strings. */
export function parseTiming(
  scale: string | undefined,
  delays: string | undefined,
): Timing {
  const parsedScale = scale === undefined || scale === '' ? 1 : Number(scale);
  if (
    !Number.isFinite(parsedScale) ||
    parsedScale < 1 ||
    parsedScale > MAX_SCALE
  )
    throw new TimingError(`The time scale must be from 1 to ${MAX_SCALE}.`);
  const value = (delays ?? 'on').toLowerCase();
  if (!['on', 'off', 'true', 'false', '1', '0'].includes(value))
    throw new TimingError('The delay switch must be on or off.');
  return {
    scale: parsedScale,
    delays: ['on', 'true', '1'].includes(value),
  };
}
```

`src/ca.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { X509Certificate } from 'node:crypto';
import { once } from 'node:events';
import { mkdtempSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import https from 'node:https';
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { createCertificates, ensureCertificates, GOOGLE_HOSTS } from './ca.ts';

test('the server certificate covers every Google hostname and chains to the CA', () => {
  const { caCert, serverCert } = createCertificates();
  const ca = new X509Certificate(caCert);
  const server = new X509Certificate(serverCert);
  for (const host of GOOGLE_HOSTS) assert.equal(server.checkHost(host), host);
  assert.equal(server.checkHost('evil.example'), undefined);
  assert.equal(server.verify(ca.publicKey), true);
  assert.equal(ca.ca, true);
  assert.equal(server.ca, false);
});

test('a client that trusts only the mock CA completes a handshake for each hostname', async () => {
  const certs = createCertificates();
  const server = https.createServer(
    { cert: certs.serverCert, key: certs.serverKey },
    (_request, response) => response.end('ok'),
  );
  server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  await once(server, 'listening');
  const { port } = server.address() as AddressInfo;
  try {
    for (const host of GOOGLE_HOSTS) {
      const body = await new Promise<string>((resolve, reject) => {
        https
          .get(
            {
              host: '127.0.0.1',
              port,
              path: '/',
              servername: host,
              headers: { host },
              ca: certs.caCert,
            },
            (response) => {
              let text = '';
              response.on('data', (chunk) => (text += chunk));
              response.on('end', () => resolve(text));
            },
          )
          .on('error', reject);
      });
      assert.equal(body, 'ok');
    }
  } finally {
    server.close();
  }
});

test('ensureCertificates creates the files once and then reuses them', () => {
  const directory = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'mock-ca-'));
  const first = ensureCertificates(directory);
  const second = ensureCertificates(directory);
  assert.equal(second.caCert, first.caCert);
  assert.equal(second.serverKey, first.serverKey);
  assert.equal(statSync(join(directory, 'ca-key.pem')).mode & 0o777, 0o600);
  assert.equal(statSync(join(directory, 'server-key.pem')).mode & 0o777, 0o600);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the certificates**

`src/ca.ts`:

```ts
import { generateKeyPairSync, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import forge from 'node-forge';

export const GOOGLE_HOSTS = [
  'oauth2.googleapis.com',
  'admin.googleapis.com',
  'www.googleapis.com',
  'chromemanagement.googleapis.com',
] as const;

export interface Certificates {
  caCert: string;
  serverCert: string;
  serverKey: string;
}

const DAY = 86_400_000;
const CA_SUBJECT = [
  { name: 'commonName', value: 'Admin SDK Mock CA' },
  { name: 'organizationName', value: 'Campus Commander test infrastructure' },
];

function keyPair(): { privateKey: string; publicKey: string } {
  return generateKeyPairSync('rsa', {
    modulusLength: 2048,
    privateKeyEncoding: { type: 'pkcs1', format: 'pem' },
    publicKeyEncoding: { type: 'spki', format: 'pem' },
  });
}

/** A positive serial number with 16 random bytes. */
function serialNumber(): string {
  return `01${randomBytes(15).toString('hex')}`;
}

/** Create a CA and one server certificate for the four Google hostnames. */
export function createCertificates(
  now = new Date(),
): Certificates & { caKey: string } {
  const ca = keyPair();
  const caKey = forge.pki.privateKeyFromPem(ca.privateKey);
  const caCert = forge.pki.createCertificate();
  caCert.publicKey = forge.pki.publicKeyFromPem(ca.publicKey);
  caCert.serialNumber = serialNumber();
  caCert.validity.notBefore = new Date(now.getTime() - DAY);
  caCert.validity.notAfter = new Date(now.getTime() + 3650 * DAY);
  caCert.setSubject(CA_SUBJECT);
  caCert.setIssuer(CA_SUBJECT);
  caCert.setExtensions([
    { name: 'basicConstraints', cA: true, critical: true },
    { name: 'keyUsage', keyCertSign: true, cRLSign: true, critical: true },
    { name: 'subjectKeyIdentifier' },
  ]);
  caCert.sign(caKey, forge.md.sha256.create());

  const server = keyPair();
  const cert = forge.pki.createCertificate();
  cert.publicKey = forge.pki.publicKeyFromPem(server.publicKey);
  cert.serialNumber = serialNumber();
  cert.validity.notBefore = new Date(now.getTime() - DAY);
  cert.validity.notAfter = new Date(now.getTime() + 825 * DAY);
  cert.setSubject([{ name: 'commonName', value: 'admin.googleapis.com' }]);
  cert.setIssuer(CA_SUBJECT);
  cert.setExtensions([
    { name: 'basicConstraints', cA: false },
    {
      name: 'keyUsage',
      digitalSignature: true,
      keyEncipherment: true,
      critical: true,
    },
    { name: 'extKeyUsage', serverAuth: true },
    {
      name: 'subjectAltName',
      altNames: GOOGLE_HOSTS.map((value) => ({ type: 2, value })),
    },
  ]);
  cert.sign(caKey, forge.md.sha256.create());

  return {
    caCert: forge.pki.certificateToPem(caCert),
    caKey: ca.privateKey,
    serverCert: forge.pki.certificateToPem(cert),
    serverKey: server.privateKey,
  };
}

const FILES = {
  caCert: 'ca.pem',
  caKey: 'ca-key.pem',
  serverCert: 'server.pem',
  serverKey: 'server-key.pem',
} as const;

/** Load the certificates from `directory`, or create them on first start. */
export function ensureCertificates(
  directory: string,
  now = new Date(),
): Certificates {
  const path = (name: keyof typeof FILES) => join(directory, FILES[name]);
  const names = Object.keys(FILES) as (keyof typeof FILES)[];
  if (names.every((name) => existsSync(path(name))))
    return {
      caCert: readFileSync(path('caCert'), 'utf8'),
      serverCert: readFileSync(path('serverCert'), 'utf8'),
      serverKey: readFileSync(path('serverKey'), 'utf8'),
    };
  mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const created = createCertificates(now);
  writeFileSync(path('caCert'), created.caCert, { mode: 0o644 });
  writeFileSync(path('caKey'), created.caKey, { mode: 0o600 });
  writeFileSync(path('serverCert'), created.serverCert, { mode: 0o644 });
  writeFileSync(path('serverKey'), created.serverKey, { mode: 0o600 });
  return {
    caCert: created.caCert,
    serverCert: created.serverCert,
    serverKey: created.serverKey,
  };
}
```

### Task 3: Seed specification and OU layouts

**Files:**
- Create: `src/seed/names.ts`, `src/seed/spec.ts`, `src/seed/layout.ts`
- Test: `src/seed/spec.test.ts`, `src/seed/layout.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createRandom`, `Random` from Task 1.
- Produces: `Tier`, `LayoutFamily`, `Counts`, `Rates`, `SeedSpec`, `ResolvedSpec`, `TIERS`, `DEFAULT_RATES`, `SpecError`, `resolveSpec(input: unknown): ResolvedSpec`.
- Produces: `OuKind`, `DeviceCategory`, `DEVICE_CATEGORIES`, `SchoolLevel`, `OrgUnitNode`, `School`, `Layout`, `MAX_DEPTH = 35`, `MAX_UNITS = 40_000`, `GRADUATION_BASE_YEAR = 2027`, `ouId(seed, path): string`, `buildLayout(spec: ResolvedSpec): Layout`.
- Produces: `FIRST_NAMES`, `LAST_NAMES`, `SCHOOL_NAMESAKES`, `PLACE_NAMES` (readonly string arrays).

`ResolvedSpec` fields: `tier`, `seed`, `districtName`, `slug`, `customerId` (`C0` plus 7 lowercase letters or digits), `primaryDomain` (`<slug>.test`), `studentDomain` (`students.<slug>.test` or the primary domain), `aliasDomain` (`<slug>-k12.test`), `layout`, `counts`, `rates`.

`OrgUnitNode` fields: `orgUnitId` (`id:0` plus 13 characters), `name`, `path`, `parentId`, `parentPath`, `depth` (root is 0), `description`, `kind` (`root`, `container`, `users`, or `devices`).

`School` fields: `index`, `name`, `level`, `grades` (0 is kindergarten), `region`, `weight`, `studentOus` (grade to path), `staffOu`, `deviceOus` (category to path).

`Layout` fields: `root`, `units` (every OU except the root, in creation order), `schools`, `districtStaffOu`, `districtDeviceOu`, `deepUserOus`, `deepDeviceOus`.

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { DEFAULT_RATES, resolveSpec, SpecError, TIERS } from './spec.ts';

test('a tier supplies the default counts and rates', () => {
  const spec = resolveSpec({ tier: 'medium', seed: 'm1' });
  assert.deepEqual(spec.counts, TIERS.medium);
  assert.deepEqual(spec.rates, DEFAULT_RATES);
  assert.equal(spec.tier, 'medium');
});

test('overrides replace individual counts and rates', () => {
  const spec = resolveSpec({
    tier: 'small',
    seed: 'o1',
    counts: { devices: 50 },
    rates: { disabled: 0.5, deepBranches: 0 },
  });
  assert.equal(spec.counts.devices, 50);
  assert.equal(spec.counts.students, TIERS.small.students);
  assert.equal(spec.rates.disabled, 0.5);
  assert.equal(spec.rates.deepBranches, 0);
});

test('the same input resolves to the same specification', () => {
  const input = { tier: 'large', seed: 'same' };
  assert.deepEqual(resolveSpec(input), resolveSpec(input));
});

test('identity fields use the documented formats', () => {
  const spec = resolveSpec({ tier: 'small', seed: 'format' });
  assert.match(spec.customerId, /^C0[0-9a-z]{7}$/);
  assert.equal(spec.primaryDomain, `${spec.slug}.test`);
  assert.equal(spec.aliasDomain, `${spec.slug}-k12.test`);
  assert.ok(
    [spec.primaryDomain, `students.${spec.slug}.test`].includes(
      spec.studentDomain,
    ),
  );
  assert.match(spec.slug, /^[a-z0-9-]{1,40}$/);
  assert.ok(['role-first', 'school-first'].includes(spec.layout));
});

test('a name and a layout override the generated values', () => {

    tier: 'small',
    seed: 'named',
    name: 'Lakeview School District',
    layout: 'school-first',
  });
  assert.equal(spec.districtName, 'Lakeview School District');
  assert.equal(spec.slug, 'lakeview-school-district');

});

    resolveSpec({ tier: 'small', seed: 'a' }).customerId,
    resolveSpec({ tier: 'small', seed: 'b' }).customerId,
  );
});

test('invalid specifications fail with a message', () => {
  const bad: unknown[] = [
    null,
    { seed: 'x' },
    { tier: 'huge', seed: 'x' },
    { tier: 'small', seed: '' },
    { tier: 'small', seed: 'has space' },

    { tier: 'small', seed: 'x', layout: 'sideways' },
    { tier: 'small', seed: 'x', name: '' },
  ];
  for (const input of bad)
    assert.throws(() => resolveSpec(input), SpecError, JSON.stringify(input));
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the specification**

`src/seed/spec.ts`:

```ts
import { createRandom } from '../random.ts';
import { PLACE_NAMES } from './names.ts';

export type Tier = 'small' | 'medium' | 'large' | 'xlarge';
export type LayoutFamily = 'role-first' | 'school-first';

export interface Counts {
  schools: number;
  students: number;
  staff: number;
  devices: number;
}

export interface Rates {
  disabled: number;
  deprovisioned: number;
  misplaced: number;
  unassigned: number;
  expired: number;
  suspended: number;
  deepBranches: number;
}

export interface SeedSpec {
  tier: Tier;
  seed: string;
  name?: string;
  layout?: LayoutFamily;
  counts?: Partial<Counts>;
  rates?: Partial<Rates>;
}

export interface ResolvedSpec {
  tier: Tier;
  seed: string;
  districtName: string;
  slug: string;
  customerId: string;
  primaryDomain: string;
  studentDomain: string;
  aliasDomain: string;
  layout: LayoutFamily;
  counts: Counts;
  rates: Rates;
}

export const TIERS: Readonly<Record<Tier, Counts>> = {
  small: { schools: 1, students: 500, staff: 60, devices: 600 },
  medium: { schools: 12, students: 6_000, staff: 700, devices: 7_000 },
  large: { schools: 60, students: 50_000, staff: 6_000, devices: 60_000 },
  xlarge: { schools: 640, students: 320_000, staff: 45_000, devices: 400_000 },
};

export const DEFAULT_RATES: Readonly<Rates> = {
  disabled: 0.02,
  deprovisioned: 0.05,
  misplaced: 0.02,
  unassigned: 0.15,
  expired: 0.15,
  suspended: 0.04,
  deepBranches: 3,
};

export class SpecError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'SpecError';
  }
}

const FIELDS = ['tier', 'seed', 'name', 'layout', 'counts', 'rates'];
const SEED_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/;
const COUNT_LIMIT = 2_000_000;
const SCHOOL_LIMIT = 2_000;

function numbers(
  value: unknown,
  label: string,
  keys: readonly string[],
  valid: (number: number, key: string) => boolean,
): Record<string, number> {
  if (value === undefined) return {};
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value))
    throw new SpecError(`${label} must be an object.`);
  const out: Record<string, number> = {};
  for (const [key, number] of Object.entries(value)) {
    if (!keys.includes(key))
      throw new SpecError(`Unknown ${label} field: ${key}.`);
    if (
      typeof number !== 'number' ||
      !Number.isFinite(number) ||
      !valid(number, key)
    )
      throw new SpecError(`${label}.${key} is outside its allowed range.`);
    out[key] = number;
  }
  return out;
}

function slugify(name: string): string {
  const slug = name
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
    .slice(0, 40)
    .replace(/-+$/, '');
  return slug || 'district';
}

/** Validate a seed specification and derive every identity value from it. */
export function resolveSpec(input: unknown): ResolvedSpec {
  if (input === null || typeof input !== 'object' || Array.isArray(input))
    throw new SpecError('The seed specification must be an object.');
  const spec = input as Record<string, unknown>;
  for (const key of Object.keys(spec))
    if (!FIELDS.includes(key))
      throw new SpecError(`Unknown seed specification field: ${key}.`);
  const tier = spec.tier;
  if (typeof tier !== 'string' || !Object.hasOwn(TIERS, tier))
    throw new SpecError('tier must be small, medium, large, or xlarge.');
  const seed = spec.seed;
  if (typeof seed !== 'string' || !SEED_PATTERN.test(seed))
    throw new SpecError(
      'seed must be 1 to 64 letters, digits, hyphens, or underscores.',
    );
  const name = spec.name;
  if (
    name !== undefined &&
    (typeof name !== 'string' || name.trim() === '' || name.length > 60)
  )
    throw new SpecError('name must be 1 to 60 characters.');
  const layoutInput = spec.layout;
  if (
    layoutInput !== undefined &&
    layoutInput !== 'role-first' &&
    layoutInput !== 'school-first'
  )
    throw new SpecError('layout must be role-first or school-first.');

  const counts = {
    ...TIERS[tier as Tier],
    ...numbers(
      spec.counts,
      'counts',
      ['schools', 'students', 'staff', 'devices'],
      (number) =>
        Number.isInteger(number) && number >= 1 && number <= COUNT_LIMIT,
    ),
  } as Counts;
  if (counts.schools > SCHOOL_LIMIT)
    throw new SpecError(`counts.schools must be ${SCHOOL_LIMIT} or less.`);
  const rates = {
    ...DEFAULT_RATES,
    ...numbers(spec.rates, 'rates', Object.keys(DEFAULT_RATES), (number, key) =>
      key === 'deepBranches'
        ? Number.isInteger(number) && number >= 0 && number <= 20
        : number >= 0 && number <= 1,
    ),
  } as Rates;
  if (rates.disabled + rates.deprovisioned > 1)
    throw new SpecError(
      'rates.disabled plus rates.deprovisioned must be 1 or less.',
    );

  const random = createRandom(`spec/${seed}`);
  const districtName =
    (name as string | undefined)?.trim() ??
    `${random.fork('place').pick(PLACE_NAMES)} ${random
      .fork('suffix')
      .pick([
        'School District',
        'Unified School District',
        'Public Schools',
        'Community Schools',
      ])}`;
  const slug = slugify(districtName);
  const primaryDomain = `${slug}.test`;
  return {
    tier: tier as Tier,
    seed,
    districtName,
    slug,
    customerId: `C0${random.fork('customer').string(7)}`,
    primaryDomain,
    studentDomain: random.fork('student-domain').chance(0.5)
      ? `students.${slug}.test`
      : primaryDomain,
    aliasDomain: `${slug}-k12.test`,
    layout:
      (layoutInput as LayoutFamily | undefined) ??
      random
        .fork('layout')
        .pick<LayoutFamily>(['role-first', 'school-first']),
    counts,
    rates,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the failing layout tests**

`src/seed/layout.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import {
  buildLayout,
  DEVICE_CATEGORIES,
  MAX_DEPTH,
  MAX_UNITS,
  type Layout,
} from './layout.ts';
import { resolveSpec } from './spec.ts';

const within = (ancestor: string, path: string) =>
  path === ancestor || path.startsWith(`${ancestor}/`);

function assertSeparated(layout: Layout) {
  const users = layout.units.filter((unit) => unit.kind === 'users');
  const devices = layout.units.filter((unit) => unit.kind === 'devices');
  assert.ok(users.length > 0 && devices.length > 0);
  for (const user of users)
    for (const device of devices) {
      assert.ok(!within(user.path, device.path), `${device.path} in ${user.path}`);
      assert.ok(!within(device.path, user.path), `${user.path} in ${device.path}`);
    }
}

function assertWellFormed(layout: Layout) {
  const byId = new Map(
    [layout.root, ...layout.units].map((unit) => [unit.orgUnitId, unit]),
  );
  const paths = new Set<string>();
  assert.equal(byId.size, layout.units.length + 1, 'IDs are unique.');
  for (const unit of layout.units) {
    assert.ok(!paths.has(unit.path), `duplicate path ${unit.path}`);
    paths.add(unit.path);
    const parent = byId.get(unit.parentId ?? '');
    assert.ok(parent, `parent of ${unit.path}`);
    assert.equal(unit.parentPath, parent.path);
    assert.equal(unit.depth, parent.depth + 1);
    assert.match(unit.orgUnitId, /^id:0[0-9a-z]{13}$/);
  }
}

test('a role-first layout puts students, staff, and devices under separate roots', () => {
  const layout = buildLayout(
    resolveSpec({
      tier: 'medium',
      seed: 'role',
      layout: 'role-first',
      counts: { schools: 6 },
    }),
  );
  assertWellFormed(layout);
  assertSeparated(layout);
  const topLevel = layout.units.filter((unit) => unit.depth === 1);
  assert.equal(topLevel.length, 3);
  for (const school of layout.schools) {
    for (const grade of school.grades)
      assert.ok(school.studentOus[grade], `${school.name} grade ${grade}`);
    for (const category of DEVICE_CATEGORIES)
      assert.ok(school.deviceOus[category]);
    assert.ok(school.staffOu);
  }
});

test('a school-first layout nests students, staff, and devices under each school', () => {
  const layout = buildLayout(
    resolveSpec({
      tier: 'medium',
      seed: 'school',
      layout: 'school-first',
      counts: { schools: 6 },
    }),
  );
  assertWellFormed(layout);
  assertSeparated(layout);
  for (const school of layout.schools) {
    const schoolPath = `/${school.name}`;
    assert.ok(within(schoolPath, school.staffOu));
    assert.ok(within(schoolPath, school.deviceOus.cart));
  }
});

test('the same specification always builds the same layout', () => {
  const spec = resolveSpec({ tier: 'medium', seed: 'repeat' });
  assert.deepEqual(buildLayout(spec), buildLayout(spec));
});

test('labels vary across seeds', () => {
  const labels = new Set<string>();
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    const layout = buildLayout(
      resolveSpec({ tier: 'small', seed: `vary-${i}`, layout: 'role-first' }),
    );
    for (const unit of layout.units.filter((unit) => unit.depth === 1))
      labels.add(unit.name);
  }
  assert.ok(labels.size >= 5, [...labels].join(', '));
});

test('deep branches reach 8 to 12 levels for both users and devices', () => {
  const layout = buildLayout(
    resolveSpec({ tier: 'small', seed: 'deep', rates: { deepBranches: 3 } }),
  );
  const depths = layout.units.map((unit) => unit.depth);
  assert.ok(Math.max(...depths) >= 8);
  assert.ok(Math.max(...depths) <= 12);
  assert.ok(layout.deepUserOus.length > 0);
  assert.ok(layout.deepDeviceOus.length > 0);
  assertSeparated(layout);
});

test('no deep branches exist when the rate is zero', () => {
  const layout = buildLayout(
    resolveSpec({ tier: 'small', seed: 'flat', rates: { deepBranches: 0 } }),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(layout.deepUserOus, []);
  assert.deepEqual(layout.deepDeviceOus, []);
  assert.ok(Math.max(...layout.units.map((unit) => unit.depth)) <= 5);
});

test('an xlarge layout adds regions and stays inside the Google limits', () => {
  const layout = buildLayout(resolveSpec({ tier: 'xlarge', seed: 'cps' }));
  assert.equal(layout.schools.length, 640);
  assert.ok(layout.schools.every((school) => school.region !== null));
  assert.ok(layout.units.length < MAX_UNITS, `${layout.units.length} units`);
  assert.ok(layout.units.length > 5_000, `${layout.units.length} units`);
  assert.ok(layout.units.every((unit) => unit.depth <= MAX_DEPTH));
});

test('school names are unique', () => {
  const layout = buildLayout(resolveSpec({ tier: 'xlarge', seed: 'names' }));
  const names = new Set(layout.schools.map((school) => school.name));
  assert.equal(names.size, layout.schools.length);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement the layout generator**

`src/seed/layout.ts`:

```ts
import { createRandom, type Random } from '../random.ts';
import { SCHOOL_NAMESAKES } from './names.ts';
import type { ResolvedSpec } from './spec.ts';

export type OuKind = 'root' | 'container' | 'users' | 'devices';
export type DeviceCategory = 'one-to-one' | 'cart' | 'staff' | 'kiosk';
export const DEVICE_CATEGORIES: readonly DeviceCategory[] = [
  'one-to-one',
  'cart',
  'staff',
  'kiosk',
];
export type SchoolLevel = 'elementary' | 'middle' | 'high' | 'k8';

export interface OrgUnitNode {
  orgUnitId: string;
  name: string;
  path: string;
  parentId: string | null;
  parentPath: string | null;
  depth: number;
  description: string;
  kind: OuKind;
}

export interface School {
  index: number;
  name: string;
  level: SchoolLevel;
  grades: number[];
  region: string | null;
  weight: number;
  studentOus: Record<number, string>;
  staffOu: string;
  deviceOus: Record<DeviceCategory, string>;
}

export interface Layout {
  root: OrgUnitNode;
  units: OrgUnitNode[];
  schools: School[];
  districtStaffOu: string;
  districtDeviceOu: string;
  deepUserOus: string[];
  deepDeviceOus: string[];
}

export const MAX_DEPTH = 35;
export const MAX_UNITS = 40_000;
/** The class of 2027 is in grade 12 during the 2026-27 school year. */
export const GRADUATION_BASE_YEAR = 2027;

const GRADES: Record<SchoolLevel, number[]> = {
  elementary: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5],
  middle: [6, 7, 8],
  high: [9, 10, 11, 12],
  k8: [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8],
};

const LEVEL_WORDS: Record<SchoolLevel, string[]> = {
  elementary: ['Elementary', 'Elementary School', 'Primary'],
  middle: ['Middle', 'Middle School', 'Junior High'],
  high: ['High', 'High School', 'Academy'],
  k8: ['K-8', 'School', 'Academy'],
};

/** A stable OU ID derived from the seed and the path. */
export function ouId(seed: string, path: string): string {
  return `id:0${createRandom(`ou/${seed}/${path}`).string(13)}`;
}

const childPath = (parent: string, name: string) =>
  parent === '/' ? `/${name}` : `${parent}/${name}`;

function makeSchools(spec: ResolvedSpec, random: Random): School[] {
  const used = new Set<string>();
  const schools: School[] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < spec.counts.schools; index++) {
    const level = random.weighted<SchoolLevel>([
      ['elementary', 0.6],
      ['middle', 0.2],
      ['high', 0.15],
      ['k8', 0.05],
    ]);
    const base = `${random.pick(SCHOOL_NAMESAKES)} ${random.pick(LEVEL_WORDS[level])}`;
    let name = base;
    for (let n = 2; used.has(name); n++) name = `${base} ${n}`;
    used.add(name);
    schools.push({
      index,
      name,
      level,
      grades: GRADES[level],
      region: null,
      weight: (0.5 + random.next()) * (level === 'high' ? 1.6 : 1),
      studentOus: {},
      staffOu: '',
      deviceOus: { 'one-to-one': '', cart: '', staff: '', kiosk: '' },
    });
  }
  return schools;
}

/** Build the OU tree, the school list, and the OU assignments for one domain. */
export function buildLayout(spec: ResolvedSpec): Layout {
  const random = createRandom(`layout/${spec.seed}`);
  const labels = {
    students: random.pick(['Students', 'Learners', 'Student Accounts']),
    staff: random.pick(['Staff', 'Employees', 'Faculty and Staff']),
    devices: random.pick(['Devices', 'Chromebooks', 'Chrome Devices']),
    gradeStyle: random.pick<'grade' | 'class-of'>(['grade', 'class-of']),
    categories: {
      'one-to-one': random.pick(['1:1', 'One to One', 'Student Devices']),
      cart: random.pick(['Carts', 'Cart Devices', 'Shared Carts']),
      staff: random.pick(['Staff Devices', 'Teacher Devices']),
      kiosk: random.pick(['Kiosk', 'Testing Kiosks', 'Signage']),
    } satisfies Record<DeviceCategory, string>,
    region: random.pick(['Network', 'Region', 'Zone']),
  };
  const gradeLabel = (grade: number) =>
    labels.gradeStyle === 'grade'
      ? grade === 0
        ? 'Kindergarten'
        : `Grade ${grade}`
      : `Class of ${GRADUATION_BASE_YEAR + 12 - grade}`;

  const root: OrgUnitNode = {
    orgUnitId: ouId(spec.seed, '/'),
    name: spec.districtName,
    path: '/',
    parentId: null,
    parentPath: null,
    depth: 0,
    description: '',
    kind: 'root',
  };
  const units: OrgUnitNode[] = [];
  const byPath = new Map<string, OrgUnitNode>([['/', root]]);
  const add = (
    parent: OrgUnitNode,
    name: string,
    kind: OuKind,
  ): OrgUnitNode => {
    let unique = name;
    for (let n = 2; byPath.has(childPath(parent.path, unique)); n++)
      unique = `${name} ${n}`;
    if (parent.depth + 1 > MAX_DEPTH)
      throw new RangeError(`OU depth exceeds ${MAX_DEPTH}.`);
    if (units.length + 1 > MAX_UNITS)
      throw new RangeError(`OU count exceeds ${MAX_UNITS}.`);
    const path = childPath(parent.path, unique);
    const node: OrgUnitNode = {
      orgUnitId: ouId(spec.seed, path),
      name: unique,
      path,
      parentId: parent.orgUnitId,
      parentPath: parent.path,
      depth: parent.depth + 1,
      description: '',
      kind,
    };
    units.push(node);
    byPath.set(path, node);
    return node;
  };
  const ensure = (
    parent: OrgUnitNode,
    name: string,
    kind: OuKind,
  ): OrgUnitNode =>
    byPath.get(childPath(parent.path, name)) ?? add(parent, name, kind);

  const schools = makeSchools(spec, random.fork('schools'));
  const regionCount =
    spec.tier === 'xlarge' || spec.counts.schools >= 200
      ? Math.min(20, Math.max(2, Math.round(spec.counts.schools / 40)))
      : 0;
  for (const school of schools)
    school.region = regionCount
      ? `${labels.region} ${(school.index % regionCount) + 1}`
      : null;
  const under = (parent: OrgUnitNode, school: School) =>
    school.region ? ensure(parent, school.region, 'container') : parent;

  let districtStaffOu: string;
  let districtDeviceOu: string;
  if (spec.layout === 'role-first') {
    const students = add(root, labels.students, 'container');
    const staff = add(root, labels.staff, 'container');
    const devices = add(root, labels.devices, 'container');
    for (const school of schools) {
      const studentSchool = add(under(students, school), school.name, 'container');
      for (const grade of school.grades)
        school.studentOus[grade] = add(studentSchool, gradeLabel(grade), 'users').path;
      school.staffOu = add(under(staff, school), school.name, 'users').path;
      const deviceSchool = add(under(devices, school), school.name, 'container');
      for (const category of DEVICE_CATEGORIES)
        school.deviceOus[category] = add(
          deviceSchool,
          labels.categories[category],
          'devices',
        ).path;
    }
    districtStaffOu = add(staff, 'District Office', 'users').path;
    districtDeviceOu = add(devices, 'District Office', 'devices').path;
  } else {
    for (const school of schools) {
      const schoolNode = add(under(root, school), school.name, 'container');
      const students = add(schoolNode, labels.students, 'container');
      for (const grade of school.grades)
        school.studentOus[grade] = add(students, gradeLabel(grade), 'users').path;
      school.staffOu = add(schoolNode, labels.staff, 'users').path;
      const devices = add(schoolNode, labels.devices, 'container');
      for (const category of DEVICE_CATEGORIES)
        school.deviceOus[category] = add(
          devices,
          labels.categories[category],
          'devices',
        ).path;
    }
    const district = add(root, 'District Office', 'container');
    districtStaffOu = add(district, labels.staff, 'users').path;
    districtDeviceOu = add(district, labels.devices, 'devices').path;
  }

  const deep = random.fork('deep');
  const deepUserOus: string[] = [];
  const deepDeviceOus: string[] = [];
  for (let branch = 0; branch < spec.rates.deepBranches; branch++) {
    const kind: OuKind = branch % 2 === 0 ? 'devices' : 'users';
    const parents = new Set(units.map((unit) => unit.parentId));
    const leaves = units.filter(
      (unit) => unit.kind === kind && !parents.has(unit.orgUnitId),
    );
    if (leaves.length === 0) continue;
    let node = deep.pick(leaves);
    const target = deep.int(8, 12);
    while (node.depth < target)
      node = add(
        node,
        deep.pick([
          `Room ${deep.int(100, 399)}`,
          `Cart ${deep.int(1, 40)}`,
          'Pilot',
          'Testing',
          `Shelf ${deep.int(1, 9)}`,
          `Group ${deep.int(1, 20)}`,
        ]),
        kind,
      );
    (kind === 'devices' ? deepDeviceOus : deepUserOus).push(node.path);
  }

  return {
    root,
    units,
    schools,
    districtStaffOu,
    districtDeviceOu,
    deepUserOus,
    deepDeviceOus,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the settings**

`src/settings.ts`:

```ts
export interface QuotaSettings {
  perMinute: number;
  ceiling: number;
  heavyRatio: number;
  overMinutesToFlag: number;
  heavyMinutesToFlag: number;
  flagMinutes: number;
  retryAfter: boolean;
}

export interface LatencySettings {
  roundTripMedianMs: number;
  roundTripP99Ms: number;
  bytesPerSecond: number;
  baseMs: number;
  perItemMs: number;
  scanPer10kRowsMs: number;
  loadThreshold: number;
  loadPercentPerRequest: number;
  batchParallelism: number;
  batchPartMs: number;
  capMs: number;
}

export interface DomainSettings {
  quota: QuotaSettings;
  chromeQuota: QuotaSettings;
  concurrency: { perMethod: number; perCustomer: number };
  latency: LatencySettings;
  telemetry: { recentReports: number };
}

const QUOTA: QuotaSettings = {
  perMinute: 2400,
  ceiling: 1.25,
  heavyRatio: 1.125,
  overMinutesToFlag: 30,
  heavyMinutesToFlag: 10,
  flagMinutes: 60,
  retryAfter: false,
};

export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: DomainSettings = Object.freeze({
  quota: QUOTA,
  chromeQuota: QUOTA,
  concurrency: { perMethod: 40, perCustomer: 120 },
  latency: {
    roundTripMedianMs: 80,
    roundTripP99Ms: 400,
    bytesPerSecond: 8_000_000,
    baseMs: 150,
    perItemMs: 3,
    scanPer10kRowsMs: 20,
    loadThreshold: 20,
    loadPercentPerRequest: 2,
    batchParallelism: 10,
    batchPartMs: 40,
    capMs: 2000,
  },
  telemetry: { recentReports: 1 },
}) as DomainSettings;

export class SettingsError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'SettingsError';
  }
}

function merge(base: unknown, patch: unknown, path: string): unknown {
  if (patch === undefined) return base;
  if (typeof base === 'object' && base !== null) {
    if (typeof patch !== 'object' || patch === null || Array.isArray(patch))
      throw new SettingsError(`${path} must be an object.`);
    const out: Record<string, unknown> = {
      ...(base as Record<string, unknown>),
    };
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(patch)) {
      if (!Object.hasOwn(out, key))
        throw new SettingsError(`Unknown setting: ${path}.${key}.`);
      out[key] = merge(out[key], value, `${path}.${key}`);
    }
    return out;
  }
  if (typeof base === 'boolean') {
    if (typeof patch !== 'boolean')
      throw new SettingsError(`${path} must be true or false.`);
    return patch;
  }
  if (typeof patch !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(patch) || patch < 0)
    throw new SettingsError(`${path} must be a non-negative number.`);
  return patch;
}

function check(condition: boolean, message: string) {
  if (!condition) throw new SettingsError(message);
}

function validateQuota(quota: QuotaSettings, path: string) {
  check(quota.perMinute >= 1, `${path}.perMinute must be 1 or more.`);
  check(quota.ceiling >= 1, `${path}.ceiling must be 1 or more.`);
  check(
    quota.heavyRatio >= 1 && quota.heavyRatio <= quota.ceiling,
    `${path}.heavyRatio must be from 1 to the ceiling.`,
  );
  for (const key of [
    'overMinutesToFlag',
    'heavyMinutesToFlag',
    'flagMinutes',
  ] as const)
    check(
      Number.isInteger(quota[key]) && quota[key] >= 1 && quota[key] <= 60,
      `${path}.${key} must be a whole number from 1 to 60.`,
    );
}

/** Apply a partial settings patch and validate the result. */
export function mergeSettings(
  base: DomainSettings,
  patch: unknown,
): DomainSettings {
  const merged = merge(base, patch, 'settings') as DomainSettings;
  validateQuota(merged.quota, 'settings.quota');
  validateQuota(merged.chromeQuota, 'settings.chromeQuota');
  check(
    merged.concurrency.perMethod >= 1 && merged.concurrency.perCustomer >= 1,
    'Concurrency caps must be 1 or more.',
  );
  const latency = merged.latency;
  check(latency.bytesPerSecond > 0, 'latency.bytesPerSecond must be above 0.');
  check(
    latency.roundTripP99Ms >= latency.roundTripMedianMs,
    'latency.roundTripP99Ms must be at least the median.',
  );
  check(
    Number.isInteger(latency.batchParallelism) && latency.batchParallelism >= 1,
    'latency.batchParallelism must be a whole number of 1 or more.',
  );
  check(
    Number.isInteger(merged.telemetry.recentReports) &&
      merged.telemetry.recentReports >= 1,
    'telemetry.recentReports must be a whole number of 1 or more.',
  );
  return merged;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the SQLite helpers**

`src/store/sqlite.ts`:

```ts
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';

/** Open a SQLite file with write-ahead logging and foreign keys on. */
export function openDatabase(path: string): DatabaseSync {
  const db = new DatabaseSync(path);
  db.exec(
    'PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL; PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL; PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON; PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000;',
  );
  return db;
}

export function transaction<T>(db: DatabaseSync, work: () => T): T {
  db.exec('BEGIN');
  try {
    const result = work();
    db.exec('COMMIT');
    return result;
  } catch (error) {
    db.exec('ROLLBACK');
    throw error;
  }
}

/** Type the rows that `node:sqlite` returns. */
export function rows<T>(value: unknown): T[] {
  return value as T[];
}

export function row<T>(value: unknown): T | undefined {
  return value as T | undefined;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Implement the registry**

`src/store/registry.ts`:

```ts
import type { SeedSpec } from '../seed/spec.ts';
import type { DomainSettings } from '../settings.ts';
import { openDatabase, row, rows, transaction } from './sqlite.ts';

export interface StoredDomain {
  customerId: string;
  primaryDomain: string;
  spec: SeedSpec;
  settings: DomainSettings;
  seededAt: number;
  createdAt: number;
}

export interface StoredServiceAccount {
  clientId: string;
  clientEmail: string;
  projectId: string;
  privateKeyId: string;
  publicKey: string;
  customerId: string;
}

export interface StoredToken {
  token: string;
  clientId: string;
  projectId: string;
  subject: string;
  customerId: string;
  scopes: string[];
  expiresAt: number;
}

export interface Registry {
  addDomain(domain: StoredDomain, names: readonly string[]): void;
  getDomain(customerId: string): StoredDomain | undefined;
  domainForName(name: string): string | undefined;
  listDomains(): StoredDomain[];
  setSettings(customerId: string, settings: DomainSettings): void;
  setSeededAt(customerId: string, seededAt: number): void;
  deleteDomain(customerId: string): void;
  addServiceAccount(account: StoredServiceAccount): void;
  serviceAccountByEmail(email: string): StoredServiceAccount | undefined;
  setGrant(customerId: string, clientId: string, scopes: readonly string[]): void;
  grant(customerId: string, clientId: string): string[] | undefined;
  saveToken(token: StoredToken): void;
  token(value: string): StoredToken | undefined;
  close(): void;
}

const SCHEMA = `
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS domains (
  customer_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  primary_domain TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  spec TEXT NOT NULL,
  settings TEXT NOT NULL,
  seeded_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
  created_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS domain_names (
  name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  customer_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES domains ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS service_accounts (
  client_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  client_email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  project_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  private_key_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  public_key TEXT NOT NULL,
  customer_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES domains ON DELETE CASCADE
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS grants (
  customer_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES domains ON DELETE CASCADE,
  client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  scopes TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (customer_id, client_id)
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tokens (
  token TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  client_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  project_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  subject TEXT NOT NULL,
  customer_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES domains ON DELETE CASCADE,
  scopes TEXT NOT NULL,
  expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);
`;

interface DomainRow {
  customer_id: string;
  primary_domain: string;
  spec: string;
  settings: string;
  seeded_at: number;
  created_at: number;
}

const toDomain = (value: DomainRow): StoredDomain => ({
  customerId: value.customer_id,
  primaryDomain: value.primary_domain,
  spec: JSON.parse(value.spec) as SeedSpec,
  settings: JSON.parse(value.settings) as DomainSettings,
  seededAt: value.seeded_at,
  createdAt: value.created_at,
});

export function openRegistry(path: string): Registry {
  const db = openDatabase(path);
  db.exec(SCHEMA);
  return {
    addDomain(domain, names) {
      transaction(db, () => {
        db.prepare(
          'INSERT INTO domains (customer_id, primary_domain, spec, settings, seeded_at, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
        ).run(
          domain.customerId,
          domain.primaryDomain.toLowerCase(),
          JSON.stringify(domain.spec),
          JSON.stringify(domain.settings),
          domain.seededAt,
          domain.createdAt,
        );
        const insert = db.prepare(
          'INSERT INTO domain_names (name, customer_id) VALUES (?, ?)',
        );
        for (const name of new Set(names.map((name) => name.toLowerCase())))
          insert.run(name, domain.customerId);
      });
    },
    getDomain(customerId) {
      const found = row<DomainRow>(
        db.prepare('SELECT * FROM domains WHERE customer_id = ?').get(customerId),
      );
      return found && toDomain(found);
    },
    domainForName(name) {
      return row<{ customer_id: string }>(
        db
          .prepare('SELECT customer_id FROM domain_names WHERE name = ?')
          .get(name.toLowerCase()),
      )?.customer_id;
    },
    listDomains() {
      return rows<DomainRow>(
        db.prepare('SELECT * FROM domains ORDER BY created_at, customer_id').all(),
      ).map(toDomain);
    },
    setSettings(customerId, settings) {
      db.prepare('UPDATE domains SET settings = ? WHERE customer_id = ?').run(
        JSON.stringify(settings),
        customerId,
      );
    },
    setSeededAt(customerId, seededAt) {
      db.prepare('UPDATE domains SET seeded_at = ? WHERE customer_id = ?').run(
        seededAt,
        customerId,
      );
    },
    deleteDomain(customerId) {
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM domains WHERE customer_id = ?').run(customerId);
    },
    addServiceAccount(account) {
      db.prepare(
        'INSERT INTO service_accounts (client_id, client_email, project_id, private_key_id, public_key, customer_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
      ).run(
        account.clientId,
        account.clientEmail.toLowerCase(),
        account.projectId,
        account.privateKeyId,
        account.publicKey,
        account.customerId,
      );
    },
    serviceAccountByEmail(email) {
      const found = row<{
        client_id: string;
        client_email: string;
        project_id: string;
        private_key_id: string;
        public_key: string;
        customer_id: string;
      }>(
        db
          .prepare('SELECT * FROM service_accounts WHERE client_email = ?')
          .get(email.toLowerCase()),
      );
      return (
        found && {
          clientId: found.client_id,
          clientEmail: found.client_email,
          projectId: found.project_id,
          privateKeyId: found.private_key_id,
          publicKey: found.public_key,
          customerId: found.customer_id,
        }
      );
    },
    setGrant(customerId, clientId, scopes) {
      db.prepare(
        'INSERT INTO grants (customer_id, client_id, scopes) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT (customer_id, client_id) DO UPDATE SET scopes = excluded.scopes',
      ).run(customerId, clientId, JSON.stringify([...scopes]));
    },
    grant(customerId, clientId) {
      const found = row<{ scopes: string }>(
        db
          .prepare(
            'SELECT scopes FROM grants WHERE customer_id = ? AND client_id = ?',
          )
          .get(customerId, clientId),
      );
      return found && (JSON.parse(found.scopes) as string[]);
    },
    saveToken(token) {
      db.prepare(
        'INSERT INTO tokens (token, client_id, project_id, subject, customer_id, scopes, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
      ).run(
        token.token,
        token.clientId,
        token.projectId,
        token.subject,
        token.customerId,
        JSON.stringify(token.scopes),
        token.expiresAt,
      );
    },
    token(value) {
      const found = row<{
        token: string;
        client_id: string;
        project_id: string;
        subject: string;
        customer_id: string;
        scopes: string;
        expires_at: number;
      }>(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM tokens WHERE token = ?').get(value));
      return (
        found && {
          token: found.token,
          clientId: found.client_id,
          projectId: found.project_id,
          subject: found.subject,
          customerId: found.customer_id,
          scopes: JSON.parse(found.scopes) as string[],
          expiresAt: found.expires_at,
        }
      );
    },
    close() {
      db.close();
    },
  };
}
```

- Produces: `DOMAIN_SCHEMA`, `openDomainDb(path: string): DatabaseSync`.

```ts
import type { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { openDatabase } from './sqlite.ts';

export const DOMAIN_SCHEMA = `
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS domains (
  domain_name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  is_primary INTEGER NOT NULL,
  verified INTEGER NOT NULL,
  creation_time INTEGER NOT NULL,
  alias_of TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS orgunits (
  org_unit_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  path TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT NOT NULL,
  parent_id TEXT,
  parent_path TEXT,
  depth INTEGER NOT NULL,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL,
  ord INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS orgunits_parent ON orgunits (parent_id, ord);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  primary_email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  given_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  family_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  org_unit_path TEXT NOT NULL,
  is_admin INTEGER NOT NULL,
  suspended INTEGER NOT NULL,
  suspension_reason TEXT,
  external_id TEXT,
  role TEXT NOT NULL,
  school INTEGER,
  creation_time INTEGER NOT NULL,
  last_login_time INTEGER,
  ord INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS users_ou ON users (org_unit_path);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS users_family ON users (family_name, id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS users_given ON users (given_name, id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS users_ord ON users (ord);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_aliases (
  alias TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES users (id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS user_aliases_user ON user_aliases (user_id);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS devices (
  device_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  serial_number TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  status TEXT NOT NULL,
  model TEXT NOT NULL,
  org_unit_path TEXT NOT NULL,
  org_unit_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  annotated_user TEXT,
  annotated_asset_id TEXT,
  annotated_location TEXT,
  notes TEXT,
  last_sync INTEGER,
  first_enrollment_time INTEGER NOT NULL,
  last_enrollment_time INTEGER NOT NULL,
  mac_address TEXT NOT NULL,
  ethernet_mac_address TEXT,
  os_version TEXT NOT NULL,
  platform_version TEXT NOT NULL,
  firmware_version TEXT NOT NULL,
  boot_mode TEXT NOT NULL,
  aue INTEGER NOT NULL,
  recent_users TEXT,
  last_user_activity INTEGER,
  has_battery INTEGER NOT NULL,
  ram_total INTEGER NOT NULL,
  storage_total INTEGER NOT NULL,
  order_number TEXT,
  license_type TEXT NOT NULL,
  deprovision_reason TEXT,
  last_deprovision INTEGER,
  category TEXT NOT NULL,
  misplaced INTEGER NOT NULL,
  ord INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS devices_ou ON devices (org_unit_path, ord);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS devices_status ON devices (status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS devices_sync ON devices (last_sync);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS devices_ord ON devices (ord);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS devices_asset ON devices (annotated_asset_id);
`;

/** Open one domain file and create its tables when they are missing. */
export function openDomainDb(path: string): DatabaseSync {
  const db = openDatabase(path);
  db.exec(DOMAIN_SCHEMA);
  return db;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the seed context and the database hash**

`src/seed/context.ts`:

```ts
import type { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import type { Random } from '../random.ts';
import type { Layout } from './layout.ts';
import type { ResolvedSpec } from './spec.ts';

export interface SeedContext {
  spec: ResolvedSpec;
  layout: Layout;
  seededAt: number;
  db: DatabaseSync;
}

export const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
export const YEAR_MS = 365.25 * DAY_MS;
export const HEX = '0123456789abcdef';

export const iso = (ms: number): string => new Date(ms).toISOString();

/** Lower-case letters and digits only, for email local parts. */
export const emailLocal = (text: string): string =>
  text.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');

/** Return a function that picks an index with probability proportional to its weight. */
export function weightedIndex(
  weights: readonly number[],
  random: Random,
): () => number {
  const cumulative: number[] = [];
  let total = 0;
  for (const weight of weights) {
    total += weight;
    cumulative.push(total);
  }
  return () => {
    const roll = random.next() * total;
    let low = 0;
    let high = cumulative.length - 1;
    while (low < high) {
      const middle = (low + high) >> 1;
      if (cumulative[middle] > roll) high = middle;
      else low = middle + 1;
    }
    return low;
  };
}
```

`src/testing/db-hash.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import type { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';

/** Hash every row of every table in insertion order. */
export function hashDatabase(db: DatabaseSync): string {
  const hash = createHash('sha256');
  const tables = rows<{ name: string }>(
    db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' ORDER BY name",
      )
      .all(),
  );
  for (const { name } of tables) {
    hash.update(name);
    for (const value of db
      .prepare(`SELECT * FROM "${name}" ORDER BY rowid`)
      .iterate())
      hash.update(JSON.stringify(value));
  }
  return hash.digest('hex');
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing user tests**

`src/seed/users.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { openDomainDb } from '../store/domain-db.ts';
import { row, rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { hashDatabase } from '../testing/db-hash.ts';
import { buildLayout } from './layout.ts';
import { resolveSpec } from './spec.ts';
import { seedUsers } from './users.ts';

const SEEDED_AT = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 12);

function seed(input: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const spec = resolveSpec(input);
  const layout = buildLayout(spec);
  const db = openDomainDb(':memory:');
  const index = seedUsers({ spec, layout, seededAt: SEEDED_AT, db });
  return { spec, layout, db, index };
}

const INPUT = {
  tier: 'medium',
  seed: 'users',
  counts: { schools: 4, students: 3000, staff: 300 },
};

const count = (db: ReturnType<typeof seed>['db'], sql: string) =>
  Number(row<{ n: number }>(db.prepare(sql).get())?.n);

test('the user count is students plus staff plus three administrators', () => {
  const { db } = seed(INPUT);
  assert.equal(count(db, 'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM users'), 3303);
  assert.equal(
    count(db, "SELECT count(*) AS n FROM users WHERE role = 'student'"),
    3000,
  );
  assert.equal(count(db, 'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM users WHERE is_admin = 1'), 3);
});

test('the first administrator is admin at the primary domain', () => {
  const { db, spec, layout, index } = seed(INPUT);
  const admin = row<{ is_admin: number; org_unit_path: string }>(
    db
      .prepare('SELECT is_admin, org_unit_path FROM users WHERE primary_email = ?')
      .get(`admin@${spec.primaryDomain}`),
  );
  assert.equal(admin?.is_admin, 1);
  assert.equal(admin?.org_unit_path, layout.districtStaffOu);
  assert.equal(index.adminEmail, `admin@${spec.primaryDomain}`);
});

test('students use the student domain and staff use the primary domain', () => {
  const { db, spec } = seed(INPUT);
  const students = rows<{ primary_email: string }>(
    db.prepare("SELECT primary_email FROM users WHERE role = 'student'").all(),
  );
  assert.ok(
    students.every((user) => user.primary_email.endsWith(`@${spec.studentDomain}`)),
  );
  const staff = rows<{ primary_email: string }>(
    db.prepare("SELECT primary_email FROM users WHERE role != 'student'").all(),
  );
  assert.ok(
    staff.every((user) => user.primary_email.endsWith(`@${spec.primaryDomain}`)),
  );
});

test('every user sits in a user OU', () => {
  const { db, layout } = seed(INPUT);
  const userOus = new Set(
    layout.units.filter((unit) => unit.kind === 'users').map((unit) => unit.path),
  );
  const paths = rows<{ org_unit_path: string }>(
    db.prepare('SELECT DISTINCT org_unit_path FROM users').all(),
  );
  for (const { org_unit_path } of paths)
    assert.ok(userOus.has(org_unit_path), org_unit_path);
});

test('the suspended share follows the rate', () => {
  const { db } = seed(INPUT);
  const share = count(db, 'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM users WHERE suspended = 1') / 3303;
  assert.ok(Math.abs(share - 0.04) < 0.02, `share ${share}`);
});

test('aliases point at users and never collide with primary addresses', () => {
  const { db } = seed(INPUT);
  assert.ok(count(db, 'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM user_aliases') > 0);
  assert.equal(
    count(
      db,
      'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM user_aliases a JOIN users u ON u.primary_email = a.alias',
    ),
    0,
  );
  assert.equal(
    count(
      db,
      'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM user_aliases a LEFT JOIN users u ON u.id = a.user_id WHERE u.id IS NULL',
    ),
    0,
  );
});

test('external IDs and user IDs are unique', () => {
  const { db } = seed(INPUT);
  assert.equal(
    count(db, 'SELECT count(DISTINCT external_id) AS n FROM users WHERE external_id IS NOT NULL'),
    count(db, 'SELECT count(*) AS n FROM users WHERE external_id IS NOT NULL'),
  );
  const ids = rows<{ id: string }>(db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users').all());
  assert.ok(ids.every(({ id }) => /^1\d{20}$/.test(id)));
});

test('the index lists students and staff per school', () => {
  const { index, layout } = seed(INPUT);
  assert.equal(index.students.length, layout.schools.length);
  assert.ok(index.students.every((list) => list.length > 0));
  assert.ok(index.staff.every((list) => list.length > 0));
  assert.ok(index.districtStaff.length > 0);
});

test('the same specification produces identical users', () => {
  assert.equal(hashDatabase(seed(INPUT).db), hashDatabase(seed(INPUT).db));
  assert.notEqual(
    hashDatabase(seed(INPUT).db),
    hashDatabase(seed({ ...INPUT, seed: 'other' }).db),
  );
});

test('student address styles vary across domains', () => {
  const styles = new Set<string>();
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    const { db } = seed({
      tier: 'small',
      seed: `style-${i}`,
      counts: { students: 20, staff: 5 },
    });
    const local = row<{ primary_email: string }>(
      db
        .prepare("SELECT primary_email FROM users WHERE role = 'student' LIMIT 1")
        .get(),
    )!.primary_email.split('@')[0];
    styles.add(/^\d+$/.test(local) ? 'id' : local.includes('.') ? 'dotted' : 'initial');
  }
  assert.ok(styles.size >= 2, [...styles].join(', '));
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement user seeding**

`src/seed/users.ts`:

```ts
import { createRandom } from '../random.ts';
import {
  DAY_MS,
  emailLocal,
  weightedIndex,
  type SeedContext,
} from './context.ts';
import { GRADUATION_BASE_YEAR } from './layout.ts';
import { FIRST_NAMES, LAST_NAMES } from './names.ts';

export interface UserIndex {
  /** Student addresses per school index. */
  students: string[][];
  /** Staff addresses per school index. */
  staff: string[][];
  districtStaff: string[];
  adminEmail: string;
}

type StudentStyle = 'first.last' | 'initial-last-year' | 'student-id';

const ADMINS = [
  ['Admin', 'Account', 'admin'],
  ['Technology', 'Director', 'technology.director'],
  ['Help', 'Desk', 'helpdesk'],
] as const;

/** Insert administrators, staff, and students. The caller owns the transaction. */
export function seedUsers(ctx: SeedContext): UserIndex {
  const { spec, layout, seededAt, db } = ctx;
  const random = createRandom(`users/${spec.seed}`);
  const studentStyle = random.pick<StudentStyle>([
    'first.last',
    'initial-last-year',
    'student-id',
  ]);
  const insert = db.prepare(
    'INSERT INTO users (id, primary_email, given_name, family_name, full_name, org_unit_path, is_admin, suspended, suspension_reason, external_id, role, school, creation_time, last_login_time, ord) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
  );
  const insertAlias = db.prepare(
    'INSERT INTO user_aliases (alias, user_id) VALUES (?, ?)',
  );
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  const locals = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  let ord = 0;

  const address = (domain: string, local: string): string => {
    let used = locals.get(domain);
    if (!used) locals.set(domain, (used = new Set()));
    let candidate = local;
    for (let n = 2; used.has(candidate); n++) candidate = `${local}${n}`;
    used.add(candidate);
    return `${candidate}@${domain}`;
  };
  const newId = (): string => {
    let id: string;
    do id = `1${random.string(20, '0123456789')}`;
    while (ids.has(id));
    ids.add(id);
    return id;
  };
  const lastLogin = (suspended: boolean): number | null =>
    suspended
      ? seededAt - random.int(60, 720) * DAY_MS
      : random.chance(0.9)
        ? seededAt - random.int(0, 14 * DAY_MS)
        : null;
  const add = (user: {
    email: string;
    given: string;
    family: string;
    ou: string;
    admin: boolean;
    suspended: boolean;
    externalId: string | null;
    role: 'student' | 'staff' | 'admin';
    school: number | null;
    createdDaysAgo: number;
  }): string => {
    const id = newId();
    insert.run(
      id,
      user.email,
      user.given,
      user.family,
      `${user.given} ${user.family}`,
      user.ou,
      user.admin ? 1 : 0,
      user.suspended ? 1 : 0,
      user.suspended ? 'ADMIN' : null,
      user.externalId,
      user.role,
      user.school,
      seededAt - user.createdDaysAgo * DAY_MS,
      lastLogin(user.suspended),
      ord++,
    );
    return id;
  };

  const students: string[][] = layout.schools.map(() => []);
  const staff: string[][] = layout.schools.map(() => []);
  const districtStaff: string[] = [];

  for (const [given, family, local] of ADMINS) {
    const email = address(spec.primaryDomain, local);
    add({
      email,
      given,
      family,
      ou: layout.districtStaffOu,
      admin: true,
      suspended: false,
      externalId: null,
      role: 'admin',
      school: null,
      createdDaysAgo: random.int(1000, 3000),
    });
    districtStaff.push(email);
  }

  const chooseSchool = weightedIndex(
    layout.schools.map((school) => school.weight),
    random.fork('school'),
  );
  const deepUserOu = () =>
    layout.deepUserOus.length > 0 && random.chance(0.02)
      ? random.pick(layout.deepUserOus)
      : null;

  for (let i = 0; i < spec.counts.staff; i++) {
    const given = random.pick(FIRST_NAMES);
    const family = random.pick(LAST_NAMES);
    const district = random.chance(0.08);
    const school = district ? null : layout.schools[chooseSchool()];
    const email = address(
      spec.primaryDomain,
      `${emailLocal(given)}.${emailLocal(family)}`,
    );
    const id = add({
      email,
      given,
      family,
      ou: deepUserOu() ?? school?.staffOu ?? layout.districtStaffOu,
      admin: false,
      suspended: random.chance(spec.rates.suspended),
      externalId: `E${100_000 + i}`,
      role: 'staff',
      school: school?.index ?? null,
      createdDaysAgo: random.int(30, 3650),
    });
    (school ? staff[school.index] : districtStaff).push(email);
    if (random.chance(0.05))
      insertAlias.run(
        address(
          spec.primaryDomain,
          `${emailLocal(given).slice(0, 1)}${emailLocal(family)}`,
        ),
        id,
      );
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < spec.counts.students; i++) {
    const given = random.pick(FIRST_NAMES);
    const family = random.pick(LAST_NAMES);
    const school = layout.schools[chooseSchool()];
    const grade = random.pick(school.grades);
    const externalId = String(200_000_000 + i);
    const year = String(GRADUATION_BASE_YEAR + 12 - grade).slice(2);
    const local =
      studentStyle === 'first.last'
        ? `${emailLocal(given)}.${emailLocal(family)}`
        : studentStyle === 'initial-last-year'
          ? `${emailLocal(given).slice(0, 1)}${emailLocal(family)}${year}`
          : externalId;
    const email = address(spec.studentDomain, local);
    add({
      email,
      given,
      family,
      ou: deepUserOu() ?? school.studentOus[grade],
      admin: false,
      suspended: random.chance(spec.rates.suspended),
      externalId,
      role: 'student',
      school: school.index,
      createdDaysAgo: random.int(30, (grade + 1) * 365),
    });
    students[school.index].push(email);
  }

  return {
    students,
    staff,
    districtStaff,
    adminEmail: `admin@${spec.primaryDomain}`,
  };
}
```

### Task 6: Device seeding, derived reports, and the full seed

**Files:**
- Create: `src/seed/models.ts`, `src/seed/devices.ts`, `src/seed/reports.ts`, `src/seed/seed.ts`
- Test: `src/seed/seed.test.ts`, `src/seed/reports.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5 context, `seedUsers`, `UserIndex`, `openDomainDb`; Task 3 layout and spec; Task 4 `transaction`, `row`.
- Produces: `ModelInfo`, `CURRENT_MODELS`, `EXPIRED_MODELS`, `MODEL_BY_NAME`, `aueMs(aue: string): number`, `CURRENT_OS`, `PLATFORM_FOR_OS`, `frozenOs(aue: string): string`.
- Produces: `StoredDevice` (one property per `devices` column, same names), `seedDevices(ctx: SeedContext, users: UserIndex): void`.
- Produces: `deviceReports(seed: string, device: StoredDevice): Record<string, unknown>`, `BatteryTelemetry`, `batteryTelemetry(seed: string, device: StoredDevice): BatteryTelemetry | null`, `batteryHealth(ratio: number): string`, `REPORT_DAYS = 30`.
- Produces: `SeedResult { counts: { orgUnits; users; devices }; adminEmail; durationMs }`, `seedDomain(db: DatabaseSync, spec: ResolvedSpec, seededAt: number): SeedResult`.

The auto-update dates come from Google's Auto Update policy page, read on 2026-10-09. Designs, capacities, and board names are plausible synthetic values.

```ts
export interface ModelInfo {
  name: string;
  manufacturer: 'Lenovo' | 'Acer' | 'Dell' | 'HP' | 'ASUS' | 'Samsung' | 'CTL';
  /** Automatic updates until, as YYYY-MM. */
  aue: string;
  ramGb: number;
  storageGb: number;
  /** Battery design capacity in mAh. */
  designCapacity: number;
  cpu: string;
  board: string;
  weight: number;
}

const model = (
  name: string,
  manufacturer: ModelInfo['manufacturer'],
  aue: string,
  ramGb: number,
  storageGb: number,
  designCapacity: number,
  cpu: string,
  board: string,
  weight: number,
): ModelInfo => ({
  name,
  manufacturer,
  aue,
  ramGb,
  storageGb,
  designCapacity,
  cpu,
  board,
  weight,
});

export const CURRENT_MODELS: readonly ModelInfo[] = [

];

export const EXPIRED_MODELS: readonly ModelInfo[] = [
  model('Lenovo N23 Chromebook', 'Lenovo', '2022-06', 4, 16, 4050, 'Intel(R) Celeron(R) N3060', 'reks', 3),
  model('Dell Chromebook 11 (3180)', 'Dell', '2022-06', 4, 16, 3900, 'Intel(R) Celeron(R) N3060', 'kefka', 3),
  model('HP Chromebook 11 G5 EE', 'HP', '2022-06', 4, 16, 3780, 'Intel(R) Celeron(R) N3060', 'setzer', 3),
  model('Acer Chromebook 11 (C740)', 'Acer', '2021-06', 2, 16, 3220, 'Intel(R) Celeron(R) 3205U', 'paine', 1),
  model('ASUS Chromebook C202SA', 'ASUS', '2022-06', 4, 16, 4800, 'Intel(R) Celeron(R) N3060', 'terra', 2),
  model('Samsung Chromebook 3', 'Samsung', '2022-06', 4, 16, 4080, 'Intel(R) Celeron(R) N3060', 'celes', 2),
];

export const MODEL_BY_NAME: ReadonlyMap<string, ModelInfo> = new Map(
  [...CURRENT_MODELS, ...EXPIRED_MODELS].map((info) => [info.name, info]),
);

/** The last second of the auto-update month, in epoch milliseconds. */
export function aueMs(aue: string): number {
  const [year, month] = aue.split('-').map(Number);
  return Date.UTC(year, month, 0, 23, 59, 59);
}

export const CURRENT_OS: readonly (readonly [string, number])[] = [
  ['141.0.7390.122', 0.7],
  ['140.0.7339.207', 0.2],
  ['139.0.7258.163', 0.1],
];

export const PLATFORM_FOR_OS: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {
  '141.0.7390.122': '16404.45.0',
  '140.0.7339.207': '16371.61.0',
  '139.0.7258.163': '16328.65.0',
  '103.0.5060.132': '14816.131.0',
  '91.0.4472.147': '13904.97.0',
};

/** The last OS version that an expired model received. */
export function frozenOs(aue: string): string {
  return aue < '2022-01' ? '91.0.4472.147' : '103.0.5060.132';
}
```
