- [ ] **Step 2: Write the device seeder**

`src/seed/devices.ts`:

```ts
import { createRandom } from '../random.ts';
import { DAY_MS, HEX, weightedIndex, type SeedContext } from './context.ts';
import type { DeviceCategory } from './layout.ts';
import {
  aueMs,
  CURRENT_MODELS,
  CURRENT_OS,
  EXPIRED_MODELS,
  frozenOs,
  PLATFORM_FOR_OS,
  type ModelInfo,
} from './models.ts';
import type { UserIndex } from './users.ts';

/** One row of the `devices` table. */
export interface StoredDevice {
  device_id: string;
  serial_number: string;
  status: string;
  model: string;
  org_unit_path: string;
  org_unit_id: string;
  annotated_user: string | null;
  annotated_asset_id: string | null;
  annotated_location: string | null;
  notes: string | null;
  last_sync: number | null;
  first_enrollment_time: number;
  last_enrollment_time: number;
  mac_address: string;
  ethernet_mac_address: string | null;
  os_version: string;
  platform_version: string;
  firmware_version: string;
  boot_mode: string;
  aue: number;
  recent_users: string | null;
  last_user_activity: number | null;
  has_battery: number;
  ram_total: number;
  storage_total: number;
  order_number: string | null;
  license_type: string;
  deprovision_reason: string | null;
  last_deprovision: number | null;
  category: string;
  misplaced: number;
  ord: number;
}

const SERIAL: Record<ModelInfo['manufacturer'], [string, number]> = {
  Lenovo: ['PF', 8],
  Acer: ['NXH', 22],
  Dell: ['', 7],
  HP: ['5CD', 10],
  ASUS: ['M', 12],
  Samsung: ['R', 15],
  CTL: ['CTL', 12],
};
const SERIAL_ALPHABET = '0123456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ';
const NOTES = [
  'Screen replaced',
  'Loaner device',
  'Keyboard sticky, monitor',
  'Returned from repair',
  'Battery swelling reported',
];
const DEPROVISION_REASONS = [
  'DEPROVISION_REASON_RETIRING_DEVICE',
  'DEPROVISION_REASON_SAME_MODEL_REPLACEMENT',
  'DEPROVISION_REASON_DIFFERENT_MODEL_REPLACEMENT',
  'DEPROVISION_REASON_REPAIR_CENTER',
];
const COLUMNS = [
  'device_id', 'serial_number', 'status', 'model', 'org_unit_path', 'org_unit_id',
  'annotated_user', 'annotated_asset_id', 'annotated_location', 'notes', 'last_sync',
  'first_enrollment_time', 'last_enrollment_time', 'mac_address', 'ethernet_mac_address',
  'os_version', 'platform_version', 'firmware_version', 'boot_mode', 'aue', 'recent_users',
  'last_user_activity', 'has_battery', 'ram_total', 'storage_total', 'order_number',
  'license_type', 'deprovision_reason', 'last_deprovision', 'category', 'misplaced', 'ord',
] as const satisfies readonly (keyof StoredDevice)[];

/** Insert every device. The caller owns the transaction. */
export function seedDevices(ctx: SeedContext, users: UserIndex): void {
  const { spec, layout, seededAt, db } = ctx;
  const { rates } = spec;
  const random = createRandom(`devices/${spec.seed}`);
  const ouIds = new Map(
    [layout.root, ...layout.units].map((unit) => [unit.path, unit.orgUnitId]),
  );
  const chooseSchool = weightedIndex(
    layout.schools.map((school) => school.weight),
    random.fork('school'),
  );
  const assetPrefix = random.pick(['', 'CB', 'A-', 'TAG-']);
  const serials = new Set<string>();
  const insert = db.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO devices (${COLUMNS.join(', ')}) VALUES (${COLUMNS.map(() => '?').join(', ')})`,
  );

  for (let ord = 0; ord < spec.counts.devices; ord++) {
    const district = random.chance(0.03);
    const school = district ? null : layout.schools[chooseSchool()];
    const category: DeviceCategory = district
      ? 'staff'
      : random.weighted<DeviceCategory>([
          ['one-to-one', 0.77],
          ['cart', 0.1],
          ['staff', 0.12],
          ['kiosk', 0.01],
        ]);
    const roll = random.next();
    const status =
      roll < rates.disabled
        ? 'DISABLED'
        : roll < rates.disabled + rates.deprovisioned
          ? 'DEPROVISIONED'
          : 'ACTIVE';
    const expired = random.chance(rates.expired);
    const info = random.weighted(
      (expired ? EXPIRED_MODELS : CURRENT_MODELS).map(
        (entry) => [entry, entry.weight] as const,
      ),
    );
    const misplaced = random.chance(rates.misplaced);
    const deep =
      !misplaced && layout.deepDeviceOus.length > 0 && random.chance(0.02);
    const ou = misplaced
      ? school
        ? school.studentOus[random.pick(school.grades)]
        : layout.districtStaffOu
      : deep
        ? random.pick(layout.deepDeviceOus)
        : school
          ? school.deviceOus[category]
          : layout.districtDeviceOu;

    const students = school ? users.students[school.index] : [];
    const staff = school ? users.staff[school.index] : users.districtStaff;
    const unassigned = random.chance(rates.unassigned);
    let annotatedUser: string | null = null;
    let recent: string[] = [];
    if (category === 'one-to-one' && students.length > 0 && !unassigned) {
      annotatedUser = random.pick(students);
      recent = [annotatedUser];
    } else if (category === 'staff' && staff.length > 0 && !unassigned) {
      annotatedUser = random.pick(staff);
      recent = [annotatedUser];
    } else if (category === 'cart' && students.length > 0)
      recent = Array.from({ length: random.int(1, 3) }, () =>
        random.pick(students),
      );
    if (status === 'DEPROVISIONED') recent = [];

    const aueYear = Number(info.aue.slice(0, 4));
    const purchase = Math.min(
      Date.UTC(
        aueYear - 10 + random.int(0, 2),
        random.int(5, 8),
        random.int(1, 28),
      ),
      seededAt - 30 * DAY_MS,
    );
    const firstEnrollment = Math.min(
      purchase + random.int(1, 60) * DAY_MS,
      seededAt - DAY_MS,
    );
    const syncRoll = random.next();
    const syncAge =
      status === 'ACTIVE'
        ? syncRoll < 0.7
          ? random.int(0, 3 * DAY_MS)
          : syncRoll < 0.9
            ? random.int(3 * DAY_MS, 30 * DAY_MS)
            : random.int(30 * DAY_MS, 365 * DAY_MS)
        : status === 'DISABLED'
          ? random.int(DAY_MS, 120 * DAY_MS)
          : random.int(365 * DAY_MS, 1095 * DAY_MS);
    const lastSync = Math.max(seededAt - syncAge, firstEnrollment + DAY_MS);
    const lastEnrollment = random.chance(0.2)
      ? firstEnrollment + random.int(0, lastSync - firstEnrollment)
      : firstEnrollment;
    const osVersion = expired
      ? frozenOs(info.aue)
      : random.weighted(CURRENT_OS);
    const [prefix, length] = SERIAL[info.manufacturer];
    let serial: string;
    do serial = prefix + random.string(length - prefix.length, SERIAL_ALPHABET);
    while (serials.has(serial));
    serials.add(serial);

    const device: StoredDevice = {
      device_id: random.uuid(),
      serial_number: serial,
      status,
      model: info.name,
      org_unit_path: ou,
      org_unit_id: ouIds.get(ou)!,
      annotated_user: annotatedUser,
      annotated_asset_id: random.chance(0.9)
        ? `${assetPrefix}${String(random.int(1, 999_999)).padStart(6, '0')}`
        : null,
      annotated_location: random.chance(0.3)
        ? random.pick([
            'Library',
            'Media Center',
            `Room ${random.int(100, 399)}`,
            category === 'cart' ? `Cart ${random.int(1, 40)}` : 'Front Office',
          ])
        : null,
      notes: random.chance(0.05) ? random.pick(NOTES) : null,
      last_sync: lastSync,
      first_enrollment_time: firstEnrollment,
      last_enrollment_time: lastEnrollment,
      mac_address: random.string(12, HEX),
      ethernet_mac_address: random.chance(0.3) ? random.string(12, HEX) : null,
      os_version: osVersion,
      platform_version: `${PLATFORM_FOR_OS[osVersion]} (Official Build) stable-channel ${info.board}`,
      firmware_version: `Google_${info.board[0].toUpperCase()}${info.board.slice(1)}.${random.int(10_000, 15_999)}.${random.int(0, 500)}.0`,
      boot_mode: random.chance(0.005) ? 'Dev' : 'Verified',
      aue: aueMs(info.aue),
      recent_users: recent.length > 0 ? JSON.stringify(recent) : null,
      last_user_activity:
        recent.length > 0 ? lastSync - random.int(0, 6 * 3_600_000) : null,
      has_battery: random.chance(0.95) ? 1 : 0,
      ram_total: info.ramGb * 1024 ** 3,
      storage_total: info.storageGb * 1_000_000_000,
      order_number: random.chance(0.5)
        ? `PO-${random.int(10_000, 99_999)}`
        : null,
      license_type: random.weighted([
        ['educationUpgradePerpetual', 0.6],
        ['educationUpgrade', 0.3],
        ['education', 0.1],
      ]),
      deprovision_reason:
        status === 'DEPROVISIONED' ? random.pick(DEPROVISION_REASONS) : null,
      last_deprovision:
        status === 'DEPROVISIONED'
          ? Math.min(lastSync + random.int(1, 30) * DAY_MS, seededAt)
          : null,
      category,
      misplaced: misplaced ? 1 : 0,
      ord,
    };
    insert.run(...COLUMNS.map((column) => device[column]));
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the report generator**

`src/seed/reports.ts`:

```ts
import { createRandom } from '../random.ts';
import { DAY_MS, HEX, iso, YEAR_MS } from './context.ts';
import type { StoredDevice } from './devices.ts';
import { MODEL_BY_NAME } from './models.ts';

export const REPORT_DAYS = 30;

/** Google's classification: above 80% normal, 75% to 80% soon, below 75% now. */
export function batteryHealth(ratio: number): string {
  return ratio > 0.8
    ? 'BATTERY_HEALTH_NORMAL'
    : ratio >= 0.75
      ? 'BATTERY_REPLACE_SOON'
      : 'BATTERY_REPLACE_NOW';
}

/** The report arrays of the FULL projection, computed the same way on every read. */
export function deviceReports(
  seed: string,
  device: StoredDevice,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (device.last_sync === null) return {};
  const random = createRandom(`reports/${seed}/${device.device_id}`);
  const sync = device.last_sync;
  const cpu = MODEL_BY_NAME.get(device.model)?.cpu ?? 'Intel(R) Celeron(R) N4500';
  const cpus = 2;
  const used = Math.round(device.storage_total * (0.2 + random.next() * 0.6));
  const recent = device.recent_users
    ? (JSON.parse(device.recent_users) as string[])
    : [];
  return {
    ...(recent.length > 0
      ? {
          recentUsers: recent.map((email) => ({
            type: 'USER_TYPE_MANAGED',
            email,
          })),
        }
      : {}),
    activeTimeRanges: Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, k) => ({
      date: iso(sync - (6 - k) * DAY_MS).slice(0, 10),
      activeTime: random.int(0, 6 * 3_600_000),
    })),
    cpuStatusReports: Array.from({ length: 3 }, (_, k) => ({
      reportTime: iso(sync - (2 - k) * 600_000),
      cpuUtilizationPercentageInfo: Array.from({ length: cpus }, () =>
        random.int(1, 95),
      ),
      cpuTemperatureInfo: [{ label: 'Core 0', temperature: random.int(38, 78) }],
    })),
    diskVolumeReports: [
      {
        volumeInfo: [
          {
            volumeId: '/home/chronos',
            storageTotal: String(device.storage_total),
            storageFree: String(device.storage_total - used),
          },
        ],
      },
    ],
    systemRamFreeReports: Array.from({ length: 3 }, (_, k) => ({
      reportTime: iso(sync - (2 - k) * 600_000),
      systemRamFreeInfo: [
        String(Math.round(device.ram_total * (0.1 + random.next() * 0.5))),
      ],
    })),
    lastKnownNetwork: [
      {
        ipAddress: `10.${random.int(0, 255)}.${random.int(0, 255)}.${random.int(1, 254)}`,
        wanIpAddress: `203.0.113.${random.int(1, 254)}`,
      },
    ],
    cpuInfo: [
      {
        model: cpu,
        architecture: 'x64',
        maxClockSpeedKhz: random.pick([2_800_000, 3_300_000, 3_400_000]),
        logicalCpus: Array.from({ length: cpus }, () => ({
          maxScalingFrequencyKhz: 2_800_000,
          currentScalingFrequencyKhz: random.int(800_000, 2_800_000),
          idleDuration: `${random.int(100, 90_000)}s`,
          cStates: [
            { displayName: 'C1', sessionDuration: `${random.int(10, 5_000)}s` },
          ],
        })),
      },
    ],
    diskSpaceUsage: {
      capacityBytes: String(device.storage_total),
      usedBytes: String(used),
    },
    tpmVersionInfo: {
      family: '322e3000',
      specLevel: '74',
      manufacturer: '49465800',
      tpmModel: '1',
      firmwareVersion: String(random.int(100_000, 999_999)),
      vendorSpecific: random.string(12, HEX),
    },
  };
}

export interface BatteryTelemetry {
  batteryInfo: Record<string, unknown>[];
  batteryStatusReport: Record<string, unknown>[];
}

/** Battery design data and 30 daily status reports, oldest first. */
export function batteryTelemetry(
  seed: string,
  device: StoredDevice,
): BatteryTelemetry | null {
  if (!device.has_battery || device.last_sync === null) return null;
  const random = createRandom(`battery/${seed}/${device.device_id}`);
  const design = MODEL_BY_NAME.get(device.model)?.designCapacity ?? 4000;
  const sync = device.last_sync;
  const ageYears = Math.max(
    0,
    (sync - device.first_enrollment_time) / YEAR_MS,
  );
  const ratio = Math.min(
    1,
    Math.max(0.5, 1 - 0.045 * ageYears + random.normal() * 0.03),
  );
  const serialNumber = `${device.serial_number}B`;
  const cycles = Math.round(ageYears * 250);
  const made = new Date(device.first_enrollment_time - 60 * DAY_MS);
  return {
    batteryInfo: [
      {
        serialNumber,
        manufacturer: random.pick(['SMP', 'LGC', 'SDI', 'Celxpert']),
        designCapacity: String(design),
        designMinVoltage: random.pick([7600, 11250]),
        technology: 'Li-ion',
        manufactureDate: {
          year: made.getUTCFullYear(),
          month: made.getUTCMonth() + 1,
          day: made.getUTCDate(),
        },
      },
    ],
    batteryStatusReport: Array.from({ length: REPORT_DAYS }, (_, index) => {
      const daysAgo = REPORT_DAYS - 1 - index;
      const charge = Math.round(design * Math.min(1, ratio + 0.0004 * daysAgo));
      return {
        reportTime: iso(sync - daysAgo * DAY_MS - random.int(0, 3_600_000)),
        serialNumber,
        fullChargeCapacity: String(charge),
        cycleCount: Math.max(0, cycles - daysAgo),
        batteryHealth: batteryHealth(charge / design),
      };
    }),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the full seed**

`src/seed/seed.ts`:

```ts
import type { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { row, transaction } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { DAY_MS } from './context.ts';
import { seedDevices } from './devices.ts';
import { buildLayout } from './layout.ts';
import type { ResolvedSpec } from './spec.ts';
import { seedUsers } from './users.ts';

export interface SeedResult {
  counts: { orgUnits: number; users: number; devices: number };
  adminEmail: string;
  durationMs: number;
}

/** Write one complete domain into an empty domain database. */
export function seedDomain(
  db: DatabaseSync,
  spec: ResolvedSpec,
  seededAt: number,
): SeedResult {
  const started = performance.now();
  const layout = buildLayout(spec);
  const ctx = { spec, layout, seededAt, db };
  const users = transaction(db, () => {
    const meta = db.prepare('INSERT INTO meta (key, value) VALUES (?, ?)');
    meta.run('spec', JSON.stringify(spec));
    meta.run('seededAt', String(seededAt));
    meta.run('deviceCount', String(spec.counts.devices));
    const domain = db.prepare(
      'INSERT INTO domains (domain_name, is_primary, verified, creation_time, alias_of) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
    );
    const created = seededAt - 3000 * DAY_MS;
    domain.run(spec.primaryDomain, 1, 1, created, null);
    if (spec.studentDomain !== spec.primaryDomain)
      domain.run(spec.studentDomain, 0, 1, created + 30 * DAY_MS, null);
    domain.run(spec.aliasDomain, 0, 1, created + 60 * DAY_MS, spec.primaryDomain);
    const unit = db.prepare(
      'INSERT INTO orgunits (org_unit_id, path, name, description, parent_id, parent_path, depth, kind, ord) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
    );
    [layout.root, ...layout.units].forEach((node, ord) =>
      unit.run(
        node.orgUnitId,
        node.path,
        node.name,
        node.description,
        node.parentId,
        node.parentPath,
        node.depth,
        node.kind,
        ord,
      ),
    );
    const index = seedUsers(ctx);
    seedDevices(ctx, index);
    return index;
  });
  const count = (table: string) =>
    Number(
      row<{ n: number }>(db.prepare(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM ${table}`).get())
        ?.n ?? 0,
    );
  return {
    counts: {
      orgUnits: count('orgunits'),
      users: count('users'),
      devices: count('devices'),
    },
    adminEmail: users.adminEmail,
    durationMs: Math.round(performance.now() - started),
  };
}
```

`src/seed/seed.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { openDomainDb } from '../store/domain-db.ts';
import { row, rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { hashDatabase } from '../testing/db-hash.ts';
import { DAY_MS } from './context.ts';
import { seedDomain } from './seed.ts';
import { resolveSpec } from './spec.ts';

const SEEDED_AT = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 12);
const INPUT = {
  tier: 'medium',
  seed: 'rates',
  counts: { schools: 8, students: 4000, staff: 400, devices: 6000 },
};

function seeded(input: Record<string, unknown> = INPUT) {
  const db = openDomainDb(':memory:');
  const spec = resolveSpec(input);
  const result = seedDomain(db, spec, SEEDED_AT);
  return { db, spec, result };
}

const shared = seeded();
const share = (where: string, of = '1 = 1') =>
  Number(
    row<{ n: number }>(
      shared.db
        .prepare(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices WHERE (${of}) AND (${where})`)
        .get(),
    )?.n,
  ) /
  Number(
    row<{ n: number }>(
      shared.db.prepare(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices WHERE ${of}`).get(),
    )?.n,
  );

test('the seed reports the requested counts', () => {
  assert.equal(shared.result.counts.devices, 6000);
  assert.equal(shared.result.counts.users, 4403);
  assert.ok(shared.result.counts.orgUnits > 50);
  assert.equal(shared.result.adminEmail, `admin@${shared.spec.primaryDomain}`);
});

test('device statuses follow the rates', () => {
  assert.ok(Math.abs(share("status = 'DISABLED'") - 0.02) < 0.01);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(share("status = 'DEPROVISIONED'") - 0.05) < 0.015);
});

test('misplaced, unassigned, and expired shares follow the rates', () => {
  assert.ok(Math.abs(share('misplaced = 1') - 0.02) < 0.01);
  assert.ok(
    Math.abs(
      share(
        'annotated_user IS NULL',
        "category IN ('one-to-one', 'staff')",
      ) - 0.15,
    ) < 0.03,
  );
  assert.ok(Math.abs(share(`aue < ${SEEDED_AT}`) - 0.15) < 0.03);
});

test('every device points at an existing OU of the expected kind', () => {
  const mismatches = rows<{ n: number }>(
    shared.db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices d LEFT JOIN orgunits o ON o.path = d.org_unit_path
         WHERE o.path IS NULL OR o.org_unit_id != d.org_unit_id
            OR (d.misplaced = 0 AND o.kind != 'devices')
            OR (d.misplaced = 1 AND o.kind != 'users')`,
      )
      .all(),
  );
  assert.equal(mismatches[0].n, 0);
});

test('timestamps stay in order and in the past', () => {
  const bad = row<{ n: number }>(
    shared.db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices WHERE last_sync > ? OR last_sync < first_enrollment_time
           OR last_enrollment_time < first_enrollment_time OR last_enrollment_time > last_sync`,
      )
      .get(SEEDED_AT),
  );
  assert.equal(bad?.n, 0);
  const recent = share(
    `last_sync >= ${SEEDED_AT - 3 * DAY_MS}`,
    "status = 'ACTIVE'",
  );
  assert.ok(Math.abs(recent - 0.7) < 0.05, `recent ${recent}`);
});

test('deprovisioned devices carry a reason and no recent users', () => {
  assert.equal(
    share(
      'deprovision_reason IS NULL OR recent_users IS NOT NULL',
      "status = 'DEPROVISIONED'",
    ),
    0,
  );
});

test('the domain table holds the primary domain and its alias', () => {
  const domains = rows<{ domain_name: string; alias_of: string | null }>(
    shared.db.prepare('SELECT domain_name, alias_of FROM domains').all(),
  );
  assert.ok(
    domains.some((domain) => domain.domain_name === shared.spec.primaryDomain),
  );
  assert.ok(
    domains.some(
      (domain) =>
        domain.domain_name === shared.spec.aliasDomain &&
        domain.alias_of === shared.spec.primaryDomain,
    ),
  );
});

test('the same specification and seeding time produce identical data', () => {
  const small = { tier: 'small', seed: 'same', counts: { devices: 300 } };
  assert.equal(hashDatabase(seeded(small).db), hashDatabase(seeded(small).db));
  assert.notEqual(
    hashDatabase(seeded(small).db),
    hashDatabase(seeded({ ...small, seed: 'different' }).db),
  );
});
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { openDomainDb } from '../store/domain-db.ts';
import { rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import type { StoredDevice } from './devices.ts';
import {
  batteryHealth,
  batteryTelemetry,
  deviceReports,
  REPORT_DAYS,
} from './reports.ts';
import { seedDomain } from './seed.ts';
import { resolveSpec } from './spec.ts';

const db = openDomainDb(':memory:');
seedDomain(
  db,
  resolveSpec({ tier: 'small', seed: 'reports', counts: { devices: 2000 } }),
  Date.UTC(2026, 9, 9, 12),
);
const devices = rows<StoredDevice>(
  db.prepare("SELECT * FROM devices WHERE status = 'ACTIVE'").all(),
);

test('reports are identical on every read', () => {
  const device = devices[0];
  assert.deepEqual(
    deviceReports('reports', device),
    deviceReports('reports', device),
  );
  assert.deepEqual(
    batteryTelemetry('reports', device),
    batteryTelemetry('reports', device),
  );
});

test('the FULL report arrays are present', () => {
  const reports = deviceReports('reports', devices[0]);
  for (const key of [
    'activeTimeRanges',
    'cpuStatusReports',
    'diskVolumeReports',
    'systemRamFreeReports',
    'lastKnownNetwork',
    'cpuInfo',
    'diskSpaceUsage',
    'tpmVersionInfo',
  ])
    assert.ok(key in reports, key);
});

test('recent users come from the stored list', () => {
  const device = devices.find((entry) => entry.recent_users !== null)!;
  const reports = deviceReports('reports', device) as {
    recentUsers: { email: string }[];
  };
  assert.deepEqual(
    reports.recentUsers.map((user) => user.email),
    JSON.parse(device.recent_users!),
  );
});

test('battery reports run oldest first and match their health class', () => {
  const telemetry = batteryTelemetry(
    'reports',
    devices.find((entry) => entry.has_battery === 1)!,
  )!;
  const reports = telemetry.batteryStatusReport as {
    reportTime: string;
    fullChargeCapacity: string;
    batteryHealth: string;
  }[];
  assert.equal(reports.length, REPORT_DAYS);
  const design = Number(
    (telemetry.batteryInfo[0] as { designCapacity: string }).designCapacity,
  );
  for (let i = 1; i < reports.length; i++)
    assert.ok(reports[i].reportTime > reports[i - 1].reportTime);
  for (const report of reports)
    assert.equal(
      report.batteryHealth,
      batteryHealth(Number(report.fullChargeCapacity) / design),
    );
});

test('devices without a battery report no battery telemetry', () => {
  const device = devices.find((entry) => entry.has_battery === 0);
  assert.ok(device, 'The seed includes a device without a battery.');
  assert.equal(batteryTelemetry('reports', device), null);
});

test('the fleet covers every battery health class', () => {
  const classes = new Set(
    devices
      .map((device) => batteryTelemetry('reports', device))
      .filter((telemetry) => telemetry !== null)
      .map(
        (telemetry) =>
          (telemetry.batteryStatusReport.at(-1) as { batteryHealth: string })
            .batteryHealth,
      ),
  );
  assert.deepEqual([...classes].sort(), [
    'BATTERY_HEALTH_NORMAL',
    'BATTERY_REPLACE_NOW',
    'BATTERY_REPLACE_SOON',
  ]);
});
```

### Task 7: HTTP core, domain lifecycle, and the dispatch pipeline

**Files:**
- Create: `src/http/types.ts`, `src/http/errors.ts`, `src/http/router.ts`, `src/domains.ts`, `src/pipeline.ts`, `src/routes.ts`, `src/mock.ts`, `src/testing/harness.ts`
- Test: `src/http/router.test.ts`, `src/domains.test.ts`, `src/mock.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything from Tasks 1 through 6.
- Produces in `src/http/types.ts`: `Api = 'oauth' | 'directory' | 'chrome' | 'batch'`, `ApiRequest`, `ApiResponse`, `Access`, `OpenDomain`, `Overrides`, `NO_OVERRIDES`, `Call`, `Route`, `Authenticator`.
- Produces in `src/http/errors.ts`: `ApiError`, `directoryError(status, reason, message, options?)`, `rpcError(status, rpcStatus, message, details?)`, `transientError(api)`, `internalError(api)`, `notFoundPage(path)`.
- Produces: `matchRoute(routes, request)`, `Pipeline`, `Admission`, `openPipeline()`, `buildRoutes(mock: Mock): Route[]`, `DomainError`, `Domains`, `createDomains(dataDir, registry)`, `Mock`, `MockOptions`, `createMock(options)`, `finalize(response, request, route)`.
- Produces in `src/testing/harness.ts`: `Harness`, `HarnessResponse`, `RequestOptions`, `startHarness(options?)`.

`OpenDomain` is `{ customerId: string; record: StoredDomain; spec: ResolvedSpec; db: DatabaseSync }`. The `record` property changes when settings or the seeding time change.

`Call` is `{ route; params; request; access: Access | null; domain: OpenDomain | null; batchPart: boolean; overrides: Overrides; dispatchPart(request): Promise<ApiResponse> }`.

`Route` is `{ id; api; hosts; methods; pattern: RegExp; scopes; admin: boolean; handler(call) }`. Route IDs use Google method names, for example `directory.chromeosdevices.list`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the request types**

`src/http/types.ts`:

```ts
import type { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import type { ResolvedSpec } from '../seed/spec.ts';
import type { StoredDomain, StoredToken } from '../store/registry.ts';

export type Api = 'oauth' | 'directory' | 'chrome' | 'batch';

export interface ApiRequest {
  method: string;
  /** Lower-case host name without a port. */
  host: string;
  /** The encoded URL path. */
  path: string;
  query: URLSearchParams;
  /** Lower-case header names. */
  headers: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  body: string;
}

export interface ApiResponse {
  status: number;
  /** A JSON body. `finalize` serializes it into `raw`. */
  body?: unknown;
  /** The serialized body. */
  raw?: string;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  /** Items returned, for the latency model. */
  items?: number;
  /** Rows scanned by a filter, for the latency model. */
  scanned?: number;
}

export interface Access {
  token: StoredToken;
  customerId: string;
  subject: string;
  isAdmin: boolean;
}

export interface OpenDomain {
  customerId: string;
  record: StoredDomain;
  spec: ResolvedSpec;
  db: DatabaseSync;
}

/** State changes that faults apply on top of stored data. */
export interface Overrides {
  grant(
    customerId: string,
    clientId: string,
    scopes: readonly string[] | undefined,
  ): readonly string[] | undefined;
  isAdmin(customerId: string, email: string, isAdmin: boolean): boolean;
  /** Device and user IDs that requests must not see. */
  hidden(customerId: string): ReadonlySet<string>;
}

const NOTHING: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set();

export const NO_OVERRIDES: Overrides = {
  grant: (_customerId, _clientId, scopes) => scopes,
  isAdmin: (_customerId, _email, isAdmin) => isAdmin,
  hidden: () => NOTHING,
};

export interface Call {
  route: Route;
  params: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  request: ApiRequest;
  access: Access | null;
  domain: OpenDomain | null;
  batchPart: boolean;
  overrides: Overrides;
  /** Run one batch part through the full pipeline. */
  dispatchPart(request: ApiRequest): Promise<ApiResponse>;
}

export interface Route {
  id: string;
  api: Api;
  hosts: readonly string[];
  methods: readonly string[];
  pattern: RegExp;
  /** The token needs at least one of these scopes. */
  scopes: readonly string[];
  /** The delegated subject must be a super administrator. */
  admin: boolean;
  handler(call: Call): ApiResponse | Promise<ApiResponse>;
}

export interface Authenticator {
  authenticate(
    request: ApiRequest,
    route: Route,
  ): { access: Access; domain: OpenDomain } | ApiResponse;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the error envelopes**

`src/http/errors.ts`:

```ts
import type { Api, ApiResponse } from './types.ts';

/** Throw from a handler to answer with a specific response. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly response: ApiResponse;
  constructor(response: ApiResponse) {
    super(`Google API error ${response.status}`);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
    this.response = response;
  }
}

export interface DirectoryErrorOptions {
  /** `errors[0].domain`. Defaults to `global`. */
  domain?: string;
  /** `errors[0].message` when it differs from the top message. */
  itemMessage?: string;
  /** Extra properties of `errors[0]`, such as `location`. */
  item?: Record<string, unknown>;
  /** Extra properties of `error`, such as `status` and `details`. */
  extra?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

/** The classic Directory API error envelope. */
export function directoryError(
  status: number,
  reason: string,
  message: string,
  options: DirectoryErrorOptions = {},
): ApiResponse {
  return {
    status,
    body: {
      error: {
        code: status,
        message,
        errors: [
          {
            message: options.itemMessage ?? message,
            domain: options.domain ?? 'global',
            reason,
            ...options.item,
          },
        ],
        ...options.extra,
      },
    },
  };
}

/** The Google RPC error envelope that Chrome Management uses. */
export function rpcError(
  status: number,
  rpcStatus: string,
  message: string,
  details?: unknown[],
): ApiResponse {
  return {
    status,
    body: {
      error: {
        code: status,
        message,
        status: rpcStatus,
        ...(details ? { details } : {}),
      },
    },
  };
}

export function transientError(api: Api): ApiResponse {
  return api === 'chrome'
    ? rpcError(503, 'UNAVAILABLE', 'The service is currently unavailable.')
    : directoryError(503, 'backendError', 'Backend Error');
}

export function internalError(api: Api): ApiResponse {
  return api === 'chrome'
    ? rpcError(500, 'INTERNAL', 'Internal error encountered.')
    : directoryError(500, 'backendError', 'Backend Error');
}

const escapeHtml = (text: string) =>
  text.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (character) => `&#${character.charCodeAt(0)};`);

/** Google answers an unknown URL with an HTML page. */
export function notFoundPage(path: string): ApiResponse {
  return {
    status: 404,
    headers: { 'content-type': 'text/html; charset=UTF-8' },
    raw: `<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=en>\n  <meta charset=utf-8>\n  <title>Error 404 (Not Found)!!1</title>\n  <p><b>404.</b> <ins>That's an error.</ins>\n  <p>The requested URL <code>${escapeHtml(path)}</code> was not found on this server.  <ins>That's all we know.</ins>\n`,
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the router, the pipeline, and the empty route table**

`src/http/router.ts`:

```ts
import type { ApiRequest, Route } from './types.ts';

/** Find the first route for the host, the method, and the path. Parameters are decoded. */
export function matchRoute(
  routes: readonly Route[],
  request: ApiRequest,
): { route: Route; params: Record<string, string> } | undefined {
  for (const route of routes) {
    if (!route.hosts.includes(request.host)) continue;
    if (!route.methods.includes(request.method)) continue;
    const match = route.pattern.exec(request.path);
    if (!match) continue;
    const params: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(match.groups ?? {})) {
      try {
        params[key] = decodeURIComponent(value);
      } catch {
        return undefined;
      }
    }
    return { route, params };
  }
  return undefined;
}
```

`src/pipeline.ts`:

```ts
import type { ApiResponse, Call } from './http/types.ts';

export type Admission =
  | { ok: true; release(): void }
  | { ok: false; response: ApiResponse };

/** The three stages around a handler. Faults, quota, and latency fill them in. */
export interface Pipeline {
  before(call: Call): Promise<ApiResponse | 'reset' | undefined>;
  admit(call: Call): Admission;
  delay(call: Call, response: ApiResponse): Promise<void>;
}

export function openPipeline(): Pipeline {
  return {
    before: async () => undefined,
    admit: () => ({ ok: true, release() {} }),
    delay: async () => {},
  };
}
```

```ts
import type { Mock } from './mock.ts';

/** Every Google route. Later tasks add their routes here. */
export function buildRoutes(_mock: Mock): Route[] {
  return [];
}
```

```ts
import test from 'node:test';
import { matchRoute } from './router.ts';
import type { ApiRequest, Route } from './types.ts';

const route = (id: string, pattern: RegExp, methods = ['GET']): Route => ({
  id,
  api: 'directory',
  hosts: ['admin.googleapis.com'],
  methods,
  pattern,
  scopes: [],
  admin: false,
  handler: () => ({ status: 200 }),
});

const request = (path: string, method = 'GET', host = 'admin.googleapis.com'): ApiRequest => ({
  method,
  host,
  path,
  query: new URLSearchParams(),
  headers: {},
  body: '',
});

const routes = [
  route('get', /^\/items\/(?<id>[^/]+)$/),
  route('nested', /^\/tree\/(?<path>.+)$/),
  route('post', /^\/items$/, ['POST']),
];

test('a route matches on host, method, and path, and decodes parameters', () => {

  assert.equal(match?.route.id, 'get');

  assert.deepEqual(
    matchRoute(routes, request('/tree/Students/Lincoln%20Elementary'))?.params,
    { path: 'Students/Lincoln Elementary' },
  );
  assert.equal(matchRoute(routes, request('/items', 'POST'))?.route.id, 'post');
});

test('a wrong host, method, or path matches nothing', () => {
  assert.equal(matchRoute(routes, request('/items/x', 'GET', 'www.googleapis.com')), undefined);
  assert.equal(matchRoute(routes, request('/items', 'GET')), undefined);
  assert.equal(matchRoute(routes, request('/other')), undefined);
});

test('a malformed percent sequence matches nothing', () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the domain lifecycle**

`src/domains.ts`:

```ts
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import type { OpenDomain } from './http/types.ts';
import { seedDomain, type SeedResult } from './seed/seed.ts';
import { resolveSpec, SpecError, type SeedSpec } from './seed/spec.ts';
import {
  DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
  mergeSettings,
  SettingsError,
  type DomainSettings,
} from './settings.ts';
import { openDomainDb } from './store/domain-db.ts';
import type { Registry, StoredDomain } from './store/registry.ts';

export class DomainError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  constructor(status: number, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'DomainError';
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export interface Domains {
  get(customerId: string): OpenDomain | undefined;
  create(input: unknown, now: number): { domain: OpenDomain; result: SeedResult };
  reset(customerId: string, now: number): SeedResult;
  delete(customerId: string): void;
  list(): StoredDomain[];
  updateSettings(customerId: string, patch: unknown): DomainSettings;
  closeAll(): void;
}

export function createDomains(dataDir: string, registry: Registry): Domains {
  const directory = join(dataDir, 'domains');
  mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true });
  const open = new Map<string, OpenDomain>();
  const fileFor = (customerId: string) => join(directory, `${customerId}.sqlite`);
  const removeFiles = (customerId: string) => {
    for (const suffix of ['', '-wal', '-shm'])
      rmSync(`${fileFor(customerId)}${suffix}`, { force: true });
  };
  const close = (customerId: string) => {
    open.get(customerId)?.db.close();
    open.delete(customerId);
  };
  const missing = (customerId: string) =>
    new DomainError(404, `Domain ${customerId} does not exist.`);

  const domains: Domains = {
    get(customerId) {
      const cached = open.get(customerId);
      if (cached) return cached;
      const record = registry.getDomain(customerId);
      if (!record || !existsSync(fileFor(customerId))) return undefined;
      const domain: OpenDomain = {
        customerId,
        record,
        spec: resolveSpec(record.spec),
        db: openDomainDb(fileFor(customerId)),
      };
      open.set(customerId, domain);
      return domain;
    },
    create(input, now) {
      let spec;
      try {
        spec = resolveSpec(input);
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof SpecError) throw new DomainError(400, error.message);
        throw error;
      }
      if (registry.getDomain(spec.customerId))
        throw new DomainError(
          409,
          `Domain ${spec.customerId} already exists. Use a different seed.`,
        );
      const names = [spec.primaryDomain, spec.studentDomain, spec.aliasDomain];
      for (const name of names)
        if (registry.domainForName(name))
          throw new DomainError(
            409,
            `The domain name ${name} is in use. Pass a different name.`,
          );
      removeFiles(spec.customerId);
      const db = openDomainDb(fileFor(spec.customerId));
      let result: SeedResult;
      try {
        result = seedDomain(db, spec, now);
      } catch (error) {
        db.close();
        removeFiles(spec.customerId);
        throw error;
      }
      const record: StoredDomain = {
        customerId: spec.customerId,
        primaryDomain: spec.primaryDomain,
        spec: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(input)) as SeedSpec,
        settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
        seededAt: now,
        createdAt: now,
      };
      registry.addDomain(record, names);
      const domain: OpenDomain = { customerId: spec.customerId, record, spec, db };
      open.set(spec.customerId, domain);
      return { domain, result };
    },
    reset(customerId, now) {
      const record = registry.getDomain(customerId);
      if (!record) throw missing(customerId);
      close(customerId);
      removeFiles(customerId);
      const spec = resolveSpec(record.spec);
      const db = openDomainDb(fileFor(customerId));
      const result = seedDomain(db, spec, now);
      registry.setSeededAt(customerId, now);
      open.set(customerId, {
        customerId,
        record: { ...record, seededAt: now },
        spec,
        db,
      });
      return result;
    },
    delete(customerId) {
      if (!registry.getDomain(customerId)) throw missing(customerId);
      close(customerId);
      removeFiles(customerId);
      registry.deleteDomain(customerId);
    },
    list: () => registry.listDomains(),
    updateSettings(customerId, patch) {
      const domain = domains.get(customerId);
      if (!domain) throw missing(customerId);
      let settings: DomainSettings;
      try {
        settings = mergeSettings(domain.record.settings, patch);
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof SettingsError)
          throw new DomainError(400, error.message);
        throw error;
      }
      registry.setSettings(customerId, settings);
      domain.record = { ...domain.record, settings };
      return settings;
    },
    closeAll() {
      for (const customerId of [...open.keys()]) close(customerId);
    },
  };
  return domains;
}
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { createDomains, DomainError } from './domains.ts';
import { openRegistry } from './store/registry.ts';
import { row } from './store/sqlite.ts';

const SPEC = {
  tier: 'small',
  seed: 'life',
  counts: { schools: 2, students: 60, staff: 10, devices: 80 },
};

function setup() {
  const dataDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'domains-'));
  const registry = openRegistry(join(dataDir, 'registry.sqlite'));
  return { dataDir, registry, domains: createDomains(dataDir, registry) };
}

const status = (work: () => unknown) => {
  try {
    work();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof DomainError) return error.status;
    throw error;
  }
  return 0;
};

test('create seeds a file and registers the domain names', () => {
  const { dataDir, registry, domains } = setup();
  const { domain, result } = domains.create(SPEC, 1_000);
  assert.equal(result.counts.devices, 80);
  assert.ok(existsSync(join(dataDir, 'domains', `${domain.customerId}.sqlite`)));
  assert.equal(registry.domainForName(domain.spec.primaryDomain), domain.customerId);
  assert.equal(registry.domainForName(domain.spec.aliasDomain), domain.customerId);
  assert.equal(domains.get(domain.customerId), domain);
});

test('duplicate seeds, duplicate names, and bad specs fail with a status', () => {
  const { domains } = setup();
  domains.create({ ...SPEC, name: 'Same District' }, 1_000);
  assert.equal(status(() => domains.create({ ...SPEC, name: 'Other' }, 1_000)), 409);
  assert.equal(
    status(() => domains.create({ ...SPEC, seed: 'two', name: 'Same District' }, 1_000)),
    409,
  );
  assert.equal(status(() => domains.create({ tier: 'huge', seed: 'x' }, 1_000)), 400);
});

test('reset reseeds from the stored specification', () => {
  const { registry, domains } = setup();
  const { domain } = domains.create(SPEC, 1_000);
  const result = domains.reset(domain.customerId, 5_000);
  assert.equal(result.counts.devices, 80);
  assert.equal(registry.getDomain(domain.customerId)?.seededAt, 5_000);
  const reopened = domains.get(domain.customerId)!;
  assert.equal(reopened.record.seededAt, 5_000);
  assert.equal(
    row<{ value: string }>(
      reopened.db.prepare("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key = 'seededAt'").get(),
    )?.value,
    '5000',
  );
});

test('delete removes the file and the registry entry', () => {
  const { dataDir, domains } = setup();
  const { domain } = domains.create(SPEC, 1_000);
  domains.delete(domain.customerId);
  assert.equal(domains.get(domain.customerId), undefined);
  assert.ok(!existsSync(join(dataDir, 'domains', `${domain.customerId}.sqlite`)));
  assert.equal(status(() => domains.delete(domain.customerId)), 404);
});

test('settings merge, persist, and reject bad values', () => {
  const { registry, domains } = setup();
  const { domain } = domains.create(SPEC, 1_000);
  domains.updateSettings(domain.customerId, { quota: { perMinute: 10 } });
  assert.equal(domains.get(domain.customerId)?.record.settings.quota.perMinute, 10);
  assert.equal(registry.getDomain(domain.customerId)?.settings.quota.perMinute, 10);
  assert.equal(
    status(() => domains.updateSettings(domain.customerId, { quota: { perMinute: -1 } })),
    400,
  );
  assert.equal(status(() => domains.updateSettings('C0missing', {})), 404);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the mock**

`src/mock.ts`:

```ts
import { once } from 'node:events';
import { mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from 'node:http';
import https from 'node:https';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { ensureCertificates, type Certificates } from './ca.ts';
import { systemClock, type Clock, type Timing } from './clock.ts';
import { createDomains, type Domains } from './domains.ts';
import {
  ApiError,
  directoryError,
  internalError,
  notFoundPage,
  transientError,
} from './http/errors.ts';
import { matchRoute } from './http/router.ts';
import {
  NO_OVERRIDES,
  type Access,
  type ApiRequest,
  type ApiResponse,
  type Authenticator,
  type Call,
  type OpenDomain,
  type Overrides,
  type Route,
} from './http/types.ts';
import { openPipeline, type Pipeline } from './pipeline.ts';
import { createRandom, type Random } from './random.ts';
import { buildRoutes } from './routes.ts';
import { openRegistry, type Registry } from './store/registry.ts';

export interface MockOptions {
  dataDir: string;
  clock?: Clock;
  timing?: Timing;
  random?: Random;
  /** Replace the Google routes. Tests use this. */
  routes?: readonly Route[];
}

export interface Mock {
  readonly dataDir: string;
  readonly registry: Registry;
  readonly domains: Domains;
  readonly certificates: Certificates;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly timing: Timing;
  readonly random: Random;
  readonly routes: Route[];
  pipeline: Pipeline;
  overrides: Overrides;
  authenticator: Authenticator;
  dispatch(request: ApiRequest, batchPart?: boolean): Promise<ApiResponse | 'reset'>;
  handleGoogle(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): void;
  listenGoogle(port: number, host?: string): Promise<https.Server>;
  close(): void;
}

export const BODY_LIMIT = 32 * 1024 * 1024;

/** Serialize the body. Later tasks add the `fields` selector here. */
export function finalize(
  response: ApiResponse,
  request: ApiRequest,
  _route: Route | null,
): ApiResponse {
  if (response.raw !== undefined) return response;
  if (response.body === undefined) return { ...response, raw: '' };
  const pretty = request.query.get('prettyPrint') !== 'false';
  return {
    ...response,
    raw: JSON.stringify(response.body, null, pretty ? 2 : undefined),
  };
}

export function createMock(options: MockOptions): Mock {
  mkdirSync(options.dataDir, { recursive: true });
  const registry = openRegistry(join(options.dataDir, 'registry.sqlite'));
  const certificates = ensureCertificates(join(options.dataDir, 'ca'));
  const domains = createDomains(options.dataDir, registry);
  const servers = new Set<https.Server>();

  const mock: Mock = {
    dataDir: options.dataDir,
    registry,
    domains,
    certificates,
    clock: options.clock ?? systemClock,
    timing: options.timing ?? { scale: 1, delays: true },
    random: options.random ?? createRandom(`mock/${Date.now()}`),
    routes: [],
    pipeline: openPipeline(),
    overrides: NO_OVERRIDES,
    authenticator: {
      authenticate: () =>
        directoryError(401, 'authError', 'Invalid Credentials'),
    },

    async dispatch(request, batchPart = false) {
      const match = matchRoute(mock.routes, request);
      if (!match) return finalize(notFoundPage(request.path), request, null);
      const { route, params } = match;
      let access: Access | null = null;
      let domain: OpenDomain | null = null;
      if (route.api === 'directory' || route.api === 'chrome') {
        const result = mock.authenticator.authenticate(request, route);
        if (!('access' in result)) return finalize(result, request, route);
        access = result.access;
        domain = result.domain;
      }
      const call: Call = {
        route,
        params,
        request,
        access,
        domain,
        batchPart,
        overrides: mock.overrides,
        dispatchPart: async (part) => {
          const answer = await mock.dispatch(part, true);
          return answer === 'reset'
            ? finalize(transientError('directory'), part, null)
            : answer;
        },
      };
      const fault = await mock.pipeline.before(call);
      if (fault === 'reset')
        return batchPart
          ? finalize(transientError(route.api), request, route)
          : 'reset';
      if (fault) return finalize(fault, request, route);
      const admission = mock.pipeline.admit(call);
      if (!admission.ok) return finalize(admission.response, request, route);
      try {
        let response: ApiResponse;
        try {
          response = await route.handler(call);
        } catch (error) {
          if (error instanceof ApiError) response = error.response;
          else {
            console.error(error);
            response = internalError(route.api);
          }
        }
        response = finalize(response, request, route);
        await mock.pipeline.delay(call, response);
        return response;
      } finally {
        admission.release();
      }
    },

    handleGoogle(incoming, outgoing) {
      void (async () => {
        const host = (incoming.headers.host ?? '')
          .replace(/:\d+$/, '')
          .toLowerCase();
        const url = new URL(incoming.url ?? '/', 'https://mock.invalid');
        const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
        let size = 0;
        for await (const chunk of incoming as AsyncIterable<Buffer>) {
          size += chunk.length;
          if (size > BODY_LIMIT) {
            outgoing.writeHead(413).end();
            return;
          }
          chunks.push(chunk);
        }
        const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
        for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(incoming.headers))
          if (value !== undefined)
            headers[key] = Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(', ') : value;
        const result = await mock.dispatch({
          method: incoming.method ?? 'GET',
          host,
          path: url.pathname,
          query: url.searchParams,
          headers,
          body: Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8'),
        });
        if (result === 'reset') {
          incoming.socket.destroy();
          return;
        }
        outgoing.writeHead(result.status, {
          'content-type': 'application/json; charset=UTF-8',
          ...result.headers,
        });
        outgoing.end(result.raw ?? '');
      })().catch((error: unknown) => {
        console.error(error);
        if (!outgoing.headersSent) outgoing.writeHead(500);
        outgoing.end();
      });
    },

    async listenGoogle(port, host = '0.0.0.0') {
      const server = https.createServer(
        { cert: certificates.serverCert, key: certificates.serverKey },
        (request, response) => mock.handleGoogle(request, response),
      );
      servers.add(server);
      server.listen(port, host);
      await once(server, 'listening');
      return server;
    },

    close() {
      for (const server of servers) {
        server.close();
        server.closeAllConnections();
      }
      domains.closeAll();
      registry.close();
    },
  };
  mock.routes.push(...(options.routes ?? buildRoutes(mock)));
  return mock;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the test harness**

`src/testing/harness.ts`:

```ts
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import type { IncomingHttpHeaders } from 'node:http';
import https from 'node:https';
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { createMock, type Mock, type MockOptions } from '../mock.ts';

export interface HarnessResponse {
  status: number;
  headers: IncomingHttpHeaders;
  text: string;
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
  json(): any;
}

export interface RequestOptions {
  host: string;
  path: string;
  method?: string;
  query?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | undefined>;
  token?: string;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  body?: string;
}

export interface Harness {
  mock: Mock;
  port: number;
  dataDir: string;
  request(options: RequestOptions): Promise<HarnessResponse>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

/** Start a mock on a random loopback port. Delays are off unless a test turns them on. */
export async function startHarness(
  options: Partial<MockOptions> = {},
): Promise<Harness> {
  const dataDir =
    options.dataDir ?? mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'admin-sdk-mock-'));
  const mock = createMock({
    timing: { scale: 1, delays: false },
    ...options,
    dataDir,
  });
  const server = await mock.listenGoogle(0, '127.0.0.1');
  const { port } = server.address() as AddressInfo;
  return {
    mock,
    port,
    dataDir,
    request({ host, path, method = 'GET', query, token, headers = {}, body }) {
      const search = new URLSearchParams();
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(query ?? {}))
        if (value !== undefined) search.set(key, String(value));
      const target = search.size > 0 ? `${path}?${search}` : path;
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const outgoing = https.request(
          {
            host: '127.0.0.1',
            port,
            path: target,
            method,
            servername: host,
            ca: mock.certificates.caCert,
            agent: false,
            headers: {
              host,
              ...(token ? { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {}),
              ...(body !== undefined
                ? { 'content-length': String(Buffer.byteLength(body)) }
                : {}),
              ...headers,
            },
          },
          (incoming) => {
            let text = '';
            incoming.setEncoding('utf8');
            incoming.on('data', (chunk: string) => (text += chunk));
            incoming.on('end', () =>
              resolve({
                status: incoming.statusCode ?? 0,
                headers: incoming.headers,
                text,
                json: () => JSON.parse(text),
              }),
            );
          },
        );
        outgoing.on('error', reject);
        if (body !== undefined) outgoing.write(body);
        outgoing.end();
      });
    },
    async close() {
      mock.close();
      rmSync(dataDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { ApiError, directoryError } from './http/errors.ts';
import type { Route } from './http/types.ts';
import { openPipeline } from './pipeline.ts';
import { startHarness } from './testing/harness.ts';

const events: string[] = [];
const route = (overrides: Partial<Route>): Route => ({
  id: 'test.route',
  api: 'oauth',
  hosts: ['oauth2.googleapis.com'],
  methods: ['GET'],
  pattern: /^\/never$/,
  scopes: [],
  admin: false,
  handler: () => ({ status: 200, body: {} }),
  ...overrides,
});
const routes: Route[] = [
  route({
    id: 'test.echo',
    pattern: /^\/echo\/(?<name>[^/]+)$/,
    handler: (call) => {
      events.push('handler');
      return {
        status: 200,
        body: { name: call.params.name, q: call.request.query.get('q') },
      };
    },
  }),
  route({
    id: 'test.protected',
    api: 'directory',
    hosts: ['admin.googleapis.com'],
    pattern: /^\/protected$/,
  }),
  route({
    id: 'test.conflict',
    pattern: /^\/conflict$/,
    handler: () => {
      throw new ApiError(directoryError(409, 'duplicate', 'Entity already exists.'));
    },
  }),
  route({
    id: 'test.crash',
    pattern: /^\/crash$/,
    handler: () => {
      throw new Error('boom');
    },
  }),
];

test('the mock routes requests and shapes responses', async (t) => {
  const harness = await startHarness({ routes });
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const oauth = 'oauth2.googleapis.com';

  const missing = await harness.request({ host: oauth, path: '/nothing' });
  assert.equal(missing.status, 404);
  assert.match(missing.text, /Error 404 \(Not Found\)/);
  assert.match(String(missing.headers['content-type']), /text\/html/);

  const echo = await harness.request({
    host: oauth,
    path: '/echo/a%20b',
    query: { q: '1' },
  });
  assert.equal(echo.status, 200);
  assert.deepEqual(echo.json(), { name: 'a b', q: '1' });
  assert.match(echo.text, /\n {2}"name"/, 'Bodies are pretty-printed by default.');
  const compact = await harness.request({
    host: oauth,
    path: '/echo/x',
    query: { prettyPrint: 'false' },
  });
  assert.equal(compact.text, '{"name":"x","q":null}');

  const wrongHost = await harness.request({ host: 'www.googleapis.com', path: '/echo/x' });
  assert.equal(wrongHost.status, 404);

  const unauthenticated = await harness.request({
    host: 'admin.googleapis.com',
    path: '/protected',
  });
  assert.equal(unauthenticated.status, 401);

  const conflict = await harness.request({ host: oauth, path: '/conflict' });
  assert.equal(conflict.status, 409);
  assert.equal(conflict.json().error.errors[0].reason, 'duplicate');

  const crash = await harness.request({ host: oauth, path: '/crash' });
  assert.equal(crash.status, 500);
  assert.equal(crash.json().error.errors[0].reason, 'backendError');
});

test('the pipeline runs before, admit, handler, delay, and release in order', async (t) => {
  const harness = await startHarness({ routes });
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  events.length = 0;
  harness.mock.pipeline = {
    before: async () => {
      events.push('before');
      return undefined;
    },
    admit: () => {
      events.push('admit');
      return { ok: true, release: () => events.push('release') };
    },
    delay: async () => {
      events.push('delay');
    },
  };
  await harness.request({ host: 'oauth2.googleapis.com', path: '/echo/x' });
  assert.deepEqual(events, ['before', 'admit', 'handler', 'delay', 'release']);
});

test('a rejected admission skips the handler', async (t) => {
  const harness = await startHarness({ routes });
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  events.length = 0;
  harness.mock.pipeline = {
    ...openPipeline(),
    admit: () => ({
      ok: false,
      response: directoryError(429, 'rateLimitExceeded', 'Rate Limit Exceeded'),
    }),
  };
  const response = await harness.request({
    host: 'oauth2.googleapis.com',
    path: '/echo/x',
  });
  assert.equal(response.status, 429);
  assert.deepEqual(events, []);
});

test('a reset fault closes the connection without a response', async (t) => {
  const harness = await startHarness({ routes });
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  harness.mock.pipeline = { ...openPipeline(), before: async () => 'reset' };
  await assert.rejects(
    harness.request({ host: 'oauth2.googleapis.com', path: '/echo/x' }),
    /socket hang up|ECONNRESET/,
  );
});
```

```ts
import type { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { row } from './sqlite.ts';

/** One row of the `users` table. */
export interface UserRow {
  id: string;
  primary_email: string;
  given_name: string;
  family_name: string;
  full_name: string;
  org_unit_path: string;
  is_admin: number;
  suspended: number;
  suspension_reason: string | null;
  external_id: string | null;
  role: string;
  school: number | null;
  creation_time: number;
  last_login_time: number | null;
  ord: number;
}

/** Find a user by primary email, alias, or user ID. */
export function findUser(db: DatabaseSync, key: string): UserRow | undefined {
  const lower = key.toLowerCase();
  return (
    row<UserRow>(
      db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE primary_email = ?').get(lower),
    ) ??
    row<UserRow>(
      db
        .prepare(
          'SELECT u.* FROM user_aliases a JOIN users u ON u.id = a.user_id WHERE a.alias = ?',
        )
        .get(lower),
    ) ??
    row<UserRow>(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?').get(key))
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Append the authentication error builders**

Append to `src/http/errors.ts`:

```ts
const SIGN_IN_HELP =
  'Expected OAuth 2 access token, login cookie or other valid authentication credential. See https://developers.google.com/identity/sign-in/web/devconsole-project.';

/** 401 for a missing or unusable bearer token. */
export function unauthenticated(api: Api, kind: 'missing' | 'invalid'): ApiResponse {
  const message =
    kind === 'missing'
      ? `Request is missing required authentication credential. ${SIGN_IN_HELP}`
      : `Request had invalid authentication credentials. ${SIGN_IN_HELP}`;
  if (api === 'chrome') return rpcError(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', message);
  return directoryError(401, kind === 'missing' ? 'required' : 'authError', message, {
    itemMessage: kind === 'missing' ? 'Login Required.' : 'Invalid Credentials',
    item: { location: 'Authorization', locationType: 'header' },
    extra: { status: 'UNAUTHENTICATED' },
  });
}

/** 403 for a token without any accepted scope. */
export function insufficientScopes(api: Api): ApiResponse {
  const message = 'Request had insufficient authentication scopes.';
  const details = [
    {
      '@type': 'type.googleapis.com/google.rpc.ErrorInfo',
      reason: 'ACCESS_TOKEN_SCOPE_INSUFFICIENT',
      domain: 'googleapis.com',
      metadata: {
        service:
          api === 'chrome' ? 'chromemanagement.googleapis.com' : 'admin.googleapis.com',
      },
    },
  ];
  if (api === 'chrome') return rpcError(403, 'PERMISSION_DENIED', message, details);
  return directoryError(403, 'insufficientPermissions', message, {
    itemMessage: 'Insufficient Permission',
    extra: { status: 'PERMISSION_DENIED', details },
  });
}

/** 403 for a subject without administrator rights or another customer's data. */
export function notAuthorized(api: Api): ApiResponse {
  return api === 'chrome'
    ? rpcError(403, 'PERMISSION_DENIED', 'The caller does not have permission')
    : directoryError(403, 'forbidden', 'Not Authorized to access this resource/api');
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the service account issuer**

`src/auth/accounts.ts`:

```ts
import { generateKeyPairSync, randomBytes, randomInt } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Registry } from '../store/registry.ts';
import { TOKEN_URI } from './scopes.ts';

/** The JSON key file format that Google issues for a service account. */
export interface ServiceAccountFile {
  type: 'service_account';
  project_id: string;
  private_key_id: string;
  private_key: string;
  client_email: string;
  client_id: string;
  auth_uri: string;
  token_uri: string;
  auth_provider_x509_cert_url: string;
  client_x509_cert_url: string;
  universe_domain: 'googleapis.com';
}

export class AccountError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'AccountError';
  }
}

const PROJECT_PATTERN = /^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{4,28}[a-z0-9]$/;

/** Create a key pair, store its public key, and return the key file. */
export function issueServiceAccount(
  registry: Registry,
  customerId: string,
  projectId?: string,
): ServiceAccountFile {
  const project = projectId ?? `admin-sdk-mock-${randomInt(100_000, 1_000_000)}`;
  if (!PROJECT_PATTERN.test(project))
    throw new AccountError(
      'projectId must be 6 to 30 lower-case letters, digits, or hyphens, and start with a letter.',
    );
  const { privateKey, publicKey } = generateKeyPairSync('rsa', {
    modulusLength: 2048,
    privateKeyEncoding: { type: 'pkcs8', format: 'pem' },
    publicKeyEncoding: { type: 'spki', format: 'pem' },
  });
  const clientId = `1${Array.from({ length: 20 }, () => randomInt(0, 10)).join('')}`;
  const clientEmail = `mock-${randomBytes(3).toString('hex')}@${project}.iam.gserviceaccount.com`;
  const privateKeyId = randomBytes(20).toString('hex');
  registry.addServiceAccount({
    clientId,
    clientEmail,
    projectId: project,
    privateKeyId,
    publicKey,
    customerId,
  });
  return {
    type: 'service_account',
    project_id: project,
    private_key_id: privateKeyId,
    private_key: privateKey,
    client_email: clientEmail,
    client_id: clientId,
    auth_uri: 'https://accounts.google.com/o/oauth2/auth',
    token_uri: TOKEN_URI,
    auth_provider_x509_cert_url: 'https://www.googleapis.com/oauth2/v1/certs',
    client_x509_cert_url: `https://www.googleapis.com/robot/v1/metadata/x509/${encodeURIComponent(clientEmail)}`,
    universe_domain: 'googleapis.com',
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the token endpoints**

`src/auth/token.ts`:

```ts
import { randomBytes, verify } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Clock } from '../clock.ts';
import type { Domains } from '../domains.ts';
import type { ApiResponse, Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import type { Registry } from '../store/registry.ts';
import { findUser } from '../store/user-lookup.ts';
import { TOKEN_URI } from './scopes.ts';

const JWT_BEARER = 'urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer';
const LIFETIME_SECONDS = 3599;
const TIMEFRAME =
  'Invalid JWT: Token must be a short-lived token (60 minutes) and in a reasonable timeframe. Check your iat and exp values in the JWT claim.';

const oauthError = (
  status: number,
  error: string,
  description: string,
): ApiResponse => ({
  status,
  body: { error, error_description: description },
});

const decode = (part: string): Record<string, unknown> =>
  JSON.parse(Buffer.from(part, 'base64url').toString('utf8')) as Record<
    string,
    unknown
  >;

/** The JWT bearer grant with domain-wide delegation. */
export function handleToken(
  deps: { registry: Registry; domains: Domains; clock: Clock },
  call: Call,
): ApiResponse {
  const form = new URLSearchParams(call.request.body);
  const grantType = form.get('grant_type');
  if (grantType !== JWT_BEARER)
    return oauthError(
      400,
      'unsupported_grant_type',
      `Invalid grant_type: ${grantType ?? ''}`,
    );
  const parts = (form.get('assertion') ?? '').split('.');
  if (parts.length !== 3) return oauthError(400, 'invalid_grant', 'Bad Request');
  let header: Record<string, unknown>;
  let claims: Record<string, unknown>;
  try {
    header = decode(parts[0]);
    claims = decode(parts[1]);
  } catch {
    return oauthError(400, 'invalid_grant', 'Bad Request');
  }
  const account = deps.registry.serviceAccountByEmail(String(claims.iss ?? ''));
  if (!account)
    return oauthError(400, 'invalid_grant', 'Invalid grant: account not found');
  const signed =
    header.alg === 'RS256' &&
    verify(
      'sha256',
      Buffer.from(`${parts[0]}.${parts[1]}`),
      account.publicKey,
      Buffer.from(parts[2], 'base64url'),
    );
  if (!signed) return oauthError(400, 'invalid_grant', 'Invalid JWT Signature.');
  if (claims.aud !== TOKEN_URI)
    return oauthError(400, 'invalid_grant', 'Invalid JWT: Failed audience check.');
  const now = deps.clock.now() / 1000;
  const { iat, exp } = claims;
  if (
    typeof iat !== 'number' ||
    typeof exp !== 'number' ||
    exp - iat > 3600 ||
    exp < now ||
    iat > now + 300
  )
    return oauthError(400, 'invalid_grant', TIMEFRAME);
  const subject = typeof claims.sub === 'string' ? claims.sub.toLowerCase() : '';
  if (!subject)
    return oauthError(
      400,
      'invalid_request',
      'The mock requires a delegated subject in the sub claim.',
    );
  const customerId = deps.registry.domainForName(subject.split('@')[1] ?? '');
  const domain = customerId ? deps.domains.get(customerId) : undefined;
  if (!customerId || !domain || !findUser(domain.db, subject))
    return oauthError(400, 'invalid_grant', 'Invalid email or User ID');
  const requested = String(claims.scope ?? '')
    .split(' ')
    .filter(Boolean);
  if (requested.length === 0)
    return oauthError(400, 'invalid_scope', 'Empty or missing scope not allowed.');
  const granted = call.overrides.grant(
    customerId,
    account.clientId,
    deps.registry.grant(customerId, account.clientId),
  );
  if (!granted || requested.some((scope) => !granted.includes(scope)))
    return oauthError(
      400,
      'unauthorized_client',
      'Client is unauthorized to retrieve access tokens using this method, or client not authorized for any of the scopes requested.',
    );
  const token = `ya29.mock.${randomBytes(32).toString('base64url')}`;
  deps.registry.saveToken({
    token,
    clientId: account.clientId,
    projectId: account.projectId,
    subject,
    customerId,
    scopes: requested,
    expiresAt: deps.clock.now() + LIFETIME_SECONDS * 1000,
  });
  return {
    status: 200,
    body: { access_token: token, expires_in: LIFETIME_SECONDS, token_type: 'Bearer' },
  };
}
```

`src/auth/tokeninfo.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock } from '../clock.ts';
import type { ApiResponse, Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import type { Registry } from '../store/registry.ts';

/** Report the scopes and lifetime of an access token. */
export function handleTokenInfo(
  deps: { registry: Registry; clock: Clock },
  call: Call,
): ApiResponse {
  const { request } = call;
  const bearer = /^Bearer\s+(\S+)$/i.exec(request.headers.authorization ?? '')?.[1];
  const value =
    request.query.get('access_token') ??
    new URLSearchParams(request.body).get('access_token') ??
    bearer;
  const token = value ? deps.registry.token(value) : undefined;
  const now = deps.clock.now();
  if (!token || token.expiresAt <= now)
    return {
      status: 400,
      body: { error: 'invalid_token', error_description: 'Invalid Value' },
    };
  return {
    status: 200,
    body: {
      azp: token.clientId,
      aud: token.clientId,
      scope: token.scopes.join(' '),
      exp: String(Math.floor(token.expiresAt / 1000)),
      expires_in: String(Math.floor((token.expiresAt - now) / 1000)),
      access_type: 'online',
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the authenticator**

`src/auth/access.ts`:

```ts
import {
  insufficientScopes,
  notAuthorized,
  unauthenticated,
} from '../http/errors.ts';
import type { Authenticator } from '../http/types.ts';
import type { Mock } from '../mock.ts';
import { findUser } from '../store/user-lookup.ts';

/** Check the bearer token, its scopes, and the subject's administrator rights. */
export function createAuthenticator(mock: Mock): Authenticator {
  return {
    authenticate(request, route) {
      const value = /^Bearer\s+(\S+)$/i.exec(request.headers.authorization ?? '')?.[1];
      if (!value) return unauthenticated(route.api, 'missing');
      const token = mock.registry.token(value);
      if (!token || token.expiresAt <= mock.clock.now())
        return unauthenticated(route.api, 'invalid');
      const domain = mock.domains.get(token.customerId);
      if (!domain) return unauthenticated(route.api, 'invalid');
      if (!token.scopes.some((scope) => route.scopes.includes(scope)))
        return insufficientScopes(route.api);
      const user = findUser(domain.db, token.subject);
      const isAdmin = mock.overrides.isAdmin(
        token.customerId,
        token.subject,
        user?.is_admin === 1,
      );
      if (route.admin && !isAdmin) return notAuthorized(route.api);
      return {
        access: { token, customerId: token.customerId, subject: token.subject, isAdmin },
        domain,
      };
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import { handleToken } from './auth/token.ts';
import { handleTokenInfo } from './auth/tokeninfo.ts';
import type { Route } from './http/types.ts';
import type { Mock } from './mock.ts';

const OAUTH = ['oauth2.googleapis.com'];

/** Every Google route. Later tasks add their routes here. */
export function buildRoutes(mock: Mock): Route[] {
  return [
    {
      id: 'oauth.token',
      api: 'oauth',
      hosts: OAUTH,
      methods: ['POST'],
      pattern: /^\/token$/,
      scopes: [],
      admin: false,
      handler: (call) => handleToken(mock, call),
    },
    {
      id: 'oauth.tokeninfo',
      api: 'oauth',
      hosts: OAUTH,
      methods: ['GET', 'POST'],
      pattern: /^\/tokeninfo$/,
      scopes: [],
      admin: false,
      handler: (call) => handleTokenInfo(mock, call),
    },
  ];
}
```

`src/testing/jwt.ts`:

```ts
import { sign } from 'node:crypto';

/** Sign a JWT bearer assertion the way google-auth-library does. */
export function signAssertion(
  account: { client_email: string; private_key: string; private_key_id: string },
  claims: Record<string, unknown>,
  now = Date.now(),
): string {
  const header = { alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT', kid: account.private_key_id };
  const seconds = Math.floor(now / 1000);
  const body = {
    iss: account.client_email,
    aud: 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',
    iat: seconds,
    exp: seconds + 3600,
    ...claims,
  };
  const encode = (value: unknown) =>
    Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value)).toString('base64url');
  const unsigned = `${encode(header)}.${encode(body)}`;
  return `${unsigned}.${sign('sha256', Buffer.from(unsigned), account.private_key).toString('base64url')}`;
}

export function tokenRequestBody(assertion: string): string {
  return new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: 'urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer',
    assertion,
  }).toString();
}
```

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { OpenDomain } from '../http/types.ts';

export const TEST_SPEC = {
  tier: 'small',
  seed: 'harness',
  counts: { schools: 2, students: 120, staff: 20, devices: 150 },
};

/** Seed a small domain for HTTP tests. */
export function seedTestDomain(
  harness: Harness,
  input: Record<string, unknown> = TEST_SPEC,
): OpenDomain {
  return harness.mock.domains.create(input, harness.mock.clock.now()).domain;
}

export const adminOf = (domain: OpenDomain): string =>
  `admin@${domain.spec.primaryDomain}`;

/** Store an access token directly, without the token endpoint. */
export function grantToken(
  harness: Harness,
  domain: OpenDomain,
  scopes: readonly string[],
  subject = adminOf(domain),
): string {
  const token = `ya29.test.${randomUUID()}`;
  harness.mock.registry.saveToken({
    token,
    clientId: '100000000000000000001',
    projectId: 'harness-project',
    subject,
    customerId: domain.customerId,
    scopes: [...scopes],
    expiresAt: harness.mock.clock.now() + 3_599_000,
  });
  return token;
}
```

```ts
function registry() {

  value.addDomain(
    {
      customerId: 'C0abc1234',
      primaryDomain: 'district.test',
      spec: { tier: 'small', seed: 'x' },

test('an issued file passes the Campus Commander credential rules', () => {
  const file = issueServiceAccount(registry(), 'C0abc1234');
  assert.equal(file.type, 'service_account');
  assert.match(file.client_id, /^\d{1,32}$/);

  assert.equal(file.token_uri, 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token');
  assert.equal(file.universe_domain, 'googleapis.com');
  assert.ok(file.private_key_id.length > 0);

test('the registry keeps the public key and never the private key', () => {
  const store = registry();

  assert.equal(stored?.projectId, 'district-project');
  assert.match(stored!.publicKey, /BEGIN PUBLIC KEY/);
  assert.doesNotMatch(JSON.stringify(stored), /PRIVATE KEY/);
});

test('a malformed project ID fails', () => {
  assert.throws(() => issueServiceAccount(registry(), 'C0abc1234', 'Bad_Project'), AccountError);
});
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { generateKeyPairSync } from 'node:crypto';
import test from 'node:test';
import { adminOf, seedTestDomain, startHarness, type Harness } from '../testing/harness.ts';
import { signAssertion, tokenRequestBody } from '../testing/jwt.ts';
import { issueServiceAccount, type ServiceAccountFile } from './accounts.ts';
import { SCOPES } from './scopes.ts';

const ORGUNIT = SCOPES.orgunit[1];
const DEVICE = SCOPES.device[1];

async function setup(t: import('node:test').TestContext) {
  const harness = await startHarness();
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness);
  const account = issueServiceAccount(harness.mock.registry, domain.customerId);
  harness.mock.registry.setGrant(domain.customerId, account.client_id, [ORGUNIT]);
  return { harness, domain, account };
}

const exchange = (harness: Harness, assertion: string) =>
  harness.request({
    host: 'oauth2.googleapis.com',
    path: '/token',
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
    body: tokenRequestBody(assertion),
  });

test('a granted scope yields a token that tokeninfo describes exactly', async (t) => {
  const { harness, domain, account } = await setup(t);
  const response = await exchange(
    harness,
    signAssertion(account, { scope: ORGUNIT, sub: adminOf(domain) }),
  );
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  const body = response.json();
  assert.equal(body.token_type, 'Bearer');
  assert.equal(body.expires_in, 3599);
  assert.match(body.access_token, /^ya29\./);
  const info = await harness.request({
    host: 'oauth2.googleapis.com',
    path: '/tokeninfo',
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${body.access_token}` },
  });
  assert.equal(info.status, 200);
  assert.equal(info.json().scope, ORGUNIT);
  assert.equal(info.json().aud, account.client_id);
});

test('an ungranted scope fails with unauthorized_client', async (t) => {

  const response = await exchange(
    harness,
    signAssertion(account, { scope: `${ORGUNIT} ${DEVICE}`, sub: adminOf(domain) }),
  );
  assert.equal(response.status, 400);
  assert.equal(response.json().error, 'unauthorized_client');
});

test('bad assertions fail with invalid_grant', async (t) => {
  const { harness, domain, account } = await setup(t);
  const other = generateKeyPairSync('rsa', {
    modulusLength: 2048,
    privateKeyEncoding: { type: 'pkcs8', format: 'pem' },
    publicKeyEncoding: { type: 'spki', format: 'pem' },
  });
  const forged: ServiceAccountFile = { ...account, private_key: other.privateKey };
  const now = Date.now();
  const cases: [string, string][] = [
    ['wrong key', signAssertion(forged, { scope: ORGUNIT, sub: adminOf(domain) })],
    ['unknown issuer', signAssertion({ ...account, client_email: 'x@y.iam.gserviceaccount.com' }, { scope: ORGUNIT, sub: adminOf(domain) })],
    ['wrong audience', signAssertion(account, { scope: ORGUNIT, sub: adminOf(domain), aud: 'https://example.test' })],
    ['expired', signAssertion(account, { scope: ORGUNIT, sub: adminOf(domain) }, now - 7_200_000)],
    ['too long', signAssertion(account, { scope: ORGUNIT, sub: adminOf(domain), exp: Math.floor(now / 1000) + 7200 })],
    ['unknown subject', signAssertion(account, { scope: ORGUNIT, sub: `nobody@${domain.spec.primaryDomain}` })],
    ['garbage', 'not.a.jwt'],
  ];
  for (const [label, assertion] of cases) {
    const response = await exchange(harness, assertion);
    assert.equal(response.status, 400, label);
    assert.equal(response.json().error, 'invalid_grant', label);
  }
});

test('a wrong grant type and a missing scope fail with their own errors', async (t) => {
```

### Task 9: The `fields` selector and page tokens

**Files:**
- Create: `src/store/fields.ts`, `src/store/page-token.ts`
- Modify: `src/mock.ts` (`finalize`)
- Test: `src/store/fields.test.ts`, `src/store/page-token.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `FieldTree = Map<string, FieldTree | true>`, `FieldsError`, `parseFields(text: string): FieldTree`, `applyFields(value: unknown, tree: FieldTree): unknown`.
- Produces: `PageTokenError`, `fingerprint(path: string, query: URLSearchParams, seededAt: number): string`, `encodePageToken(fingerprint: string, cursor: readonly (string | number)[]): string`, `decodePageToken(token: string, fingerprint: string): (string | number)[]`.
- Changes: `finalize` applies `fields` to 2xx JSON bodies. A bad selector returns `400 invalidParameter` for Directory routes and `400 INVALID_ARGUMENT` for Chrome routes.

A page token stores the sort value and the ID of the last returned row. The fingerprint covers the path, every filtering parameter, and the domain's seeding time. A reset therefore invalidates every older token.

```ts
test('paging parameters do not change the fingerprint', () => {

    fingerprint('/x', params('query=a&maxResults=5&pageToken=t&fields=x&prettyPrint=false'), 1),
    fingerprint('/x', params('query=a'), 1),
  );

test('filters, paths, order, and the seeding time change the fingerprint', () => {
  const base = fingerprint('/x', params('query=a&orderBy=status'), 1);
  assert.notEqual(base, fingerprint('/x', params('query=b&orderBy=status'), 1));
  assert.notEqual(base, fingerprint('/y', params('query=a&orderBy=status'), 1));
  assert.notEqual(base, fingerprint('/x', params('query=a&orderBy=notes'), 1));
  assert.notEqual(base, fingerprint('/x', params('query=a&orderBy=status'), 2));
  assert.equal(base, fingerprint('/x', params('orderBy=status&query=a'), 1));
});

test('a token from another query or a garbled token fails', () => {

  const other = fingerprint('/x', params('query=b'), 1);
  assert.throws(() => decodePageToken(encodePageToken(other, ['b', 2]), fp), PageTokenError);
  for (const token of ['', 'garbage', Buffer.from('{"f":1}').toString('base64url')])
    assert.throws(() => decodePageToken(token, fp), PageTokenError);
```
