- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the selector**

`src/store/fields.ts`:

```ts
/** `true` selects the whole field. A map selects named subfields. */
export type FieldTree = Map<string, FieldTree | true>;

export class FieldsError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'FieldsError';
  }
}

const NAME = /[A-Za-z0-9_*]/;

function merge(target: FieldTree, source: FieldTree): void {
  for (const [key, value] of source) {
    const existing = target.get(key);
    if (existing === true) continue;
    if (value === true) target.set(key, true);
    else if (existing) merge(existing, value);
    else target.set(key, value);
  }
}

function insert(
  tree: FieldTree,
  segments: readonly string[],
  sub: FieldTree | undefined,
): void {
  let node = tree;
  for (let index = 0; index < segments.length; index++) {
    const key = segments[index];
    const last = index === segments.length - 1;
    const existing = node.get(key);
    if (existing === true) return;
    if (last && !sub) {
      node.set(key, true);
      return;
    }
    let child = existing;
    if (!child) {
      child = new Map();
      node.set(key, child);
    }
    if (last && sub) {
      merge(child, sub);
      return;
    }
    node = child;
  }
}

/** Parse Google's partial response syntax: `a,b/c,d(e,f)`. */
export function parseFields(text: string): FieldTree {
  const source = text.replace(/\s+/g, '');
  let index = 0;
  const fail = (): never => {
    throw new FieldsError(`Invalid field selection ${text}`);
  };
  const name = (): string => {
    const start = index;
    while (index < source.length && NAME.test(source[index])) index++;
    if (index === start) fail();
    return source.slice(start, index);
  };
  const list = (tree: FieldTree): void => {
    for (;;) {
      const segments = [name()];
      while (source[index] === '/') {
        index++;
        segments.push(name());
      }
      let sub: FieldTree | undefined;
      if (source[index] === '(') {
        index++;
        sub = new Map();
        list(sub);
        if (source[index] !== ')') fail();
        index++;
      }
      insert(tree, segments, sub);
      if (source[index] !== ',') return;
      index++;
    }
  };
  const tree: FieldTree = new Map();
  if (source === '') fail();
  list(tree);
  if (index !== source.length) fail();
  return tree;
}

/** Keep only the selected fields. Arrays apply the selection to each element. */
export function applyFields(value: unknown, tree: FieldTree): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => applyFields(item, tree));
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return value;
  const source = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  const wildcard = tree.get('*');
  if (wildcard)
    for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(source))
      out[key] = wildcard === true ? item : applyFields(item, wildcard);
  for (const [key, sub] of tree) {
    if (key === '*' || !Object.hasOwn(source, key)) continue;
    out[key] = sub === true ? source[key] : applyFields(source[key], sub);
  }
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement page tokens**

`src/store/page-token.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

export class PageTokenError extends Error {
  constructor() {
    super('Invalid page token.');
    this.name = 'PageTokenError';
  }
}

const IGNORED = new Set([
  'pageToken',
  'maxResults',
  'pageSize',
  'fields',
  'prettyPrint',
  'alt',
  'key',
  'quotaUser',
  'userIp',
  '$.xgafv',
  'access_token',
  'callback',
]);

/** A short hash of everything that selects or orders the rows. */
export function fingerprint(
  path: string,
  query: URLSearchParams,
  seededAt: number,
): string {
  const entries = [...query.entries()]
    .filter(([key]) => !IGNORED.has(key))
    .sort(([a, av], [b, bv]) =>
      a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : av < bv ? -1 : av > bv ? 1 : 0,
    );
  return createHash('sha256')
    .update(JSON.stringify([path, seededAt, entries]))
    .digest('base64url')
    .slice(0, 16);
}

export function encodePageToken(
  fp: string,
  cursor: readonly (string | number)[],
): string {
  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ f: fp, c: cursor })).toString('base64url');
}

export function decodePageToken(token: string, fp: string): (string | number)[] {
  let parsed: unknown;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(token, 'base64url').toString('utf8'));
  } catch {
    throw new PageTokenError();
  }
  if (parsed === null || typeof parsed !== 'object') throw new PageTokenError();
  const { f, c } = parsed as { f?: unknown; c?: unknown };
  if (
    f !== fp ||
    !Array.isArray(c) ||
    !c.every((value) => typeof value === 'string' || typeof value === 'number')
  )
    throw new PageTokenError();
  return c as (string | number)[];
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Apply the selector in `finalize`**

In `src/mock.ts`, add these imports:

```ts
import { rpcError } from './http/errors.ts';
import { applyFields, FieldsError, parseFields } from './store/fields.ts';
```

Merge `rpcError` into the existing `./http/errors.ts` import. Replace the whole `finalize` function:

```ts
/** Apply `fields` to a 2xx JSON body and serialize the body. */
export function finalize(
  response: ApiResponse,
  request: ApiRequest,
  route: Route | null,
): ApiResponse {
  if (response.raw !== undefined) return response;
  if (response.body === undefined) return { ...response, raw: '' };
  let body = response.body;
  const fields = request.query.get('fields');
  if (fields && response.status >= 200 && response.status < 300) {
    try {
      body = applyFields(body, parseFields(fields));
    } catch (error) {
      if (!(error instanceof FieldsError)) throw error;
      return finalize(
        route?.api === 'chrome'
          ? rpcError(400, 'INVALID_ARGUMENT', error.message)
          : directoryError(400, 'invalidParameter', error.message),
        request,
        route,
      );
    }
  }
  const pretty = request.query.get('prettyPrint') !== 'false';
  return {
    ...response,
    body,
    raw: JSON.stringify(body, null, pretty ? 2 : undefined),
  };
}
```

```ts
import {

  directoryError,
  notAuthorized,
  rpcError,
} from '../http/errors.ts';
import type { Call, OpenDomain } from '../http/types.ts';

export function etag(value: unknown): string {
  return `"${createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify(value)).digest('base64url').slice(0, 27)}"`;
}

export function withEtag<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(
  value: T,
): T & { etag: string } {
  return { ...value, etag: etag(value) };
}

export const iso = (ms: number | null | undefined): string | undefined =>
  ms === null || ms === undefined ? undefined : new Date(ms).toISOString();

export function domainOf(call: Call): OpenDomain {
  if (!call.domain || !call.access)
    throw new Error(`Route ${call.route.id} needs authentication.`);
  return call.domain;
}

/** Accept `my_customer` or the token's own customer ID. */
export function resolveCustomer(call: Call, value: string | undefined | null): string {
  const access = call.access;
  if (!access) throw new Error(`Route ${call.route.id} needs authentication.`);
  if (value === 'my_customer' || value === access.customerId)
    return access.customerId;
  throw new ApiError(notAuthorized(call.route.api));
}

export function invalidArgument(
  call: Call,
  message: string,
  reason = 'invalid',
): ApiError {
  return new ApiError(
    call.route.api === 'chrome'
      ? rpcError(400, 'INVALID_ARGUMENT', message)
      : directoryError(400, reason, message),
  );
}

export function intParam(
  call: Call,
  name: string,
  options: { fallback: number; min: number; max: number; clamp?: boolean },
): number {
  const raw = call.request.query.get(name);
  if (raw === null || raw === '') return options.fallback;
  const value = Number(raw);
  const message = `Invalid value '${raw}'. Values must be within the range: [${options.min}, ${options.max}]`;
  if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < options.min)
    throw invalidArgument(call, message);
  if (value > options.max && !options.clamp) throw invalidArgument(call, message);
  return Math.min(value, options.max);
}

export function boolParam(call: Call, name: string): boolean {
  const raw = call.request.query.get(name);
  if (raw === null || raw === '' || raw === 'false') return false;
  if (raw === 'true') return true;
  throw invalidArgument(call, `Invalid value '${raw}' for ${name}.`);
}

/** Match an enum parameter case-insensitively, ignoring underscores. */
export function enumParam<T extends string>(
  call: Call,
  name: string,
  values: Readonly<Record<string, T>>,
  fallback: T,
): T {
  const raw = call.request.query.get(name);
  if (raw === null || raw === '') return fallback;
  const found = values[raw.toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, '')];
  if (!found) throw invalidArgument(call, `Invalid value '${raw}' for ${name}.`);
  return found;
}
```

```ts
import type { ApiResponse, Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import { DAY_MS } from '../seed/context.ts';
import { domainOf, resolveCustomer, withEtag } from './common.ts';

export function getCustomer(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  const customerId = resolveCustomer(call, call.params.customerKey);
  const { spec, record } = domain;
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: 1,
    body: withEtag({
      kind: 'admin#directory#customer',
      id: customerId,
      customerDomain: spec.primaryDomain,
      alternateEmail: `it-admin@${spec.slug}-backup.test`,
      customerCreationTime: new Date(record.seededAt - 3000 * DAY_MS).toISOString(),
      language: 'en',
      phoneNumber: '+15555550100',
      postalAddress: {
        contactName: 'Technology Department',
        organizationName: spec.districtName,
        addressLine1: '100 Main Street',
        locality: spec.districtName.split(' ')[0],
        region: 'IL',
        postalCode: '60601',
        countryCode: 'US',
      },
    }),
  };
}
```

`src/directory/domains.ts`:

```ts
import type { ApiResponse, Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import { rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { domainOf, resolveCustomer, withEtag } from './common.ts';

interface DomainRow {
  domain_name: string;
  is_primary: number;
  verified: number;
  creation_time: number;
  alias_of: string | null;
}

export function listDomains(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  resolveCustomer(call, call.params.customer);
  const all = rows<DomainRow>(
    domain.db
      .prepare('SELECT * FROM domains ORDER BY is_primary DESC, creation_time')
      .all(),
  );
  const domains = all
    .filter((entry) => entry.alias_of === null)
    .map((entry) => {
      const aliases = all
        .filter((alias) => alias.alias_of === entry.domain_name)
        .map((alias) =>
          withEtag({
            kind: 'admin#directory#domainAlias',
            domainAliasName: alias.domain_name,
            parentDomainName: entry.domain_name,
            verified: alias.verified === 1,
            creationTime: String(alias.creation_time),
          }),
        );
      return withEtag({
        kind: 'admin#directory#domain',
        domainName: entry.domain_name,
        isPrimary: entry.is_primary === 1,
        verified: entry.verified === 1,
        creationTime: String(entry.creation_time),
        ...(aliases.length > 0 ? { domainAliases: aliases } : {}),
      });
    });
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: domains.length,
    body: withEtag({ kind: 'admin#directory#domains', domains }),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the OU handlers**

`src/directory/orgunits.ts`:

```ts
import type { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { ApiError, directoryError } from '../http/errors.ts';
import type { ApiResponse, Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import { row, rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { domainOf, enumParam, resolveCustomer, withEtag } from './common.ts';

export interface OuRow {
  org_unit_id: string;
  path: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  parent_id: string | null;
  parent_path: string | null;
  depth: number;
  kind: string;
  ord: number;
}

/** The root carries no parent fields. Campus Commander relies on that shape. */
export function orgUnitResource(unit: OuRow): Record<string, unknown> {
  return withEtag({
    kind: 'admin#directory#orgUnit',
    name: unit.name,
    ...(unit.description ? { description: unit.description } : {}),
    orgUnitPath: unit.path,
    orgUnitId: unit.org_unit_id,
    ...(unit.parent_id
      ? { parentOrgUnitPath: unit.parent_path, parentOrgUnitId: unit.parent_id }
      : {}),
    blockInheritance: false,
  });
}

/** Resolve a path without its leading slash, a path with it, or an `id:` value. */
export function resolveOrgUnit(
  _call: Call,
  db: DatabaseSync,
  raw: string | null | undefined,
): OuRow {
  const value = (raw ?? '').trim();
  const found =
    value === '' || value === '/'
      ? row<OuRow>(db.prepare("SELECT * FROM orgunits WHERE path = '/'").get())
      : value.startsWith('id:')
        ? row<OuRow>(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM orgunits WHERE org_unit_id = ?').get(value))
        : row<OuRow>(
            db
              .prepare('SELECT * FROM orgunits WHERE path = ?')
              .get(`/${value.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`),
          );
  if (!found)
    throw new ApiError(directoryError(404, 'notFound', 'Org unit not found'));
  return found;
}

const TYPES = {
  all: 'all',
  children: 'children',
  allincludingparent: 'allIncludingParent',
} as const;

export function listOrgUnits(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  resolveCustomer(call, call.params.customerId);
  const type = enumParam(call, 'type', TYPES, 'children');
  const parent = resolveOrgUnit(call, domain.db, call.request.query.get('orgUnitPath'));
  let units: OuRow[];
  if (type === 'children')
    units = rows<OuRow>(
      domain.db
        .prepare('SELECT * FROM orgunits WHERE parent_id = ? ORDER BY ord')
        .all(parent.org_unit_id),
    );
  else {
    const prefix = parent.path === '/' ? '/' : `${parent.path}/`;
    units = rows<OuRow>(
      domain.db
        .prepare(
          'SELECT * FROM orgunits WHERE substr(path, 1, ?) = ? AND path != ? ORDER BY ord',
        )
        .all(prefix.length, prefix, parent.path),
    );
    if (type === 'allIncludingParent') units = [parent, ...units];
  }
  const organizationUnits = units.map(orgUnitResource);
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: units.length,
    scanned: type === 'children' ? 0 : units.length,
    body: withEtag({
      kind: 'admin#directory#orgUnits',
      ...(organizationUnits.length > 0 ? { organizationUnits } : {}),
    }),
  };
}

export function getOrgUnit(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  resolveCustomer(call, call.params.customerId);
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: 1,
    body: orgUnitResource(resolveOrgUnit(call, domain.db, call.params.orgUnitPath)),
  };
}
```

```ts
const ADMIN = ['admin.googleapis.com'];

/** A Directory API read route on admin.googleapis.com. */
export function directoryRoute(
  id: string,
  pattern: RegExp,
  scopes: readonly string[],
  handler: Route['handler'],
  admin = true,
): Route {
  return { id, api: 'directory', hosts: ADMIN, methods: ['GET'], pattern, scopes, admin, handler };
}
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { READ_SCOPES, SCOPES } from '../auth/scopes.ts';
import { buildLayout } from '../seed/layout.ts';
import { row } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { grantToken, seedTestDomain, startHarness } from '../testing/harness.ts';

const SPEC = {
  tier: 'small',
  seed: 'directory',
  layout: 'role-first',
  counts: { schools: 2, students: 120, staff: 20, devices: 150 },
};
const BASE = '/admin/directory/v1';

async function setup(t: import('node:test').TestContext) {
  const harness = await startHarness();
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness, SPEC);
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const get = (path: string, query?: Record<string, string>, bearer = token) =>
    harness.request({ host: 'admin.googleapis.com', path: `${BASE}${path}`, query, token: bearer });
  return { harness, domain, token, get, layout: buildLayout(domain.spec) };
}

test('customers.get answers for my_customer and the customer ID only', async (t) => {
  const { domain, get } = await setup(t);
  const mine = await get('/customers/my_customer');
  assert.equal(mine.status, 200);
  assert.equal(mine.json().id, domain.customerId);
  assert.equal(mine.json().customerDomain, domain.spec.primaryDomain);
  assert.equal((await get(`/customers/${domain.customerId}`)).status, 200);
  const other = await get('/customers/C0zzzzzzz');
  assert.equal(other.status, 403);
  assert.equal(other.json().error.errors[0].reason, 'forbidden');
});

test('domains.list nests aliases under their parent', async (t) => {
  const { domain, get } = await setup(t);
  const body = (await get('/customer/my_customer/domains')).json();
  const primary = body.domains.find((entry: { isPrimary: boolean }) => entry.isPrimary);
  assert.equal(primary.domainName, domain.spec.primaryDomain);
  assert.deepEqual(
    primary.domainAliases.map((alias: { domainAliasName: string; parentDomainName: string }) => [
      alias.domainAliasName,
      alias.parentDomainName,
    ]),
    [[domain.spec.aliasDomain, domain.spec.primaryDomain]],
  );
});

test('orgunits.list returns children by default and whole trees on request', async (t) => {
  const { domain, get, layout } = await setup(t);
  const children = (await get('/customer/my_customer/orgunits')).json();
  assert.equal(children.organizationUnits.length, 3);
  const all = (await get('/customer/my_customer/orgunits', { type: 'all' })).json();
  assert.equal(all.organizationUnits.length, layout.units.length);
  const withParent = (
    await get(`/customer/${domain.customerId}/orgunits`, { type: 'all_including_parent' })
  ).json();
  assert.equal(withParent.organizationUnits.length, layout.units.length + 1);
  const root = withParent.organizationUnits[0];
  assert.equal(root.orgUnitPath, '/');
  assert.equal(root.parentOrgUnitId, undefined);
  assert.equal(root.parentOrgUnitPath, undefined);
  assert.ok(withParent.organizationUnits.slice(1).every((unit: { parentOrgUnitId?: string }) => unit.parentOrgUnitId));
});

test('orgunits.list scopes to an OU path with spaces', async (t) => {
  const { get, layout } = await setup(t);
  const school = layout.schools[0];
  assert.match(school.staffOu, / /, 'School names always contain a space.');
  const response = await get('/customer/my_customer/orgunits', {
    orgUnitPath: school.staffOu.slice(1),
    type: 'allIncludingParent',
  });
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.equal(response.json().organizationUnits[0].orgUnitPath, school.staffOu);
});

test('orgunits.get finds units by encoded path and by ID', async (t) => {
  const { domain, get, layout } = await setup(t);
  const unit = layout.units.find((entry) => entry.path.includes(' '))!;
  const byPath = await get(
    `/customer/my_customer/orgunits/${unit.path.slice(1).split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/')}`,
  );
  assert.equal(byPath.status, 200);
  assert.equal(byPath.json().orgUnitId, unit.orgUnitId);
  const byId = await get(`/customer/my_customer/orgunits/${encodeURIComponent(unit.orgUnitId)}`);
  assert.equal(byId.json().orgUnitPath, unit.path);
  const missing = await get('/customer/my_customer/orgunits/Nowhere');
  assert.equal(missing.status, 404);
  assert.equal(missing.json().error.errors[0].reason, 'notFound');
  assert.ok(domain);
});

test('a token without the OU scope fails with 403', async (t) => {
  const { harness, domain, get } = await setup(t);
  const customerOnly = grantToken(harness, domain, [SCOPES.customer[1]]);
  const response = await get('/customer/my_customer/orgunits', undefined, customerOnly);
  assert.equal(response.status, 403);
  assert.equal(response.json().error.errors[0].reason, 'insufficientPermissions');
});

test('fields trims an OU list', async (t) => {
  const { get } = await setup(t);
  const body = (
    await get('/customer/my_customer/orgunits', { fields: 'organizationUnits(orgUnitPath)' })
  ).json();
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(body), ['organizationUnits']);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(body.organizationUnits[0]), ['orgUnitPath']);
});

test('the root row exists in the database', async (t) => {
  const { domain } = await setup(t);
  assert.equal(
    row<{ n: number }>(domain.db.prepare("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM orgunits WHERE path = '/'").get())?.n,
    1,
  );
});
```

- Produces in `sql.ts`: `SqlFilter { where: string[]; params: (string | number)[] }`, `emptyFilter()`, `whereClause(filter)`, `escapeLike(value)`, `likeContains(value)`, `likePrefix(value)`, `subtree(column, path, filter)`.
- Produces: `QueryError`, `dateRange(value): { from?: number; to?: number }`, `parseDeviceQuery(query: string): SqlFilter`.
- Produces: `deviceResource(device, projection, domain)`, `deviceCount(domain)`, `listDevices(call)`, `getDevice(call)`.

Paging uses keys. The cursor is `[sort value, device ID]`. `maxResults` above 300 is clamped to 300. The spec lists this value for calibration.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the SQL helpers**

`src/store/sql.ts`:

```ts
export interface SqlFilter {
  where: string[];
  params: (string | number)[];
}

export const emptyFilter = (): SqlFilter => ({ where: [], params: [] });

export const whereClause = (filter: SqlFilter): string =>
  filter.where.length > 0 ? `WHERE ${filter.where.join(' AND ')}` : '';

/** Escape LIKE wildcards. Use with `ESCAPE '\'`. */
export const escapeLike = (value: string): string =>
  value.replace(/[\\%_]/g, (character) => `\\${character}`);

export const likeContains = (value: string): string => `%${escapeLike(value)}%`;
export const likePrefix = (value: string): string => `${escapeLike(value)}%`;

/** Match `path` and every OU below it. The root matches everything. */
export function subtree(column: string, path: string, filter: SqlFilter): void {
  if (path === '/') return;
  filter.where.push(`(${column} = ? OR substr(${column}, 1, ?) = ?)`);
  filter.params.push(path, path.length + 1, `${path}/`);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the query parser**

`src/directory/device-query.ts`:

```ts
import {
  emptyFilter,
  likeContains,
  likePrefix,
  type SqlFilter,
} from '../store/sql.ts';

export class QueryError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'QueryError';
  }
}

const DATE = /^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})(?:T(\d{2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2}))?$/;
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const LIKE = "LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'";

function instant(text: string): { start: number; end: number } {
  const match = DATE.exec(text);
  if (!match) throw new QueryError(`Invalid date: ${text}`);
  const [, year, month, day, hour, minute, second] = match;
  if (hour !== undefined) {
    const time = Date.UTC(+year, +month - 1, +day, +hour, +minute, +second);
    return { start: time, end: time + 1000 };
  }
  const start = Date.UTC(+year, +month - 1, +day);
  return { start, end: start + DAY_MS };
}

/** `D`, `..D`, `D..`, and `D1..D2`. The end of a range includes its whole day. */
export function dateRange(value: string): { from?: number; to?: number } {
  const index = value.indexOf('..');
  if (index === -1) {
    const { start, end } = instant(value);
    return { from: start, to: end };
  }
  const left = value.slice(0, index);
  const right = value.slice(index + 2);
  if (!left && !right) throw new QueryError(`Invalid date range: ${value}`);
  return {
    ...(left ? { from: instant(left).start } : {}),
    ...(right ? { to: instant(right).end } : {}),
  };
}

function range(column: string, value: string, filter: SqlFilter): void {
  const { from, to } = dateRange(value);
  if (from !== undefined) {
    filter.where.push(`${column} >= ?`);
    filter.params.push(from);
  }
  if (to !== undefined) {
    filter.where.push(`${column} < ?`);
    filter.params.push(to);
  }
}

const STATUS: Record<string, string> = {
  provisioned: "status = 'ACTIVE'",
  disabled: "status = 'DISABLED'",
  deprovisioned: "status = 'DEPROVISIONED'",
  managed: "status != 'DEPROVISIONED'",
};

const FREE_TEXT_COLUMNS = [
  'serial_number',
  'annotated_asset_id',
  'annotated_user',
  'annotated_location',
  'notes',
  'model',
];

function addTerm(filter: SqlFilter, key: string | null, value: string): void {
  const contains = (column: string) => {
    filter.where.push(`${column} ${LIKE}`);
    filter.params.push(likeContains(value));
  };
  switch (key) {
    case null:
      filter.where.push(`(${FREE_TEXT_COLUMNS.map((column) => `${column} ${LIKE}`).join(' OR ')})`);
      filter.params.push(...FREE_TEXT_COLUMNS.map(() => likeContains(value)));
      return;
    case 'id':
      if (value.length < 3)
        throw new QueryError('A serial number search needs at least three characters.');
      return contains('serial_number');
    case 'asset_id':
      return contains('annotated_asset_id');
    case 'user':
      return contains('annotated_user');
    case 'location':
      return contains('annotated_location');
    case 'note':
      return contains('notes');
    case 'public_model_name':
      return contains('model');
    case 'recent_user':
      filter.where.push(
        'EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM json_each(recent_users) WHERE json_each.value = ?)',
      );
      filter.params.push(value.toLowerCase());
      return;
    case 'status': {
      const clause = STATUS[value.toLowerCase()];
      if (!clause) throw new QueryError(`Invalid status: ${value}`);
      filter.where.push(clause);
      return;
    }
    case 'wifi_mac':
      filter.where.push('mac_address = ?');
      filter.params.push(value.toLowerCase());
      return;
    case 'ethernet_mac':
      filter.where.push('ethernet_mac_address = ?');
      filter.params.push(value.toLowerCase());
      return;
    case 'boot_mode': {
      const mode = value.toLowerCase();
      if (mode !== 'verified' && mode !== 'dev')
        throw new QueryError(`Invalid boot mode: ${value}`);
      filter.where.push('boot_mode = ?');
      filter.params.push(mode === 'dev' ? 'Dev' : 'Verified');
      return;
    }
    case 'register':
      return range('last_enrollment_time', value, filter);
    case 'sync':
      return range('last_sync', value, filter);
    case 'last_user_activity':
      return range('last_user_activity', value, filter);
    case 'aue':
      return range('aue', value, filter);
    case 'chrome_version':
      if (value.endsWith(';prefix')) {
        filter.where.push(`os_version ${LIKE}`);
        filter.params.push(likePrefix(value.slice(0, -';prefix'.length)));
        return;
      }
      return contains('os_version');
    default:
      throw new QueryError(`Unsupported query operator: ${key}`);
  }
}

const TOKEN = /([A-Za-z_]+):"([^"]*)"|"([^"]*)"|(\S+)/g;

/** Translate the documented Chrome device search operators into SQL. Terms combine with AND. */
export function parseDeviceQuery(query: string): SqlFilter {
  const filter = emptyFilter();
  for (const match of query.matchAll(TOKEN)) {
    if (match[1] !== undefined) addTerm(filter, match[1].toLowerCase(), match[2]);
    else if (match[3] !== undefined) addTerm(filter, null, match[3]);
    else {
      const token = match[4];
      const colon = token.indexOf(':');
      if (colon > 0)
        addTerm(filter, token.slice(0, colon).toLowerCase(), token.slice(colon + 1));
      else addTerm(filter, null, token);
    }
  }
  return filter;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement the device handlers**

`src/directory/devices.ts`:

```ts
import { ApiError, directoryError } from '../http/errors.ts';
import type { ApiResponse, Call, OpenDomain } from '../http/types.ts';
import type { StoredDevice } from '../seed/devices.ts';
import { deviceReports } from '../seed/reports.ts';
import {
  decodePageToken,
  encodePageToken,
  fingerprint,
} from '../store/page-token.ts';
import { emptyFilter, subtree, whereClause } from '../store/sql.ts';
import { row, rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import {
  boolParam,
  domainOf,
  enumParam,
  intParam,
  invalidArgument,
  iso,
  resolveCustomer,
  withEtag,
} from './common.ts';
import { parseDeviceQuery, QueryError } from './device-query.ts';
import { resolveOrgUnit } from './orgunits.ts';

const SORT = {
  ord: 'ord',
  annotatedLocation: "COALESCE(annotated_location, '')",
  annotatedUser: "COALESCE(annotated_user, '')",
  lastSync: 'COALESCE(last_sync, 0)',
  notes: "COALESCE(notes, '')",
  serialNumber: 'serial_number',
  status: 'status',
} as const;
type SortKey = keyof typeof SORT;

const ORDER_BY: Readonly<Record<string, SortKey>> = {
  annotatedlocation: 'annotatedLocation',
  annotateduser: 'annotatedUser',
  lastsync: 'lastSync',
  notes: 'notes',
  serialnumber: 'serialNumber',
  status: 'status',
};
const PROJECTION = { basic: 'BASIC', full: 'FULL' } as const;
const SORT_ORDER = { ascending: 'ASC', descending: 'DESC' } as const;

/** Render one stored device as a Directory API ChromeOsDevice. */
export function deviceResource(
  device: StoredDevice,
  projection: 'BASIC' | 'FULL',
  domain: OpenDomain,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const resource: Record<string, unknown> = {
    kind: 'admin#directory#chromeosdevice',
    deviceId: device.device_id,
    serialNumber: device.serial_number,
    status: device.status,
    lastSync: iso(device.last_sync),
    firstEnrollmentTime: iso(device.first_enrollment_time),
    lastEnrollmentTime: iso(device.last_enrollment_time),
    ...(device.annotated_user ? { annotatedUser: device.annotated_user } : {}),
    ...(device.annotated_location ? { annotatedLocation: device.annotated_location } : {}),
    ...(device.annotated_asset_id ? { annotatedAssetId: device.annotated_asset_id } : {}),
    ...(device.notes ? { notes: device.notes } : {}),
    model: device.model,
    ...(device.order_number ? { orderNumber: device.order_number } : {}),
    willAutoRenew: false,
    osVersion: device.os_version,
    platformVersion: device.platform_version,
    firmwareVersion: device.firmware_version,
    macAddress: device.mac_address,
    ...(device.ethernet_mac_address
      ? { ethernetMacAddress: device.ethernet_mac_address }
      : {}),
    bootMode: device.boot_mode,
    orgUnitPath: device.org_unit_path,
    orgUnitId: device.org_unit_id.replace(/^id:/, ''),
    autoUpdateExpiration: String(device.aue),
    autoUpdateThrough: iso(device.aue)!.slice(0, 10),
    osVersionCompliance:
      device.aue < domain.record.seededAt ? 'notCompliant' : 'compliant',
    deviceLicenseType: device.license_type,
    chromeOsType: 'chromeOs',
    systemRamTotal: String(device.ram_total),
    ...(device.deprovision_reason
      ? {
          deprovisionReason: device.deprovision_reason,
          lastDeprovisionTimestamp: iso(device.last_deprovision),
        }
      : {}),
  };
  if (projection === 'FULL' && device.status !== 'DEPROVISIONED')
    Object.assign(resource, deviceReports(domain.spec.seed, device));
  return withEtag(resource);
}

export function deviceCount(domain: OpenDomain): number {
  return Number(
    row<{ value: string }>(
      domain.db.prepare("SELECT value FROM meta WHERE key = 'deviceCount'").get(),
    )?.value ?? 0,
  );
}

export function listDevices(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  resolveCustomer(call, call.params.customerId);
  const query = call.request.query;
  const maxResults = intParam(call, 'maxResults', {
    fallback: 100,
    min: 1,
    max: 300,
    clamp: true,
  });
  const projection = enumParam(call, 'projection', PROJECTION, 'FULL');
  const sortKey = enumParam<SortKey>(call, 'orderBy', ORDER_BY, 'ord');
  const descending = enumParam(call, 'sortOrder', SORT_ORDER, 'ASC') === 'DESC';
  const filter = emptyFilter();

  const ouParam = query.get('orgUnitPath');
  const children = boolParam(call, 'includeChildOrgunits');
  if (children && !ouParam)
    throw invalidArgument(
      call,
      'orgUnitPath is required when includeChildOrgunits is true.',
    );
  if (ouParam) {
    const unit = resolveOrgUnit(call, domain.db, ouParam);
    if (children) subtree('org_unit_path', unit.path, filter);
    else {
      filter.where.push('org_unit_path = ?');
      filter.params.push(unit.path);
    }
  }
  const text = query.get('query');
  if (text) {
    try {
      const parsed = parseDeviceQuery(text);
      filter.where.push(...parsed.where);
      filter.params.push(...parsed.params);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof QueryError)
        throw invalidArgument(call, `Invalid Input: ${error.message}`);
      throw error;
    }
  }
  const hidden = [...call.overrides.hidden(domain.customerId)];
  if (hidden.length > 0) {
    filter.where.push(`device_id NOT IN (${hidden.map(() => '?').join(', ')})`);
    filter.params.push(...hidden);
  }

  const sort = SORT[sortKey];
  const fp = fingerprint(call.request.path, query, domain.record.seededAt);
  const pageToken = query.get('pageToken');
  if (pageToken) {
    let cursor: (string | number)[];
    try {
      cursor = decodePageToken(pageToken, fp);
    } catch {
      throw invalidArgument(call, 'Invalid Input: pageToken');
    }
    filter.where.push(`(${sort}, device_id) ${descending ? '<' : '>'} (?, ?)`);
    filter.params.push(cursor[0], cursor[1]);
  }
  const direction = descending ? 'DESC' : 'ASC';
  const found = rows<StoredDevice & { sort_key: string | number }>(
    domain.db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT *, ${sort} AS sort_key FROM devices ${whereClause(filter)} ORDER BY ${sort} ${direction}, device_id ${direction} LIMIT ?`,
      )
      .all(...filter.params, maxResults + 1),
  );
  const page = found.slice(0, maxResults);
  const last = page.at(-1);
  const nextPageToken =
    found.length > maxResults && last
      ? encodePageToken(fp, [last.sort_key, last.device_id])
      : undefined;
  const chromeosdevices = page.map((device) => deviceResource(device, projection, domain));
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: page.length,
    scanned: text ? deviceCount(domain) : 0,
    body: withEtag({
      kind: 'admin#directory#chromeosdevices',
      ...(chromeosdevices.length > 0 ? { chromeosdevices } : {}),
      ...(nextPageToken ? { nextPageToken } : {}),
    }),
  };
}

export function getDevice(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  resolveCustomer(call, call.params.customerId);
  const projection = enumParam(call, 'projection', PROJECTION, 'FULL');
  const id = call.params.deviceId;
  const device = call.overrides.hidden(domain.customerId).has(id)
    ? undefined
    : row<StoredDevice>(domain.db.prepare('SELECT * FROM devices WHERE device_id = ?').get(id));
  if (!device)
    throw new ApiError(
      directoryError(404, 'notFound', 'Resource Not Found: deviceId'),
    );
  return { status: 200, items: 1, body: deviceResource(device, projection, domain) };
}
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../auth/scopes.ts';
import { NO_OVERRIDES } from '../http/types.ts';
import { buildLayout } from '../seed/layout.ts';
import { row, rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { grantToken, seedTestDomain, startHarness } from '../testing/harness.ts';

const SPEC = {
  tier: 'small',
  seed: 'devices',
  layout: 'role-first',
  counts: { schools: 2, students: 300, staff: 40, devices: 700 },
};
const LIST = '/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/devices/chromeos';

interface Device {
  deviceId: string;
  serialNumber: string;
  lastSync: string;
  recentUsers?: unknown[];
  activeTimeRanges?: unknown[];
}

async function setup(t: import('node:test').TestContext) {
  const harness = await startHarness();
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness, SPEC);
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const list = (query: Record<string, string | number> = {}) =>
    harness.request({ host: 'admin.googleapis.com', path: LIST, token, query });
  const all = async (query: Record<string, string | number> = {}) => {
    const devices: Device[] = [];
    let pageToken: string | undefined;
    do {
      const response = await list({ ...query, ...(pageToken ? { pageToken } : {}) });
      assert.equal(response.status, 200, response.text);
      const body = response.json();
      devices.push(...(body.chromeosdevices ?? []));
      pageToken = body.nextPageToken;
    } while (pageToken);
    return devices;
  };
  const count = (where: string) =>
    Number(row<{ n: number }>(domain.db.prepare(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices WHERE ${where}`).get())?.n);
  return { harness, domain, token, list, all, count };
}

test('paging returns every device exactly once', async (t) => {
  const { all, list } = await setup(t);
  const devices = await all();
  assert.equal(devices.length, 700);
  assert.equal(new Set(devices.map((device) => device.deviceId)).size, 700);
  assert.equal((await list()).json().chromeosdevices.length, 100);
});

test('maxResults clamps at 300 and rejects values below 1', async (t) => {
  const { list } = await setup(t);
  assert.equal((await list({ maxResults: 300 })).json().chromeosdevices.length, 300);
  assert.equal((await list({ maxResults: 301 })).json().chromeosdevices.length, 300);
  assert.equal((await list({ maxResults: 0 })).status, 400);
  assert.equal((await list({ maxResults: 'abc' })).status, 400);
});

test('ordering holds across pages in both directions', async (t) => {
  const { all } = await setup(t);
  const ascending = await all({ orderBy: 'SERIAL_NUMBER', maxResults: 250 });
  const serials = ascending.map((device) => device.serialNumber);
  assert.deepEqual(serials, [...serials].sort());
  const descending = await all({ orderBy: 'lastSync', sortOrder: 'DESCENDING', maxResults: 250 });
  assert.equal(descending.length, 700);
  for (let i = 1; i < descending.length; i++)
    assert.ok(descending[i - 1].lastSync >= descending[i].lastSync);
});

test('orgUnitPath selects one OU or its subtree', async (t) => {
  const { all, count, domain, list } = await setup(t);
  const school = buildLayout(domain.spec).schools[0];
  const container = school.deviceOus.cart.split('/').slice(0, -1).join('/');
  assert.equal((await all({ orgUnitPath: container.slice(1) })).length, 0);
  const subtree = await all({ orgUnitPath: container.slice(1), includeChildOrgunits: 'true' });
  assert.equal(subtree.length, count(`org_unit_path LIKE '${container}/%'`));
  assert.ok(subtree.length > 0);
  assert.equal((await list({ includeChildOrgunits: 'true' })).status, 400);
});

test('query operators match the stored data', async (t) => {
  const { all, count, domain, list } = await setup(t);
  assert.equal((await all({ query: 'status:disabled' })).length, count("status = 'DISABLED'"));
  const sample = row<{ serial_number: string; device_id: string; annotated_asset_id: string }>(
    domain.db.prepare('SELECT serial_number, device_id, annotated_asset_id FROM devices WHERE annotated_asset_id IS NOT NULL LIMIT 1').get(),
  )!;
  const bySerial = await all({ query: `id:${sample.serial_number.slice(0, 5)}` });
  assert.ok(bySerial.some((device) => device.deviceId === sample.device_id));
  const byText = await all({ query: sample.annotated_asset_id });
  assert.ok(byText.some((device) => device.deviceId === sample.device_id));
  const since = new Date(domain.record.seededAt - 3 * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  assert.equal(
    (await all({ query: `sync:${since}..` })).length,
    count(`last_sync >= ${Date.parse(`${since}T00:00:00Z`)}`),
  );
  assert.equal((await list({ query: 'robots:1' })).status, 400);
});

test('projection BASIC drops the report arrays', async (t) => {
  const { list } = await setup(t);
  const full = (await list({ query: 'status:provisioned', maxResults: 50 })).json().chromeosdevices as Device[];
  assert.ok(full.some((device) => device.activeTimeRanges));
  assert.ok(full.some((device) => device.recentUsers));
  const basic = (await list({ query: 'status:provisioned', maxResults: 50, projection: 'BASIC' })).json().chromeosdevices as Device[];
  assert.ok(basic.every((device) => !device.activeTimeRanges && !device.recentUsers));
});

test('get returns one device or the Google not-found error', async (t) => {
  const { harness, token, domain } = await setup(t);
  const id = rows<{ device_id: string }>(domain.db.prepare('SELECT device_id FROM devices LIMIT 1').all())[0].device_id;
  const found = await harness.request({ host: 'admin.googleapis.com', path: `${LIST}/${id}`, token });
  assert.equal(found.json().deviceId, id);
  const missing = await harness.request({ host: 'admin.googleapis.com', path: `${LIST}/nope`, token });
  assert.equal(missing.status, 404);
  assert.equal(missing.json().error.message, 'Resource Not Found: deviceId');
});

test('hidden devices disappear from lists and gets', async (t) => {
  const { harness, token, domain, all } = await setup(t);
  const id = rows<{ device_id: string }>(domain.db.prepare('SELECT device_id FROM devices LIMIT 1').all())[0].device_id;
  harness.mock.overrides = { ...NO_OVERRIDES, hidden: () => new Set([id]) };
  const devices = await all();
  assert.equal(devices.length, 699);
  const response = await harness.request({ host: 'admin.googleapis.com', path: `${LIST}/${id}`, token });
  assert.equal(response.status, 404);
});

test('a page token fails for another query and after a reset', async (t) => {
  const { harness, domain, list } = await setup(t);
  const token = (await list({ query: 'status:provisioned' })).json().nextPageToken as string;
  assert.equal((await list({ query: 'status:disabled', pageToken: token })).status, 400);
  harness.mock.domains.reset(domain.customerId, domain.record.seededAt + 1);
  assert.equal((await list({ query: 'status:provisioned', pageToken: token })).status, 400);
});

test('fields trims each device', async (t) => {
  const { list } = await setup(t);
  const body = (await list({ fields: 'chromeosdevices(deviceId,serialNumber),nextPageToken' })).json();
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(body).sort(), ['chromeosdevices', 'nextPageToken']);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(body.chromeosdevices[0]).sort(), ['deviceId', 'serialNumber']);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement and register the text functions**

`src/store/text.ts`:

```ts
import type { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';

const WORD = /[\p{L}\p{N}]+/gu;

export const words = (text: string): string[] =>
  text.toLowerCase().match(WORD) ?? [];

/** True when the phrase appears in the text as consecutive whole words. */
export function containsWords(text: string | null, phrase: string): boolean {
  if (text === null) return false;
  const haystack = words(text);
  const needle = words(phrase);
  if (needle.length === 0) return false;
  outer: for (let i = 0; i + needle.length <= haystack.length; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < needle.length; j++)
      if (haystack[i + j] !== needle[j]) continue outer;
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** True when the text, or any word in it, starts with the prefix. */
export function hasWordPrefix(text: string | null, prefix: string): boolean {
  if (text === null) return false;
  const lower = prefix.toLowerCase();
  return (
    text.toLowerCase().startsWith(lower) ||
    words(text).some((word) => word.startsWith(lower))
  );
}

export function registerTextFunctions(db: DatabaseSync): void {
  db.function('cc_contains_words', { deterministic: true }, (text, phrase) =>
    containsWords(text as string | null, String(phrase)) ? 1 : 0,
  );
  db.function('cc_word_prefix', { deterministic: true }, (text, prefix) =>
    hasWordPrefix(text as string | null, String(prefix)) ? 1 : 0,
  );
}
```

In `src/store/domain-db.ts`, import `registerTextFunctions` from `./text.ts` and call it in `openDomainDb` before `return db;`.

```ts
test('clauses become SQL with parameters', () => {
  const filter = parseUserQuery("givenName:Jane familyName:Smi* isSuspended=true orgUnitPath='/Staff/Lincoln Elementary'");
  assert.deepEqual(filter.where, [
    'cc_contains_words(given_name, ?)',
    'cc_word_prefix(family_name, ?)',
    'suspended = ?',
    '(org_unit_path = ? OR substr(org_unit_path, 1, ?) = ?)',

    'Jane',
    'Smi',
    1,
    '/Staff/Lincoln Elementary',
    '/Staff/Lincoln Elementary'.length + 1,
    '/Staff/Lincoln Elementary/',
  ]);
});

});

test('the root OU and a bare term need no OU clause', () => {

});

test('unsupported fields and bad booleans fail', () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the user query parser**

`src/directory/user-query.ts`:

```ts
import { emptyFilter, likePrefix, subtree, type SqlFilter } from '../store/sql.ts';
import { QueryError } from './device-query.ts';

const CLAUSE = /([A-Za-z]+)(=|:)('(?:[^'\\]|\\.)*'|\S*)|('(?:[^'\\]|\\.)*'|\S+)/g;

const unquote = (value: string): string =>
  value.length >= 2 && value.startsWith("'") && value.endsWith("'")
    ? value.slice(1, -1).replace(/\\(.)/g, '$1')
    : value;

function addClause(filter: SqlFilter, field: string, op: '=' | ':', value: string): void {
  const text = (column: string) => {
    if (op === '=') {
      filter.where.push(`lower(${column}) = lower(?)`);
      filter.params.push(value);
    } else if (value.endsWith('*')) {
      filter.where.push(`cc_word_prefix(${column}, ?)`);
      filter.params.push(value.slice(0, -1));
    } else {
      filter.where.push(`cc_contains_words(${column}, ?)`);
      filter.params.push(value);
    }
  };
  const flag = (column: string) => {
    const lower = value.toLowerCase();
    if (op !== '=' || (lower !== 'true' && lower !== 'false'))
      throw new QueryError(`${field} accepts only =true or =false.`);
    filter.where.push(`${column} = ?`);
    filter.params.push(lower === 'true' ? 1 : 0);
  };
  switch (field) {
    case 'name':
      return text('full_name');
    case 'givenName':
      return text('given_name');
    case 'familyName':
      return text('family_name');
    case 'email':
      if (op === '=') {
        filter.where.push(
          '(primary_email = lower(?) OR id IN (SELECT user_id FROM user_aliases WHERE alias = lower(?)))',
        );
        filter.params.push(value, value);
        return;
      }
      if (value.endsWith('*')) {
        const prefix = likePrefix(value.slice(0, -1).toLowerCase());
        filter.where.push(
          "(primary_email LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\' OR id IN (SELECT user_id FROM user_aliases WHERE alias LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\'))",
        );
        filter.params.push(prefix, prefix);
        return;
      }
      return text('primary_email');
    case 'isAdmin':
      return flag('is_admin');
    case 'isSuspended':
      return flag('suspended');
    case 'externalId':
      if (op === '=') {
        filter.where.push('external_id = ?');
        filter.params.push(value);
        return;
      }
      return text('external_id');
    case 'orgUnitPath': {
      if (op !== '=') throw new QueryError('orgUnitPath accepts only =.');
      const path = value === '' ? '/' : value.startsWith('/') ? value : `/${value}`;
      subtree('org_unit_path', path, filter);
      return;
    }
    default:
      throw new QueryError(`Unsupported query field: ${field}`);
  }
}

/** Translate the documented user search syntax into SQL. Clauses combine with AND. */
export function parseUserQuery(query: string): SqlFilter {
  const filter = emptyFilter();
  for (const match of query.matchAll(CLAUSE)) {
    if (match[1] !== undefined)
      addClause(filter, match[1], match[2] as '=' | ':', unquote(match[3]));
    else {
      const value = unquote(match[4]);
      filter.where.push(
        '(cc_contains_words(given_name, ?) OR cc_contains_words(family_name, ?) OR cc_contains_words(primary_email, ?))',
      );
      filter.params.push(value, value, value);
    }
  }
  return filter;
}
```

`src/directory/users.ts`:

```ts
import type { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { ApiError, directoryError, notAuthorized } from '../http/errors.ts';
import type { ApiResponse, Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import { decodePageToken, encodePageToken, fingerprint } from '../store/page-token.ts';
import { emptyFilter, whereClause } from '../store/sql.ts';
import { row, rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { findUser, type UserRow } from '../store/user-lookup.ts';
import {
  domainOf,
  enumParam,
  intParam,
  invalidArgument,
  iso,
  resolveCustomer,
  withEtag,
} from './common.ts';
import { QueryError } from './device-query.ts';
import { parseUserQuery } from './user-query.ts';

type View = 'admin_view' | 'domain_public';
const VIEW = { adminview: 'admin_view', domainpublic: 'domain_public' } as const;
const PROJECTION = { basic: 'basic', full: 'full', custom: 'custom' } as const;
const SORT_ORDER = { ascending: 'ASC', descending: 'DESC' } as const;
const SORT = { email: 'primary_email', familyName: 'family_name', givenName: 'given_name' } as const;
const ORDER_BY: Readonly<Record<string, keyof typeof SORT>> = {
  email: 'email',
  familyname: 'familyName',
  givenname: 'givenName',
};
const NEVER = '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';
const counts = new WeakMap<DatabaseSync, number>();

function userCount(db: DatabaseSync): number {
  let count = counts.get(db);
  if (count === undefined) {
    count = Number(row<{ n: number }>(db.prepare('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM users').get())?.n);
    counts.set(db, count);
  }
  return count;
}

export function userResource(
  user: UserRow,
  view: View,
  aliases: readonly string[],
  customerId: string,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const name = {
    givenName: user.given_name,
    familyName: user.family_name,
    fullName: user.full_name,
  };
  const emails = [
    { address: user.primary_email, primary: true },
    ...aliases.map((address) => ({ address })),
  ];
  if (view === 'domain_public')
    return withEtag({
      kind: 'admin#directory#user',
      id: user.id,
      primaryEmail: user.primary_email,
      name,
      emails,
    });
  return withEtag({
    kind: 'admin#directory#user',
    id: user.id,
    primaryEmail: user.primary_email,
    name,
    isAdmin: user.is_admin === 1,
    isDelegatedAdmin: false,
    lastLoginTime: iso(user.last_login_time) ?? NEVER,
    creationTime: iso(user.creation_time),
    agreedToTerms: true,
    suspended: user.suspended === 1,
    ...(user.suspended === 1
      ? { suspensionReason: user.suspension_reason ?? 'ADMIN' }
      : {}),
    archived: false,
    changePasswordAtNextLogin: false,
    ipWhitelisted: false,
    emails,
    ...(aliases.length > 0 ? { aliases: [...aliases] } : {}),
    ...(user.external_id
      ? { externalIds: [{ value: user.external_id, type: 'organization' }] }
      : {}),
    customerId,
    orgUnitPath: user.org_unit_path,
    isMailboxSetup: true,
    isEnrolledIn2Sv: false,
    isEnforcedIn2Sv: false,
    includeInGlobalAddressList: true,
  });
}

function aliasesFor(db: DatabaseSync, ids: readonly string[]): Map<string, string[]> {
  const out = new Map<string, string[]>();
  if (ids.length === 0) return out;
  for (const { alias, user_id } of rows<{ alias: string; user_id: string }>(
    db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT alias, user_id FROM user_aliases WHERE user_id IN (${ids.map(() => '?').join(', ')}) ORDER BY alias`,
      )
      .all(...ids),
  ))
    out.set(user_id, [...(out.get(user_id) ?? []), alias]);
  return out;
}

function viewOf(call: Call): View {
  const view = enumParam<View>(call, 'viewType', VIEW, 'admin_view');
  if (view === 'admin_view' && !call.access?.isAdmin)
    throw new ApiError(notAuthorized('directory'));
  return view;
}

export function listUsers(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  const query = call.request.query;
  const customer = query.get('customer');
  const domainName = query.get('domain')?.toLowerCase();
  if (!customer && !domainName) throw invalidArgument(call, 'Bad Request', 'badRequest');
  const filter = emptyFilter();
  if (customer) resolveCustomer(call, customer);
  else {
    const known = row(
      domain.db
        .prepare('SELECT domain_name FROM domains WHERE domain_name = ? AND alias_of IS NULL')
        .get(domainName!),
    );
    if (!known) throw new ApiError(notAuthorized('directory'));
    filter.where.push('substr(primary_email, ?) = ?');
    filter.params.push(-(domainName!.length + 1), `@${domainName}`);
  }
  const view = viewOf(call);
  const maxResults = intParam(call, 'maxResults', { fallback: 100, min: 1, max: 500 });
  enumParam(call, 'projection', PROJECTION, 'basic');
  const sort = SORT[enumParam(call, 'orderBy', ORDER_BY, 'email')];
  const descending = enumParam(call, 'sortOrder', SORT_ORDER, 'ASC') === 'DESC';
  const text = query.get('query');
  if (text) {
    try {
      const parsed = parseUserQuery(text);
      filter.where.push(...parsed.where);
      filter.params.push(...parsed.params);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof QueryError)
        throw invalidArgument(call, `Invalid Input: ${error.message}`);
      throw error;
    }
  }
  const hidden = [...call.overrides.hidden(domain.customerId)];
  if (hidden.length > 0) {
    const marks = hidden.map(() => '?').join(', ');
    filter.where.push(`id NOT IN (${marks}) AND primary_email NOT IN (${marks})`);
    filter.params.push(...hidden, ...hidden);
  }
  const fp = fingerprint(call.request.path, query, domain.record.seededAt);
  const pageToken = query.get('pageToken');
  if (pageToken) {
    let cursor: (string | number)[];
    try {
      cursor = decodePageToken(pageToken, fp);
    } catch {
      throw invalidArgument(call, 'Invalid Input: pageToken');
    }
    filter.where.push(`(${sort}, id) ${descending ? '<' : '>'} (?, ?)`);
    filter.params.push(cursor[0], cursor[1]);
  }
  const direction = descending ? 'DESC' : 'ASC';
  const found = rows<UserRow & { sort_key: string }>(
    domain.db
      .prepare(
        `SELECT *, ${sort} AS sort_key FROM users ${whereClause(filter)} ORDER BY ${sort} ${direction}, id ${direction} LIMIT ?`,
      )
      .all(...filter.params, maxResults + 1),
  );
  const page = found.slice(0, maxResults);
  const aliases = aliasesFor(domain.db, page.map((user) => user.id));
  const last = page.at(-1);
  const nextPageToken =
    found.length > maxResults && last ? encodePageToken(fp, [last.sort_key, last.id]) : undefined;
  const users = page.map((user) =>
    userResource(user, view, aliases.get(user.id) ?? [], domain.customerId),
  );
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: page.length,
    scanned: text ? userCount(domain.db) : 0,
    body: withEtag({
      kind: 'admin#directory#users',
      ...(users.length > 0 ? { users } : {}),
      ...(nextPageToken ? { nextPageToken } : {}),
    }),
  };
}

export function getUser(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  const view = viewOf(call);
  const user = findUser(domain.db, call.params.userKey);
  const hidden = call.overrides.hidden(domain.customerId);
  if (!user || hidden.has(user.id) || hidden.has(user.primary_email))
    throw new ApiError(directoryError(404, 'notFound', 'Resource Not Found: userKey'));
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: 1,
    body: userResource(
      user,
      view,
      aliasesFor(domain.db, [user.id]).get(user.id) ?? [],
      domain.customerId,
    ),
  };
}
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../auth/scopes.ts';
import { NO_OVERRIDES } from '../http/types.ts';
import { row } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { adminOf, grantToken, seedTestDomain, startHarness } from '../testing/harness.ts';

const SPEC = {
  tier: 'small',
  seed: 'users',
  layout: 'school-first',
  counts: { schools: 2, students: 400, staff: 60, devices: 50 },
};
const LIST = '/admin/directory/v1/users';

interface User {
  id: string;
  primaryEmail: string;
  name: { familyName: string };
  isAdmin?: boolean;
  lastLoginTime?: string;
}

async function setup(t: import('node:test').TestContext) {
  const harness = await startHarness();
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness, SPEC);
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const list = (query: Record<string, string | number>, bearer = token) =>
    harness.request({ host: 'admin.googleapis.com', path: LIST, token: bearer, query });
  const all = async (query: Record<string, string | number>) => {
    const users: User[] = [];
    let pageToken: string | undefined;
    do {
      const response = await list({ customer: 'my_customer', ...query, ...(pageToken ? { pageToken } : {}) });
      assert.equal(response.status, 200, response.text);
      users.push(...(response.json().users ?? []));
      pageToken = response.json().nextPageToken;
    } while (pageToken);
    return users;
  };
  const count = (where: string, ...params: (string | number)[]) =>
    Number(row<{ n: number }>(domain.db.prepare(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM users WHERE ${where}`).get(...params))?.n);
  const one = <T>(sql: string) => row<T>(domain.db.prepare(sql).get())!;
  return { harness, domain, token, list, all, count, one };
}

test('paging returns every user exactly once', async (t) => {
  const { all, count } = await setup(t);
  const users = await all({ maxResults: 500 });
  assert.equal(users.length, count('1 = 1'));
  assert.equal(new Set(users.map((user) => user.id)).size, users.length);
});

test('the domain parameter limits users to one domain', async (t) => {
  const { all, list, domain } = await setup(t);
  const response = await list({ domain: domain.spec.primaryDomain, maxResults: 500 });
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  const users = response.json().users as User[];
  assert.ok(users.every((user) => user.primaryEmail.endsWith(`@${domain.spec.primaryDomain}`)));
  assert.ok((await all({})).length >= users.length);
});

test('missing customer and domain, and bad page sizes, fail with 400', async (t) => {
  const { list } = await setup(t);
  assert.equal((await list({})).status, 400);
  const tooMany = await list({ customer: 'my_customer', maxResults: 501 });
  assert.equal(tooMany.status, 400);
  assert.match(tooMany.json().error.message, /\[1, 500\]/);
  assert.equal((await list({ customer: 'my_customer', maxResults: 0 })).status, 400);
});

test('orderBy familyName sorts across pages', async (t) => {
  const { all } = await setup(t);
  const users = await all({ orderBy: 'familyName', maxResults: 100 });
  const names = users.map((user) => user.name.familyName);
  assert.deepEqual(names, [...names].sort((a, b) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0)));
});

test('query clauses match the stored data', async (t) => {
  const { all, count, one, domain } = await setup(t);
  assert.equal((await all({ query: `email=${adminOf(domain)}` })).length, 1);
  assert.equal((await all({ query: 'isAdmin=true' })).length, 3);
  assert.equal((await all({ query: 'isSuspended=true' })).length, count('suspended = 1'));
  const alias = one<{ alias: string; user_id: string }>('SELECT alias, user_id FROM user_aliases LIMIT 1');
  if (alias) {
    const found = await all({ query: `email=${alias.alias}` });
    assert.deepEqual(found.map((user) => user.id), [alias.user_id]);
  }
  const staffOu = one<{ org_unit_path: string }>("SELECT org_unit_path FROM users WHERE role = 'staff' AND org_unit_path LIKE '% %' LIMIT 1");
  if (staffOu) {
    const path = staffOu.org_unit_path;
    const found = await all({ query: `orgUnitPath='${path}'` });
    assert.equal(found.length, count('org_unit_path = ? OR substr(org_unit_path, 1, ?) = ?', path, path.length + 1, `${path}/`));
  }
  const family = one<{ family_name: string }>('SELECT family_name FROM users LIMIT 1').family_name;
  const prefix = await all({ query: `familyName:${family.slice(0, 3)}*` });
  assert.ok(prefix.every((user) => user.name.familyName.toLowerCase().startsWith(family.slice(0, 3).toLowerCase())));
  const external = await all({ query: 'externalId=E100000' });
  assert.equal(external.length, count("external_id = 'E100000'"));
  assert.equal((await all({ query: "orgUnitPath='/'" })).length, count('1 = 1'));
});

test('an unsupported query field fails with 400', async (t) => {
  const { list } = await setup(t);
  assert.equal((await list({ customer: 'my_customer', query: 'robot=1' })).status, 400);
});

test('a non-administrator reads only the public view', async (t) => {
  const { harness, domain, list, one } = await setup(t);
  const staff = one<{ primary_email: string }>('SELECT primary_email FROM users WHERE is_admin = 0 LIMIT 1').primary_email;
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES, staff);
  assert.equal((await list({ customer: 'my_customer' }, token)).status, 403);
  const open = await list({ customer: 'my_customer', viewType: 'domain_public' }, token);
  assert.equal(open.status, 200);
  assert.ok((open.json().users as User[]).every((user) => user.isAdmin === undefined));
});

test('get finds users by email, alias, and ID', async (t) => {
  const { harness, token, domain, one } = await setup(t);
  const get = (key: string) =>
    harness.request({ host: 'admin.googleapis.com', path: `${LIST}/${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, token });
  const admin = (await get(adminOf(domain))).json();
  assert.equal(admin.isAdmin, true);
  assert.equal((await get(admin.id)).json().primaryEmail, adminOf(domain));
  const alias = one<{ alias: string; user_id: string } | undefined>('SELECT alias, user_id FROM user_aliases LIMIT 1');
  if (alias) assert.equal((await get(alias.alias)).json().id, alias.user_id);
  const missing = await get(`nobody@${domain.spec.primaryDomain}`);
  assert.equal(missing.status, 404);
  assert.equal(missing.json().error.message, 'Resource Not Found: userKey');
});

test('a user who never signed in reports the epoch as the last login', async (t) => {
  const { harness, token, one } = await setup(t);
  const never = one<{ primary_email: string } | undefined>('SELECT primary_email FROM users WHERE last_login_time IS NULL LIMIT 1');
  assert.ok(never, 'The seed includes a user who never signed in.');
  const user = (await harness.request({ host: 'admin.googleapis.com', path: `${LIST}/${never.primary_email}`, token })).json();
  assert.equal(user.lastLoginTime, '1970-01-01T00:00:00.000Z');
});

test('hidden users disappear', async (t) => {
  const { harness, all, domain, count } = await setup(t);
  harness.mock.overrides = { ...NO_OVERRIDES, hidden: () => new Set([adminOf(domain)]) };
  assert.equal((await all({ maxResults: 500 })).length, count('1 = 1') - 1);
});
```

### Task 13: Chrome Management telemetry

**Files:**
- Create: `src/chrome/telemetry-filter.ts`, `src/chrome/telemetry.ts`
- Modify: `src/routes.ts`
- Test: `src/chrome/telemetry.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 6 and 9 through 11. `batteryTelemetry` from Task 6.
- Produces: `FilterError`, `TelemetryFilter { sql: SqlFilter; reportsFrom: number | null }`, `parseTelemetryFilter(text: string): TelemetryFilter`.
- Produces: `telemetryDevice(device, domain, mask, reportsFrom, recentReports)`, `listTelemetry(call)`, `getTelemetry(call)`.

Google documents that requests without `reports_timestamp` "default to returning only recent reports". The mock returns the newest `settings.telemetry.recentReports` reports, which defaults to 1. `reports_timestamp>=0` returns all 30. Campus Commander sends no filter today, so it receives one battery report per device. Report this finding to the owner in the Task 24 record.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`src/chrome/telemetry.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../auth/scopes.ts';
import { row } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { grantToken, seedTestDomain, startHarness } from '../testing/harness.ts';
import { FilterError, parseTelemetryFilter } from './telemetry-filter.ts';

const SPEC = { tier: 'small', seed: 'telemetry', counts: { schools: 2, students: 100, staff: 20, devices: 400 } };
const LIST = '/v1/customers/my_customer/telemetry/devices';

async function setup(t: import('node:test').TestContext) {
  const harness = await startHarness();
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness, SPEC);
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const request = (path: string, query: Record<string, string | number> = {}) =>
    harness.request({ host: 'chromemanagement.googleapis.com', path, token, query });
  const count = (where: string) =>
    Number(row<{ n: number }>(domain.db.prepare(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices WHERE ${where}`).get())?.n);
  return { harness, domain, request, count };
}

test('the filter parser accepts the documented fields', () => {
  const parsed = parseTelemetryFilter('serialNumber="PF123" AND reports_timestamp>=0');
  assert.deepEqual(parsed.sql.where, ['serial_number = ?']);
  assert.deepEqual(parsed.sql.params, ['PF123']);
  assert.equal(parsed.reportsFrom, 0);
  assert.equal(parseTelemetryFilter('reports_timestamp>="2026-10-01T00:00:00Z"').reportsFrom, Date.parse('2026-10-01T00:00:00Z'));
  assert.deepEqual(parseTelemetryFilter('orgUnitId=03abc').sql.params, ['id:03abc']);
  for (const text of ['robot=1', 'reports_timestamp<5', 'serialNumber>x', 'reports_timestamp>=soon'])
    assert.throws(() => parseTelemetryFilter(text), FilterError, text);
});

test('paging lists every device that is not deprovisioned', async (t) => {
  const { request, count } = await setup(t);
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  let pageToken: string | undefined;
  do {
    const response = await request(LIST, { readMask: 'deviceId', pageSize: 1000, ...(pageToken ? { pageToken } : {}) });
    assert.equal(response.status, 200, response.text);
    for (const device of response.json().devices ?? []) ids.add(device.deviceId);
    pageToken = response.json().nextPageToken;
  } while (pageToken);
  assert.equal(ids.size, count("status != 'DEPROVISIONED'"));
});

test('pageSize defaults to 100 and clamps at 1000', async (t) => {
  const { request } = await setup(t);
  assert.equal((await request(LIST, { readMask: 'deviceId' })).json().devices.length, 100);
  assert.ok((await request(LIST, { readMask: 'deviceId', pageSize: 5000 })).json().devices.length <= 1000);
});

test('readMask is required and selects the returned fields', async (t) => {
  const { request } = await setup(t);
  const missing = await request(LIST);
  assert.equal(missing.status, 400);
  assert.equal(missing.json().error.status, 'INVALID_ARGUMENT');
  const device = (await request(LIST, { readMask: 'deviceId,serialNumber', pageSize: 1 })).json().devices[0];
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(device).sort(), ['deviceId', 'serialNumber']);
});

test('only recent battery reports return without a timestamp filter', async (t) => {
  const { harness, domain, request } = await setup(t);
  const mask = { readMask: 'deviceId,batteryInfo,batteryStatusReport', pageSize: 50 };
  const withBattery = (body: { devices: { batteryStatusReport?: unknown[] }[] }) =>
    body.devices.filter((device) => device.batteryStatusReport);
  const recent = withBattery((await request(LIST, mask)).json());
  assert.ok(recent.length > 0);
  assert.ok(recent.every((device) => device.batteryStatusReport!.length === 1));
  const all = withBattery((await request(LIST, { ...mask, filter: 'reports_timestamp>=0' })).json());
  assert.ok(all.every((device) => device.batteryStatusReport!.length === 30));
  harness.mock.domains.updateSettings(domain.customerId, { telemetry: { recentReports: 5 } });
  const five = withBattery((await request(LIST, mask)).json());
  assert.ok(five.every((device) => device.batteryStatusReport!.length === 5));
});

test('filters select by serial number and OU', async (t) => {
  const { domain, request, count } = await setup(t);
  const sample = row<{ serial_number: string; org_unit_id: string }>(
    domain.db.prepare("SELECT serial_number, org_unit_id FROM devices WHERE status = 'ACTIVE' LIMIT 1").get(),
  )!;
  const bySerial = (await request(LIST, { readMask: 'serialNumber', filter: `serialNumber="${sample.serial_number}"` })).json();
  assert.deepEqual(bySerial.devices.map((device: { serialNumber: string }) => device.serialNumber), [sample.serial_number]);
  const byOu = (await request(LIST, { readMask: 'deviceId', pageSize: 1000, filter: `orgUnitId=${sample.org_unit_id.slice(3)}` })).json();
  assert.equal(byOu.devices.length, count(`org_unit_id = '${sample.org_unit_id}' AND status != 'DEPROVISIONED'`));
  const bad = await request(LIST, { readMask: 'deviceId', filter: 'robot=1' });
  assert.equal(bad.status, 400);
});

test('get returns one device, and 404 for missing or deprovisioned devices', async (t) => {
  const { domain, request } = await setup(t);
  const active = row<{ device_id: string }>(domain.db.prepare("SELECT device_id FROM devices WHERE status = 'ACTIVE' LIMIT 1").get())!;
  const found = await request(`${LIST}/${active.device_id}`, { readMask: 'name,deviceId' });
  assert.equal(found.json().deviceId, active.device_id);
  assert.equal(found.json().name, `customers/${domain.customerId}/telemetry/devices/${active.device_id}`);
  const missing = await request(`${LIST}/nope`, { readMask: 'deviceId' });
  assert.equal(missing.status, 404);
  assert.equal(missing.json().error.status, 'NOT_FOUND');
  const gone = row<{ device_id: string }>(domain.db.prepare("SELECT device_id FROM devices WHERE status = 'DEPROVISIONED' LIMIT 1").get());
  if (gone) assert.equal((await request(`${LIST}/${gone.device_id}`, { readMask: 'deviceId' })).status, 404);
});

test('another customer fails with PERMISSION_DENIED', async (t) => {
  const { request } = await setup(t);
  const response = await request('/v1/customers/C0zzzzzzz/telemetry/devices', { readMask: 'deviceId' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 403);
  assert.equal(response.json().error.status, 'PERMISSION_DENIED');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the filter parser**

`src/chrome/telemetry-filter.ts`:

```ts
import { emptyFilter, type SqlFilter } from '../store/sql.ts';

export class FilterError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'FilterError';
  }
}

export interface TelemetryFilter {
  sql: SqlFilter;
  /** The earliest report time to return, or null for recent reports only. */
  reportsFrom: number | null;
}

const CLAUSE = /^([A-Za-z_]+)(>=|<=|>|<|=)(.+)$/;

function timestamp(value: string): number {
  if (/^\d+$/.test(value)) return Number(value);
  const parsed = Date.parse(value);
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/.test(value) || Number.isNaN(parsed))
    throw new FilterError(`Invalid reports_timestamp: ${value}`);
  return parsed;
}

/** Parse `field=value` clauses joined by spaces or AND. */
export function parseTelemetryFilter(text: string): TelemetryFilter {
  const result: TelemetryFilter = { sql: emptyFilter(), reportsFrom: null };
  for (const clause of text.split(/\s+(?:AND\s+)?/).filter(Boolean)) {
    const match = CLAUSE.exec(clause);
    if (!match) throw new FilterError(`Invalid filter clause: ${clause}`);
    const [, field, op, raw] = match;
    const value = raw.replace(/^"(.*)"$/, '$1');
    if (field === 'reports_timestamp') {
      if (op !== '>=' && op !== '>')
        throw new FilterError('reports_timestamp supports only >= and >.');
      const time = timestamp(value);
      result.reportsFrom = op === '>' ? time + 1 : time;
      continue;
    }
    if (op !== '=') throw new FilterError(`${field} supports only =.`);
    const column = {
      orgUnitId: 'org_unit_id',
      serialNumber: 'serial_number',
      deviceId: 'device_id',
    }[field];
    if (!column) throw new FilterError(`Unsupported filter field: ${field}`);
    result.sql.where.push(`${column} = ?`);
    result.sql.params.push(
      field === 'orgUnitId' && !value.startsWith('id:') ? `id:${value}` : value,
    );
  }
  return result;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the telemetry handlers**

`src/chrome/telemetry.ts`:

```ts
import { invalidArgument, domainOf, intParam, resolveCustomer } from '../directory/common.ts';
import { ApiError, rpcError } from '../http/errors.ts';
import type { ApiResponse, Call, OpenDomain } from '../http/types.ts';
import type { StoredDevice } from '../seed/devices.ts';
import { batteryTelemetry } from '../seed/reports.ts';
import { decodePageToken, encodePageToken, fingerprint } from '../store/page-token.ts';
import { whereClause } from '../store/sql.ts';
import { row, rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { FilterError, parseTelemetryFilter } from './telemetry-filter.ts';

function readMask(call: Call): Set<string> {
  const raw = call.request.query.get('readMask');
  const paths = (raw ?? '')
    .split(',')
    .map((path) => path.trim().split('.')[0])
    .filter(Boolean);
  if (paths.length === 0) throw invalidArgument(call, 'readMask is required');
  return new Set(paths);
}

/** Render the fields of one telemetry device that the read mask selects. */
export function telemetryDevice(
  device: StoredDevice,
  domain: OpenDomain,
  mask: ReadonlySet<string>,
  reportsFrom: number | null,
  recentReports: number,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (mask.has('name'))
    out.name = `customers/${domain.customerId}/telemetry/devices/${device.device_id}`;
  if (mask.has('customer')) out.customer = `customers/${domain.customerId}`;
  if (mask.has('orgUnitId')) out.orgUnitId = device.org_unit_id.replace(/^id:/, '');
  if (mask.has('deviceId')) out.deviceId = device.device_id;
  if (mask.has('serialNumber')) out.serialNumber = device.serial_number;
  if (mask.has('batteryInfo') || mask.has('batteryStatusReport')) {
    const battery = batteryTelemetry(domain.spec.seed, device);
    if (battery) {
      if (mask.has('batteryInfo')) out.batteryInfo = battery.batteryInfo;
      if (mask.has('batteryStatusReport')) {
        const reports =
          reportsFrom === null
            ? battery.batteryStatusReport.slice(-recentReports)
            : battery.batteryStatusReport.filter(
                (report) => Date.parse(String(report.reportTime)) >= reportsFrom,
              );
        if (reports.length > 0) out.batteryStatusReport = reports;
      }
    }
  }
  if (mask.has('memoryInfo'))
    out.memoryInfo = {
      totalRamBytes: String(device.ram_total),
      availableRamBytes: String(Math.round(device.ram_total * 0.4)),
    };
  if (mask.has('storageInfo'))
    out.storageInfo = {
      totalDiskBytes: String(device.storage_total),
      availableDiskBytes: String(Math.round(device.storage_total * 0.5)),
    };
  return out;
}

export function listTelemetry(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  resolveCustomer(call, call.params.customer);
  const mask = readMask(call);
  const pageSize =
    intParam(call, 'pageSize', { fallback: 100, min: 0, max: 1000, clamp: true }) || 100;
  let parsed;
  try {
    parsed = parseTelemetryFilter(call.request.query.get('filter') ?? '');
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof FilterError) throw invalidArgument(call, error.message);
    throw error;
  }
  const filter = parsed.sql;
  filter.where.push("status != 'DEPROVISIONED'");
  const hidden = [...call.overrides.hidden(domain.customerId)];
  if (hidden.length > 0) {
    filter.where.push(`device_id NOT IN (${hidden.map(() => '?').join(', ')})`);
    filter.params.push(...hidden);
  }
  const fp = fingerprint(call.request.path, call.request.query, domain.record.seededAt);
  const pageToken = call.request.query.get('pageToken');
  if (pageToken) {
    let cursor: (string | number)[];
    try {
      cursor = decodePageToken(pageToken, fp);
    } catch {
      throw invalidArgument(call, 'Invalid page token.');
    }
    filter.where.push('ord > ?');
    filter.params.push(cursor[0]);
  }
  const found = rows<StoredDevice>(
    domain.db
      .prepare(`SELECT * FROM devices ${whereClause(filter)} ORDER BY ord LIMIT ?`)
      .all(...filter.params, pageSize + 1),
  );
  const page = found.slice(0, pageSize);
  const last = page.at(-1);
  const recent = domain.record.settings.telemetry.recentReports;
  const devices = page.map((device) =>
    telemetryDevice(device, domain, mask, parsed.reportsFrom, recent),
  );
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: page.length,
    body: {
      ...(devices.length > 0 ? { devices } : {}),
      ...(found.length > pageSize && last
        ? { nextPageToken: encodePageToken(fp, [last.ord]) }
        : {}),
    },
  };
}

export function getTelemetry(call: Call): ApiResponse {
  const domain = domainOf(call);
  resolveCustomer(call, call.params.customer);
  const mask = readMask(call);
  const id = call.params.deviceId;
  const device = call.overrides.hidden(domain.customerId).has(id)
    ? undefined
    : row<StoredDevice>(
        domain.db
          .prepare("SELECT * FROM devices WHERE device_id = ? AND status != 'DEPROVISIONED'")
          .get(id),
      );
  if (!device)
    throw new ApiError(rpcError(404, 'NOT_FOUND', 'Requested entity was not found.'));
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: 1,
    body: telemetryDevice(
      device,
      domain,
      mask,
      null,
      domain.record.settings.telemetry.recentReports,
    ),
  };
}
```

### Task 14: The batch endpoint

**Files:**
- Create: `src/batch/multipart.ts`, `src/batch/batch.ts`
- Modify: `src/routes.ts`
- Test: `src/batch/multipart.test.ts`, `src/batch/batch.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7 `Call.dispatchPart`, Task 7 errors.
- Produces: `BatchPart { contentId: string | null; method; host; path; query; headers; body }`, `BatchFormatError`, `parseBatchRequest(contentType, body): BatchPart[]`, `BatchPartResult { contentId; status; headers; body }`, `buildBatchResponse(results, boundary): string`.
- Produces: `MAX_BATCH_PARTS = 1000`, `BATCH_WAVE = 10`, `handleBatch(call): Promise<ApiResponse>`. The batch response sets `items` to the part count for the latency model.

Each part runs through the full pipeline with `batchPart: true`. Quota admission therefore counts each part. A part without an `Authorization` header uses the outer request's header.

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

/** The exact body that Campus Commander's buildMultipartBody produces. */
const CAMPUS_COMMANDER = [
  '--batch_x',
  'Content-Type: application/http',
  'Content-ID: <cc-1>',
  '',
  'GET /admin/directory/v1/customer/C0123456/devices/chromeos/d1?fields=deviceId',
  '',
  '--batch_x',
  'Content-Type: application/http',
  'Content-ID: <cc-2>',
  '',
  'POST /admin/directory/v1/users',
  'Content-Type: application/json; charset=UTF-8',
  '',
  '{"a":1}',
  '--batch_x--',
  '',
].join('\r\n');

test('the Campus Commander format parses into parts', () => {

  assert.match(text, /HTTP\/1\.1 404 Not Found/);
  assert.ok(text.endsWith('--resp--\r\n'));
});
```
