- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the multipart codec**

`src/batch/multipart.ts`:

```ts
import { STATUS_CODES } from 'node:http';

export interface BatchPart {
  contentId: string | null;
  method: string;
  host: string;
  path: string;
  query: URLSearchParams;
  headers: Record<string, string>;
  body: string;
}

export interface BatchPartResult {
  contentId: string | null;
  status: number;
  headers: Record<string, string>;
  body: string;
}

export class BatchFormatError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'BatchFormatError';
  }
}

function splitHead(text: string): [string, string] {
  const match = /\r?\n\r?\n/.exec(text);
  if (!match) return [text, ''];
  return [text.slice(0, match.index), text.slice(match.index + match[0].length)];
}

function parseHeaders(text: string): Record<string, string> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const line of text.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const colon = line.indexOf(':');
    if (colon > 0)
      headers[line.slice(0, colon).trim().toLowerCase()] = line.slice(colon + 1).trim();
  }
  return headers;
}

/** Parse a multipart/mixed batch body into its HTTP requests. */
export function parseBatchRequest(contentType: string, body: string): BatchPart[] {
  const match = /boundary=(?:"([^"]+)"|([^;\s]+))/i.exec(contentType);
  const boundary = match?.[1] ?? match?.[2];
  if (!/^multipart\/mixed/i.test(contentType.trim()) || !boundary)
    throw new BatchFormatError(
      'A batch request needs a multipart/mixed content type with a boundary.',
    );
  const sections = body.split(`--${boundary}`);
  const parts: BatchPart[] = [];
  for (let index = 1; index < sections.length; index++) {
    const section = sections[index];
    if (section.startsWith('--')) break;
    const text = section.replace(/^\r?\n/, '');
    if (text.trim() === '') continue;
    const [outer, rest] = splitHead(text);
    const [head, requestBody] = splitHead(rest);
    const [requestLine, ...headerLines] = head.split(/\r?\n/);
    const line = /^([A-Z]+)\s+(\S+)(?:\s+HTTP\/\d(?:\.\d)?)?$/.exec(requestLine.trim());
    if (!line) throw new BatchFormatError(`Batch part ${index} has an invalid request line.`);
    const url = new URL(line[2], 'https://admin.googleapis.com');
    parts.push({
      contentId: (parseHeaders(outer)['content-id'] ?? '').replace(/^<|>$/g, '') || null,
      method: line[1],
      host: url.hostname,
      path: url.pathname,
      query: url.searchParams,
      headers: parseHeaders(headerLines.join('\n')),
      body: requestBody.replace(/\r?\n$/, ''),
    });
  }
  return parts;
}

/** Build the multipart/mixed response that Google returns for a batch. */
export function buildBatchResponse(
  results: readonly BatchPartResult[],
  boundary: string,
): string {
  return (
    results
      .map((result) =>
        [
          `--${boundary}`,
          'Content-Type: application/http',
          ...(result.contentId !== null ? [`Content-ID: <response-${result.contentId}>`] : []),
          '',
          `HTTP/1.1 ${result.status} ${STATUS_CODES[result.status] ?? 'Unknown'}`,
          ...Object.entries(result.headers).map(([name, value]) => `${name}: ${value}`),
          '',
          result.body,
          '',
        ].join('\r\n'),
      )
      .join('') + `--${boundary}--\r\n`
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the batch handler**

`src/batch/batch.ts`:

```ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { directoryError } from '../http/errors.ts';
import type { ApiResponse, Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import {
  BatchFormatError,
  buildBatchResponse,
  parseBatchRequest,
  type BatchPart,
  type BatchPartResult,
} from './multipart.ts';

export const MAX_BATCH_PARTS = 1000;
/** Parts that run at the same time inside one batch. */
export const BATCH_WAVE = 10;

export async function handleBatch(call: Call): Promise<ApiResponse> {
  const { request } = call;
  let parts: BatchPart[];
  try {
    parts = parseBatchRequest(request.headers['content-type'] ?? '', request.body);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof BatchFormatError)
      return directoryError(400, 'badRequest', error.message);
    throw error;
  }
  if (parts.length === 0)
    return directoryError(400, 'badRequest', 'The batch request holds no parts.');
  if (parts.length > MAX_BATCH_PARTS)
    return directoryError(
      400,
      'badRequest',
      `A batch request holds ${MAX_BATCH_PARTS} parts at most.`,
    );
  const results: BatchPartResult[] = new Array(parts.length);
  for (let start = 0; start < parts.length; start += BATCH_WAVE)
    await Promise.all(
      parts.slice(start, start + BATCH_WAVE).map(async (part, offset) => {
        const authorization = part.headers.authorization ?? request.headers.authorization;
        const response = await call.dispatchPart({
          method: part.method,
          host: 'admin.googleapis.com',
          path: part.path,
          query: part.query,
          headers: { ...part.headers, ...(authorization ? { authorization } : {}) },
          body: part.body,
        });
        results[start + offset] = {
          contentId: part.contentId,
          status: response.status,
          headers: {
            'Content-Type':
              response.headers?.['content-type'] ?? 'application/json; charset=UTF-8',
          },
          body: response.raw ?? '',
        };
      }),
    );
  const boundary = `batch_${randomBytes(12).toString('base64url')}`;
  return {
    status: 200,
    items: parts.length,
    headers: { 'content-type': `multipart/mixed; boundary=${boundary}` },
    raw: buildBatchResponse(results, boundary),
  };
}
```

`src/batch/batch.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../auth/scopes.ts';
import { rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { grantToken, seedTestDomain, startHarness } from '../testing/harness.ts';

interface Part {
  contentId: string | undefined;
  status: number;
  body: string;
}

function parseResponse(contentType: string, text: string): Part[] {
  const boundary = /boundary=([^;\s]+)/.exec(contentType)![1];
  return text
    .split(`--${boundary}`)
    .slice(1)
    .filter((section) => !section.startsWith('--'))
    .map((section) => {
      const body = section.replace(/^\r\n/, '');
      const outerEnd = body.indexOf('\r\n\r\n');
      const outer = body.slice(0, outerEnd);
      const rest = body.slice(outerEnd + 4);
      const innerEnd = rest.indexOf('\r\n\r\n');
      return {
        contentId: /Content-ID: <([^>]+)>/.exec(outer)?.[1],
        status: Number(/^HTTP\/1\.1 (\d{3})/.exec(rest)![1]),
        body: rest.slice(innerEnd + 4).replace(/\r\n$/, ''),
      };
    });
}

function body(parts: { id: string; line: string; auth?: string }[]): string {
  return (
    parts
      .map(({ id, line, auth }) =>
        ['--b', 'Content-Type: application/http', `Content-ID: <${id}>`, '', line, ...(auth ? [`Authorization: Bearer ${auth}`] : []), '', ''].join('\r\n'),
      )
      .join('') + '--b--\r\n'
  );
}

async function setup(t: import('node:test').TestContext) {
  const harness = await startHarness();
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness);
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const ids = rows<{ device_id: string }>(domain.db.prepare('SELECT device_id FROM devices LIMIT 2').all()).map((r) => r.device_id);
  const send = (text: string, options: { host?: string; path?: string; bearer?: string; contentType?: string } = {}) =>
    harness.request({
      host: options.host ?? 'www.googleapis.com',
      path: options.path ?? '/batch/admin/directory_v1',
      method: 'POST',
      token: options.bearer,
      headers: { 'content-type': options.contentType ?? 'multipart/mixed; boundary=b' },
      body: text,
    });
  return { harness, domain, token, ids, send };
}

test('a batch answers each part with its own status and echoed Content-ID', async (t) => {
  const { token, ids, send, domain } = await setup(t);
  const devices = `/admin/directory/v1/customer/${domain.customerId}/devices/chromeos`;
  const response = await send(
    body([
      { id: 'cc-1', line: `GET ${devices}/${ids[0]}` },
      { id: 'cc-2', line: `GET ${devices}/${ids[1]}?fields=deviceId` },
      { id: 'cc-3', line: `GET ${devices}/missing` },
    ]),
    { bearer: token },
  );
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  const parts = parseResponse(String(response.headers['content-type']), response.text);
  assert.deepEqual(parts.map((part) => [part.contentId, part.status]), [
    ['response-cc-1', 200],
    ['response-cc-2', 200],
    ['response-cc-3', 404],
  ]);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(parts[0].body).deviceId, ids[0]);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(JSON.parse(parts[1].body)), ['deviceId']);
});

test('a part uses its own token before the outer token', async (t) => {
  const { token, send } = await setup(t);
  const response = await send(
    body([
      { id: 'a', line: 'GET /admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits' },
      { id: 'b', line: 'GET /admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits', auth: token },
    ]),
  );
  const parts = parseResponse(String(response.headers['content-type']), response.text);
  assert.deepEqual(parts.map((part) => part.status), [401, 200]);
});

test('the admin host and the short batch path also work', async (t) => {
  const { token, send } = await setup(t);
  const response = await send(body([{ id: 'x', line: 'GET /admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits' }]), {
    host: 'admin.googleapis.com',
    path: '/batch',
    bearer: token,
  });
  assert.equal(parseResponse(String(response.headers['content-type']), response.text)[0].status, 200);
});

test('oversized and malformed batches fail with 400', async (t) => {
  const { token, send } = await setup(t);
  const many = body(Array.from({ length: 1001 }, (_, i) => ({ id: `p${i}`, line: 'GET /admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits' })));
  assert.equal((await send(many, { bearer: token })).status, 400);
  assert.equal((await send('{}', { bearer: token, contentType: 'application/json' })).status, 400);
});
```

### Task 15: Quota buckets and concurrency caps

**Files:**
- Create: `src/quota/bucket.ts`, `src/quota/limits.ts`
- Modify: `src/mock.ts`
- Test: `src/quota/bucket.test.ts`, `src/quota/limits.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DomainSettings` (Task 4), `Call`, `Admission` (Task 7), errors (Tasks 7 and 8).
- Produces: `QuotaRules`, `MinuteClass = 'over' | 'heavy'`, `BucketSnapshot`, `QuotaBucket` with `admit(now): boolean`, `retryAfterMs(now): number`, `snapshot(now): BucketSnapshot`, and readonly `rules` and `minuteMs`.
- Produces: `QuotaReport`, `Limits { admit(call): Admission; inFlight(customerId): number; report(customerId): QuotaReport; reset(): void }`, `createLimits(deps: { clock: Clock; timing: Timing }): Limits`.
- Changes: `Mock` gains `readonly limits: Limits`. The default pipeline's `admit` calls `limits.admit`.

The rules come from the owner decision D4 in the spec:

1. Unflagged, a minute admits requests until its count reaches `floor(perMinute * ceiling)`.
2. When a minute closes, it is `over` above `perMinute` and `heavy` above `perMinute * heavyRatio`. A heavy minute is also an over minute.
3. The bucket is flagged when the closed minute and the 59 before it hold `overMinutesToFlag` over minutes or `heavyMinutesToFlag` heavy minutes.
4. A flag lasts `flagMinutes` from the end of the minute that triggered it. A flagged minute admits until its count reaches `perMinute`.
5. A minute closes when the first request of a later minute arrives. Minutes without requests are normal.

Rejected requests do not count. A bucket key is `api|customerId|projectId|subject`. A bucket is rebuilt when its rules or its minute length change. A time-scale change therefore starts fresh buckets.

`src/quota/bucket.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { QuotaBucket, type QuotaRules } from './bucket.ts';

const RULES: QuotaRules = {
  perMinute: 100,
  ceiling: 1.25,
  heavyRatio: 1.125,
  overMinutesToFlag: 30,
  heavyMinutesToFlag: 10,
  flagMinutes: 60,
};
const MINUTE = 1000;

/** Send `attempts` requests inside one minute and return how many it admits. */
function drive(bucket: QuotaBucket, minute: number, attempts: number): number {
  let admitted = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < attempts; i++)
    if (bucket.admit(minute * MINUTE + (i % (MINUTE - 1)))) admitted++;
  return admitted;
}

const fresh = () => new QuotaBucket(RULES, MINUTE);

test('an unflagged minute admits up to the 125% ceiling', () => {
  assert.equal(drive(fresh(), 0, 200), 125);
});

test('short bursts once or twice an hour cause no flag', () => {
  const bucket = fresh();
  let minute = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) assert.equal(drive(bucket, minute++, 110), 110);
  for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) drive(bucket, minute++, 100);
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) assert.equal(drive(bucket, minute++, 110), 110);
  assert.equal(bucket.snapshot(minute * MINUTE).flagged, false);
});

test('30 over minutes flag the bucket for 60 minutes, then admission recovers', () => {
  const bucket = fresh();
  for (let minute = 0; minute < 30; minute++) assert.equal(drive(bucket, minute, 110), 110);
  assert.equal(drive(bucket, 30, 110), 100, 'The flag caps the next minute at 100%.');
  const snapshot = bucket.snapshot(30 * MINUTE + 500);
  assert.equal(snapshot.flagged, true);
  assert.equal(snapshot.flaggedUntil, 90 * MINUTE);
  assert.equal(snapshot.limit, 100);
  assert.equal(drive(bucket, 89, 200), 100);
  assert.equal(drive(bucket, 90, 200), 125, 'The flag ends after 60 minutes.');
  assert.equal(bucket.snapshot(91 * MINUTE).flagged, false);
});

test('10 heavy minutes flag the bucket and 9 do not', () => {
  const bucket = fresh();
  for (let minute = 0; minute < 9; minute++) drive(bucket, minute, 120);
  drive(bucket, 9, 1);
  assert.equal(bucket.snapshot(9 * MINUTE + 1).flagged, false);
  const second = fresh();
  for (let minute = 0; minute < 10; minute++) drive(second, minute, 120);
  drive(second, 10, 1);
  assert.equal(second.snapshot(10 * MINUTE + 1).flagged, true);
});

test('over minutes older than 60 minutes drop out of the count', () => {
  const bucket = fresh();
  for (let minute = 0; minute < 29; minute++) drive(bucket, minute, 110);
  for (let minute = 29; minute < 60; minute++) drive(bucket, minute, 50);
  drive(bucket, 60, 110);
  drive(bucket, 61, 1);
  const snapshot = bucket.snapshot(61 * MINUTE + 1);
  assert.equal(snapshot.flagged, false);
  assert.equal(snapshot.overMinutes, 29);
});

test('retryAfterMs reports the time to the next minute', () => {
  const bucket = fresh();
  bucket.admit(5 * MINUTE + 300);
  assert.equal(bucket.retryAfterMs(5 * MINUTE + 300), 700);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the bucket**

`src/quota/bucket.ts`:

```ts
export interface QuotaRules {
  perMinute: number;
  ceiling: number;
  heavyRatio: number;
  overMinutesToFlag: number;
  heavyMinutesToFlag: number;
  flagMinutes: number;
}

export type MinuteClass = 'over' | 'heavy';

export interface BucketSnapshot {
  windowStart: number;
  count: number;
  limit: number;
  flagged: boolean;
  flaggedUntil: number | null;
  overMinutes: number;
  heavyMinutes: number;
  history: { start: number; class: MinuteClass }[];
}

/** One per-minute quota with the owner's overage and flag rules. */
export class QuotaBucket {
  readonly rules: QuotaRules;
  readonly minuteMs: number;
  #windowStart = Number.NaN;
  #count = 0;
  #history: { start: number; class: MinuteClass }[] = [];
  #flaggedUntil: number | null = null;

  constructor(rules: QuotaRules, minuteMs: number) {
    this.rules = rules;
    this.minuteMs = minuteMs;
  }

  #roll(now: number): void {
    const start = Math.floor(now / this.minuteMs) * this.minuteMs;
    if (start === this.#windowStart) return;
    if (this.#count > 0) this.#close(this.#windowStart);
    this.#windowStart = start;
    this.#count = 0;
    if (this.#flaggedUntil !== null && now >= this.#flaggedUntil)
      this.#flaggedUntil = null;
  }

  #close(windowStart: number): void {
    const { perMinute, heavyRatio } = this.rules;
    const kind: MinuteClass | null =
      this.#count > perMinute * heavyRatio
        ? 'heavy'
        : this.#count > perMinute
          ? 'over'
          : null;
    if (kind) this.#history.push({ start: windowStart, class: kind });
    const horizon = windowStart - 59 * this.minuteMs;
    this.#history = this.#history.filter((entry) => entry.start >= horizon);
    if (this.#flaggedUntil !== null) return;
    const heavy = this.#history.filter((entry) => entry.class === 'heavy').length;
    if (
      this.#history.length >= this.rules.overMinutesToFlag ||
      heavy >= this.rules.heavyMinutesToFlag
    )
      this.#flaggedUntil =
        windowStart + this.minuteMs + this.rules.flagMinutes * this.minuteMs;
  }

  #flagged(now: number): boolean {
    return this.#flaggedUntil !== null && now < this.#flaggedUntil;
  }

  #limit(now: number): number {
    return this.#flagged(now)
      ? this.rules.perMinute
      : Math.floor(this.rules.perMinute * this.rules.ceiling);
  }

  admit(now: number): boolean {
    this.#roll(now);
    if (this.#count >= this.#limit(now)) return false;
    this.#count++;
    return true;
  }

  retryAfterMs(now: number): number {
    this.#roll(now);
    return this.#windowStart + this.minuteMs - now;
  }

  snapshot(now: number): BucketSnapshot {
    this.#roll(now);
    return {
      windowStart: this.#windowStart,
      count: this.#count,
      limit: this.#limit(now),
      flagged: this.#flagged(now),
      flaggedUntil: this.#flagged(now) ? this.#flaggedUntil : null,
      overMinutes: this.#history.length,
      heavyMinutes: this.#history.filter((entry) => entry.class === 'heavy').length,
      history: [...this.#history],
    };
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement the limits service**

`src/quota/limits.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, Timing } from '../clock.ts';
import { directoryError, rpcError } from '../http/errors.ts';
import type { Api, ApiResponse, Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import type { Admission } from '../pipeline.ts';
import { QuotaBucket, type BucketSnapshot } from './bucket.ts';

export interface QuotaReport {
  buckets: {
    api: string;
    projectId: string;
    subject: string;
    snapshot: BucketSnapshot;
  }[];
  inFlight: { total: number; byMethod: Record<string, number> };
}

export interface Limits {
  admit(call: Call): Admission;
  inFlight(customerId: string): number;
  report(customerId: string): QuotaReport;
  reset(): void;
}

const SERVICE: Record<string, string> = {
  directory: 'admin.googleapis.com',
  chrome: 'chromemanagement.googleapis.com',
};

function quotaError(api: Api, retryAfterMs: number | null): ApiResponse {
  const message = `Quota exceeded for quota metric 'Queries' and limit 'Queries per minute per user' of service '${SERVICE[api]}'.`;
  const response =
    api === 'chrome'
      ? rpcError(429, 'RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED', message)
      : directoryError(403, 'userRateLimitExceeded', message, { domain: 'usageLimits' });
  return retryAfterMs === null
    ? response
    : {
        ...response,
        headers: { 'retry-after': String(Math.max(1, Math.ceil(retryAfterMs / 1000))) },
      };
}

function concurrencyError(api: Api, scope: 'method' | 'customer'): ApiResponse {
  if (api === 'chrome')
    return rpcError(429, 'RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED', 'Too many concurrent requests.');
  return scope === 'method'
    ? directoryError(403, 'quotaExceeded', 'Quota exceeded.', { domain: 'usageLimits' })
    : directoryError(429, 'rateLimitExceeded', 'Rate Limit Exceeded', {
        domain: 'usageLimits',
      });
}

interface Entry {
  bucket: QuotaBucket;
  signature: string;
  customerId: string;
  api: string;
  projectId: string;
  subject: string;
}

/** Quota buckets per project and subject, plus in-flight caps per customer. */
export function createLimits(deps: { clock: Clock; timing: Timing }): Limits {
  const buckets = new Map<string, Entry>();
  const inFlight = new Map<string, number>();
  const bump = (key: string, delta: number) => {
    const next = (inFlight.get(key) ?? 0) + delta;
    if (next <= 0) inFlight.delete(key);
    else inFlight.set(key, next);
  };
  return {
    admit(call) {
      const { access, domain, route } = call;
      if (!access || !domain || (route.api !== 'directory' && route.api !== 'chrome'))
        return { ok: true, release() {} };
      const settings = domain.record.settings;
      const customerKey = access.customerId;
      const methodKey = `${access.customerId}|${route.id}`;
      if ((inFlight.get(customerKey) ?? 0) >= settings.concurrency.perCustomer)
        return { ok: false, response: concurrencyError(route.api, 'customer') };
      if ((inFlight.get(methodKey) ?? 0) >= settings.concurrency.perMethod)
        return { ok: false, response: concurrencyError(route.api, 'method') };
      const rules = route.api === 'chrome' ? settings.chromeQuota : settings.quota;
      const minuteMs = 60_000 / deps.timing.scale;
      const key = `${route.api}|${access.customerId}|${access.token.projectId}|${access.subject}`;
      const signature = JSON.stringify([rules, minuteMs]);
      let entry = buckets.get(key);
      if (!entry || entry.signature !== signature) {
        entry = {
          bucket: new QuotaBucket(rules, minuteMs),
          signature,
          customerId: access.customerId,
          api: route.api,
          projectId: access.token.projectId,
          subject: access.subject,
        };
        buckets.set(key, entry);
      }
      const now = deps.clock.now();
      if (!entry.bucket.admit(now))
        return {
          ok: false,
          response: quotaError(
            route.api,
            rules.retryAfter ? entry.bucket.retryAfterMs(now) : null,
          ),
        };
      bump(customerKey, 1);
      bump(methodKey, 1);
      let released = false;
      return {
        ok: true,
        release() {
          if (released) return;
          released = true;
          bump(customerKey, -1);
          bump(methodKey, -1);
        },
      };
    },
    inFlight: (customerId) => inFlight.get(customerId) ?? 0,
    report(customerId) {
      const now = deps.clock.now();
      const byMethod: Record<string, number> = {};
      for (const [key, count] of inFlight)
        if (key.startsWith(`${customerId}|`)) byMethod[key.slice(customerId.length + 1)] = count;
      return {
        buckets: [...buckets.values()]
          .filter((entry) => entry.customerId === customerId)
          .map((entry) => ({
            api: entry.api,
            projectId: entry.projectId,
            subject: entry.subject,
            snapshot: entry.bucket.snapshot(now),
          })),
        inFlight: { total: inFlight.get(customerId) ?? 0, byMethod },
      };
    },
    reset() {
      buckets.clear();
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../auth/scopes.ts';
import { fakeClock } from '../clock.ts';
import type { Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import { grantToken, seedTestDomain, startHarness } from '../testing/harness.ts';
import { createLimits } from './limits.ts';

async function setup(t: import('node:test').TestContext, perMinute = 4) {
  const clock = fakeClock();
  const harness = await startHarness({ clock });
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness);
  harness.mock.domains.updateSettings(domain.customerId, {
    quota: { perMinute },
    chromeQuota: { perMinute },
  });
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const orgunits = () =>
    harness.request({
      host: 'admin.googleapis.com',
      path: '/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits',
      token,
    });
  return { clock, harness, domain, token, orgunits };
}

test('a Directory bucket rejects with 403 userRateLimitExceeded above the ceiling', async (t) => {
  const { orgunits, clock } = await setup(t);
  const statuses: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) statuses.push((await orgunits()).status);
  assert.deepEqual(statuses, [200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 403]);
  const rejected = await orgunits();
  assert.equal(rejected.json().error.errors[0].reason, 'userRateLimitExceeded');
  assert.equal(rejected.json().error.errors[0].domain, 'usageLimits');
  assert.equal(rejected.headers['retry-after'], undefined);
  await clock.advance(60_000);
  assert.equal((await orgunits()).status, 200, 'A new minute admits again.');
});

test('Retry-After appears when the setting enables it', async (t) => {
  const { orgunits, harness, domain } = await setup(t);
  harness.mock.domains.updateSettings(domain.customerId, { quota: { retryAfter: true } });
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) await orgunits();
  const rejected = await orgunits();
  assert.equal(rejected.status, 403);
  assert.ok(Number(rejected.headers['retry-after']) >= 1);
});

test('Chrome Management rejects with 429 RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED', async (t) => {
  const { harness, token } = await setup(t);
  const statuses: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++)
    statuses.push(
      (
        await harness.request({
          host: 'chromemanagement.googleapis.com',
          path: '/v1/customers/my_customer/telemetry/devices',
          query: { readMask: 'deviceId' },
          token,
        })
      ).status,
    );
  assert.deepEqual(statuses, [200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 429]);
});

test('each batch part counts against the quota', async (t) => {
  const { harness, token } = await setup(t);
  const part = (id: number) =>
    `--b\r\nContent-Type: application/http\r\nContent-ID: <p${id}>\r\n\r\nGET /admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits\r\n\r\n`;
  const response = await harness.request({
    host: 'www.googleapis.com',
    path: '/batch/admin/directory_v1',
    method: 'POST',
    token,
    headers: { 'content-type': 'multipart/mixed; boundary=b' },
    body: Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => part(i)).join('') + '--b--\r\n',
  });
  const statuses = [...response.text.matchAll(/HTTP\/1\.1 (\d{3})/g)].map((match) => Number(match[1]));
  assert.deepEqual(statuses, [200, 200, 200, 200, 200, 403, 403, 403]);
});

test('the report shows the bucket and its count', async (t) => {
  const { orgunits, harness, domain } = await setup(t);
  await orgunits();
  const report = harness.mock.limits.report(domain.customerId);
  assert.equal(report.buckets.length, 1);
  assert.equal(report.buckets[0].snapshot.count, 1);
  assert.equal(report.buckets[0].snapshot.limit, 5);
});

test('concurrency caps reject in-flight requests until release', async (t) => {
  const { harness, domain, token } = await setup(t, 1000);
  harness.mock.domains.updateSettings(domain.customerId, {
    concurrency: { perMethod: 1, perCustomer: 2 },
  });
  const call = (id: string) =>
    ({
      route: { id, api: 'directory' },
      access: {
        customerId: domain.customerId,
        subject: 'admin',
        token: { projectId: 'p', token },
      },
      domain: harness.mock.domains.get(domain.customerId),
    }) as unknown as Call;
  const limits = createLimits({ clock: harness.mock.clock, timing: harness.mock.timing });
  const first = limits.admit(call('m1'));
  assert.ok(first.ok);
  const sameMethod = limits.admit(call('m1'));
  assert.equal(!sameMethod.ok && sameMethod.response.status, 403);
  const second = limits.admit(call('m2'));
  assert.ok(second.ok);
  const thirdMethod = limits.admit(call('m3'));
  assert.equal(!thirdMethod.ok && thirdMethod.response.status, 429);
  if (first.ok) first.release();
  assert.ok(limits.admit(call('m1')).ok);
  assert.equal(limits.inFlight(domain.customerId), 2);
});

test('a time-scale change starts fresh buckets', async (t) => {
  const { orgunits, harness, domain } = await setup(t);
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) await orgunits();
  assert.equal((await orgunits()).status, 403);
  harness.mock.timing.scale = 60;
  assert.equal((await orgunits()).status, 200);
  assert.equal(harness.mock.limits.report(domain.customerId).buckets[0].snapshot.count, 1);
});
```

### Task 16: Latency and time scale

**Files:**
- Create: `src/latency/model.ts`, `src/latency/delays.ts`
- Modify: `src/mock.ts`
- Test: `src/latency/model.test.ts`, `src/latency/delays.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LatencySettings`, `DEFAULT_SETTINGS` (Task 4), `Limits` (Task 15), `Random`, `Clock`, `Timing` (Task 1).
- Produces: `DelayInput { api; bytes; items; scanned; inFlight; parts }`, `computeDelayMs(input, settings, random): number`.
- Produces: `Delays { delay(call, response): Promise<void> }`, `createDelays(deps: { clock; timing; random; limits }): Delays`.
- Changes: the default pipeline's `delay` calls `delays.delay`.

The model returns unscaled milliseconds. `delay` divides by `timing.scale` before it sleeps. Batch parts skip the delay. The outer batch response pays one delay for all its parts.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing model tests**

`src/latency/model.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { createRandom } from '../random.ts';
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from '../settings.ts';
import { computeDelayMs, type DelayInput } from './model.ts';

const settings = DEFAULT_SETTINGS.latency;
const samples = (input: DelayInput, count = 500) => {
  const random = createRandom('latency');
  return Array.from({ length: count }, () => computeDelayMs(input, settings, random)).sort(
    (a, b) => a - b,
  );
};
const median = (values: number[]) => values[Math.floor(values.length / 2)];
const base: DelayInput = { api: 'directory', bytes: 0, items: 0, scanned: 0, inFlight: 0, parts: 0 };

test('a full 300-device page lands between 1 and 2 seconds', () => {
  const values = samples({ ...base, bytes: 1_100_000, items: 300 });
  assert.ok(values[0] >= 1000, `min ${values[0]}`);
  assert.ok(values.at(-1)! <= 2000, `max ${values.at(-1)}`);
});

test('a single get has a median from 150 to 300 ms', () => {
  const value = median(samples({ ...base, bytes: 4_000, items: 1 }));
  assert.ok(value >= 150 && value <= 300, `median ${value}`);
});

test('the cap holds without load', () => {
  const values = samples({ ...base, bytes: 50_000_000, items: 10_000 });
  assert.equal(values.at(-1), 2000);
});

test('load above the threshold multiplies the delay', () => {
  const quiet = computeDelayMs({ ...base, items: 1 }, settings, createRandom('x'));
  const busy = computeDelayMs({ ...base, items: 1, inFlight: 70 }, settings, createRandom('x'));
  assert.ok(Math.abs(busy / quiet - 2) < 1e-9, `ratio ${busy / quiet}`);
});

test('a batch pays once per wave of parts', () => {
  const ten = computeDelayMs({ ...base, api: 'batch', parts: 10 }, settings, createRandom('y'));
  const twenty = computeDelayMs({ ...base, api: 'batch', parts: 20 }, settings, createRandom('y'));
  assert.ok(Math.abs(twenty - ten - settings.batchPartMs) < 1e-9);
});

test('the same random stream gives the same delays', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(samples({ ...base, items: 5 }, 20), samples({ ...base, items: 5 }, 20));
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the model**

`src/latency/model.ts`:

```ts
import type { Api } from '../http/types.ts';
import type { Random } from '../random.ts';
import type { LatencySettings } from '../settings.ts';

export interface DelayInput {
  api: Api;
  bytes: number;
  items: number;
  scanned: number;
  inFlight: number;
  parts: number;
}

/** z for the 99th percentile of a standard normal distribution. */
const Z99 = 2.326;

/** The modeled response time in unscaled milliseconds. */
export function computeDelayMs(
  input: DelayInput,
  settings: LatencySettings,
  random: Random,
): number {
  const sigma = Math.log(settings.roundTripP99Ms / settings.roundTripMedianMs) / Z99;
  const roundTrip = Math.min(
    settings.roundTripMedianMs * Math.exp(sigma * random.normal()),
    settings.roundTripP99Ms * 2,
  );
  const transfer = (input.bytes / settings.bytesPerSecond) * 1000;
  const compute =
    input.api === 'batch'
      ? settings.baseMs +
        Math.ceil(input.parts / settings.batchParallelism) * settings.batchPartMs
      : input.api === 'oauth'
        ? 30
        : settings.baseMs +
          settings.perItemMs * input.items +
          (settings.scanPer10kRowsMs * input.scanned) / 10_000;
  const base = Math.min(roundTrip + transfer + compute, settings.capMs);
  const load =
    1 +
    (Math.max(0, input.inFlight - settings.loadThreshold) *
      settings.loadPercentPerRequest) /
      100;
  return base * load;
}
```

Run: `node --test src/latency/model.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the delay stage**

`src/latency/delays.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, Timing } from '../clock.ts';
import type { ApiResponse, Call } from '../http/types.ts';
import type { Limits } from '../quota/limits.ts';
import type { Random } from '../random.ts';
import { DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from '../settings.ts';
import { computeDelayMs } from './model.ts';

export interface Delays {
  delay(call: Call, response: ApiResponse): Promise<void>;
}

export function createDelays(deps: {
  clock: Clock;
  timing: Timing;
  random: Random;
  limits: Limits;
}): Delays {
  return {
    async delay(call, response) {
      if (!deps.timing.delays || call.batchPart) return;
      const settings = call.domain?.record.settings.latency ?? DEFAULT_SETTINGS.latency;
      const ms = computeDelayMs(
        {
          api: call.route.api,
          bytes: Buffer.byteLength(response.raw ?? ''),
          items: response.items ?? 0,
          scanned: response.scanned ?? 0,
          inFlight: call.access ? deps.limits.inFlight(call.access.customerId) : 0,
          parts: call.route.api === 'batch' ? (response.items ?? 0) : 0,
        },
        settings,
        deps.random,
      );
      await deps.clock.sleep(ms / deps.timing.scale);
    },
  };
}
```

`src/latency/delays.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { setImmediate as tick } from 'node:timers/promises';
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../auth/scopes.ts';
import { fakeClock } from '../clock.ts';
import { grantToken, seedTestDomain, startHarness } from '../testing/harness.ts';

async function waitForSleeper(clock: ReturnType<typeof fakeClock>) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 1000 && clock.pending() === 0; i++) await tick();
  assert.equal(clock.pending(), 1, 'The response waits on one delay.');
}

test('a response waits for the modeled delay', async (t) => {
  const clock = fakeClock();
  const harness = await startHarness({ clock, timing: { scale: 1, delays: true } });
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness);
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  let done = false;
  const pending = harness
    .request({ host: 'admin.googleapis.com', path: '/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits', token })
    .then((response) => {
      done = true;
      return response;
    });
  await waitForSleeper(clock);
  await clock.advance(100);
  await tick();
  assert.equal(done, false, 'The delay exceeds 100 ms.');
  await clock.advance(2000);
  assert.equal((await pending).status, 200);
});

test('the time scale divides the delay', async (t) => {
  const clock = fakeClock();
  const harness = await startHarness({ clock, timing: { scale: 100, delays: true } });
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness);
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const pending = harness.request({
    host: 'admin.googleapis.com',
    path: '/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits',
    token,
  });
  await waitForSleeper(clock);
  await clock.advance(21);
  assert.equal((await pending).status, 200, 'A capped 2 s delay at scale 100 ends within 21 ms.');
});

test('no delay applies when delays are off', async (t) => {
  const clock = fakeClock();
  const harness = await startHarness({ clock, timing: { scale: 1, delays: false } });
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness);
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const response = await harness.request({
    host: 'admin.googleapis.com',
    path: '/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits',
    token,
  });
  assert.equal(response.status, 200);
  assert.equal(clock.pending(), 0);
});
```

### Task 17: Fault injection

**Files:**
- Create: `src/faults/faults.ts`
- Modify: `src/mock.ts`
- Test: `src/faults/faults.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Overrides`, `Call`, `ApiResponse` (Task 7), errors (Tasks 7 and 8), `Clock`, `Timing`, `Random` (Task 1).
- Produces: `FaultRule` (the union below), `FaultError`, `StoredFault { id; rule; remaining: number | null }`, `Faults extends Overrides { add(customerId, input): string; list(customerId): StoredFault[]; clear(customerId): void; before(call): Promise<ApiResponse | 'reset' | undefined> }`, `createFaults(deps: { clock; timing; random }): Faults`.
- Changes: `Mock` gains `readonly faults: Faults`. `mock.overrides` starts as `faults`. The default pipeline's `before` calls `faults.before`.

```ts
type FaultRule =
  | { kind: 'error-rate'; method?: string; rate: number }
  | { kind: 'status'; method?: string; status: number; reason: string; count: number }
  | { kind: 'reset'; method?: string; count: number }
  | { kind: 'hang'; method?: string; ms: number; count: number }
  | { kind: 'revoke-delegation'; clientId: string }
  | { kind: 'remove-scope'; clientId: string; scope: string }
  | { kind: 'demote-subject'; subject: string }
  | { kind: 'remove-entity'; entityId: string };
```

`method` is a route ID, for example `directory.chromeosdevices.list`. The last four kinds change state through the `Overrides` interface. Clearing the faults restores the stored state. A `hang` waits `ms / timing.scale`, then the request continues.

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { setImmediate as tick } from 'node:timers/promises';
import { issueServiceAccount } from '../auth/accounts.ts';
import { READ_SCOPES, SCOPES } from '../auth/scopes.ts';
import { fakeClock } from '../clock.ts';
import { rows } from '../store/sqlite.ts';
import { adminOf, grantToken, seedTestDomain, startHarness } from '../testing/harness.ts';
import { signAssertion, tokenRequestBody } from '../testing/jwt.ts';
import { FaultError } from './faults.ts';

const ORGUNITS = '/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits';

async function setup(t: import('node:test').TestContext, clock = fakeClock()) {
  const harness = await startHarness({ clock });
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness);
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const get = (path = ORGUNITS, host = 'admin.googleapis.com', query?: Record<string, string>) =>
    harness.request({ host, path, token, query });
  const add = (rule: unknown) => harness.mock.faults.add(domain.customerId, rule);
  return { clock, harness, domain, token, get, add };
}

test('a status fault answers the next N matching calls', async (t) => {
  const { get, add } = await setup(t);
  add({ kind: 'status', method: 'directory.orgunits.list', status: 503, reason: 'backendError', count: 2 });
  assert.equal((await get('/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/domains')).status, 200);
  const first = await get();
  assert.equal(first.status, 503);
  assert.equal(first.json().error.errors[0].reason, 'backendError');
  assert.equal((await get()).status, 503);
  assert.equal((await get()).status, 200);
});

test('a Chrome status fault uses the RPC envelope', async (t) => {
  const { get, add } = await setup(t);
  add({ kind: 'status', status: 429, reason: 'RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED', count: 1 });
  const response = await get('/v1/customers/my_customer/telemetry/devices', 'chromemanagement.googleapis.com', { readMask: 'deviceId' });
  assert.equal(response.status, 429);
  assert.equal(response.json().error.status, 'RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED');
});

test('a reset fault closes the connection once', async (t) => {
  const { get, add } = await setup(t);
  add({ kind: 'reset', count: 1 });
  await assert.rejects(get(), /socket hang up|ECONNRESET/);
  assert.equal((await get()).status, 200);
});

test('a hang fault holds the response for the scaled duration', async (t) => {
  const { get, add, clock } = await setup(t);
  add({ kind: 'hang', ms: 5000, count: 1 });
  let done = false;
  const pending = get().then((response) => {
    done = true;
    return response;
  });
  for (let i = 0; i < 1000 && clock.pending() === 0; i++) await tick();
  await clock.advance(4000);
  await tick();
  assert.equal(done, false);
  await clock.advance(1000);
  assert.equal((await pending).status, 200);
});

test('an error rate of 1 always fails and 0 never fails', async (t) => {
  const { get, add, harness, domain } = await setup(t);
  add({ kind: 'error-rate', rate: 1 });
  assert.equal((await get()).status, 503);
  harness.mock.faults.clear(domain.customerId);
  add({ kind: 'error-rate', rate: 0 });
  assert.equal((await get()).status, 200);
});

test('revoked delegation and removed scopes fail the token exchange until cleared', async (t) => {
  const { harness, domain, add } = await setup(t, undefined);
  const account = issueServiceAccount(harness.mock.registry, domain.customerId);
  harness.mock.registry.setGrant(domain.customerId, account.client_id, [SCOPES.orgunit[1], SCOPES.device[1]]);
  const exchange = (scope: string) =>
    harness.request({

      path: '/token',
      method: 'POST',
      body: tokenRequestBody(signAssertion(account, { scope, sub: adminOf(domain) }, harness.mock.clock.now())),

  assert.equal((await exchange(SCOPES.orgunit[1])).status, 200);
  assert.equal((await exchange(SCOPES.device[1])).json().error, 'unauthorized_client');
  add({ kind: 'revoke-delegation', clientId: account.client_id });
  assert.equal((await exchange(SCOPES.orgunit[1])).json().error, 'unauthorized_client');
  harness.mock.faults.clear(domain.customerId);
  assert.equal((await exchange(SCOPES.device[1])).status, 200);
});

test('a demoted subject loses administrator access', async (t) => {

  assert.equal(response.json().error.errors[0].reason, 'forbidden');
});

  const id = rows<{ device_id: string }>(domain.db.prepare('SELECT device_id FROM devices LIMIT 1').all())[0].device_id;
  add({ kind: 'remove-entity', entityId: id });

    token,

});

test('invalid rules fail and the list shows stored rules', async (t) => {
  const { harness, domain, add } = await setup(t);
  for (const rule of [
    null,
    { kind: 'meteor' },
    { kind: 'status', status: 200, reason: 'x', count: 1 },
    { kind: 'status', status: 503, reason: '', count: 1 },
    { kind: 'error-rate', rate: 2 },
    { kind: 'hang', ms: 0, count: 1 },
    { kind: 'reset', count: 0 },
    { kind: 'revoke-delegation', clientId: 'abc' },
    { kind: 'remove-scope', clientId: '1', scope: 'scope' },
    { kind: 'status', status: 503, reason: 'x', count: 1, extra: true },
  ])
    assert.throws(() => add(rule), FaultError, JSON.stringify(rule));
  const id = add({ kind: 'reset', count: 3 });
  assert.deepEqual(harness.mock.faults.list(domain.customerId), [
    { id, rule: { kind: 'reset', count: 3 }, remaining: 3 },
  ]);
  harness.mock.faults.clear(domain.customerId);
  assert.deepEqual(harness.mock.faults.list(domain.customerId), []);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the faults**

`src/faults/faults.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Clock, Timing } from '../clock.ts';
import { directoryError, rpcError, transientError } from '../http/errors.ts';
import type { Api, ApiResponse, Call, Overrides } from '../http/types.ts';
import type { Random } from '../random.ts';

export type FaultRule =
  | { kind: 'error-rate'; method?: string; rate: number }
  | { kind: 'status'; method?: string; status: number; reason: string; count: number }
  | { kind: 'reset'; method?: string; count: number }
  | { kind: 'hang'; method?: string; ms: number; count: number }
  | { kind: 'revoke-delegation'; clientId: string }
  | { kind: 'remove-scope'; clientId: string; scope: string }
  | { kind: 'demote-subject'; subject: string }
  | { kind: 'remove-entity'; entityId: string };

export interface StoredFault {
  id: string;
  rule: FaultRule;
  remaining: number | null;
}

export class FaultError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'FaultError';
  }
}

export interface Faults extends Overrides {
  add(customerId: string, input: unknown): string;
  list(customerId: string): StoredFault[];
  clear(customerId: string): void;
  before(call: Call): Promise<ApiResponse | 'reset' | undefined>;
}

const FIELDS: Record<FaultRule['kind'], readonly string[]> = {
  'error-rate': ['kind', 'method', 'rate'],
  status: ['kind', 'method', 'status', 'reason', 'count'],
  reset: ['kind', 'method', 'count'],
  hang: ['kind', 'method', 'ms', 'count'],
  'revoke-delegation': ['kind', 'clientId'],
  'remove-scope': ['kind', 'clientId', 'scope'],
  'demote-subject': ['kind', 'subject'],
  'remove-entity': ['kind', 'entityId'],
};

const MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  backendError: 'Backend Error',
  quotaExceeded: 'Quota exceeded.',
  rateLimitExceeded: 'Rate Limit Exceeded',
  userRateLimitExceeded: 'User Rate Limit Exceeded',
  forbidden: 'Not Authorized to access this resource/api',
  notFound: 'Not Found',
};
const USAGE_LIMITS = new Set(['quotaExceeded', 'rateLimitExceeded', 'userRateLimitExceeded']);

function check(condition: boolean, message: string): void {
  if (!condition) throw new FaultError(message);
}

const wholeNumber = (value: unknown, min: number, max: number) =>
  typeof value === 'number' && Number.isInteger(value) && value >= min && value <= max;

function validate(input: unknown): FaultRule {
  check(input !== null && typeof input === 'object' && !Array.isArray(input), 'A fault must be an object.');
  const rule = input as Record<string, unknown>;
  const kind = rule.kind as FaultRule['kind'];
  check(typeof kind === 'string' && Object.hasOwn(FIELDS, kind), 'Unknown fault kind.');
  for (const key of Object.keys(rule))
    check(FIELDS[kind].includes(key), `Unknown fault field: ${key}.`);
  if ('method' in rule) check(typeof rule.method === 'string' && rule.method.length > 0, 'method must be a route ID.');
  if ('count' in rule || ['status', 'reset', 'hang'].includes(kind))
    check(wholeNumber(rule.count, 1, 1_000_000), 'count must be a whole number from 1 to 1000000.');
  switch (kind) {
    case 'error-rate':
      check(typeof rule.rate === 'number' && rule.rate >= 0 && rule.rate <= 1, 'rate must be from 0 to 1.');
      break;
    case 'status':
      check(wholeNumber(rule.status, 400, 599), 'status must be from 400 to 599.');
      check(typeof rule.reason === 'string' && rule.reason.length > 0 && rule.reason.length <= 64, 'reason must be 1 to 64 characters.');
      break;
    case 'hang':
      check(wholeNumber(rule.ms, 1, 600_000), 'ms must be a whole number from 1 to 600000.');
      break;
    case 'revoke-delegation':
    case 'remove-scope':
      check(typeof rule.clientId === 'string' && /^\d{1,32}$/.test(rule.clientId), 'clientId must be numeric.');
      if (kind === 'remove-scope')
        check(typeof rule.scope === 'string' && rule.scope.startsWith('https://www.googleapis.com/auth/'), 'scope must be a Google scope URL.');
      break;
    case 'demote-subject':
      check(typeof rule.subject === 'string' && rule.subject.includes('@'), 'subject must be an email address.');
      break;
    case 'remove-entity':
      check(typeof rule.entityId === 'string' && rule.entityId.length > 0 && rule.entityId.length <= 256, 'entityId must be 1 to 256 characters.');
      break;
  }
  return rule as unknown as FaultRule;
}

function statusResponse(api: Api, status: number, reason: string): ApiResponse {
  if (api === 'chrome') return rpcError(status, reason, `Injected fault: ${reason}`);
  return directoryError(status, reason, MESSAGES[reason] ?? reason, {
    domain: USAGE_LIMITS.has(reason) ? 'usageLimits' : 'global',
  });
}

export function createFaults(deps: { clock: Clock; timing: Timing; random: Random }): Faults {
  const store = new Map<string, StoredFault[]>();
  const rulesOf = (customerId: string) => store.get(customerId) ?? [];
  const ofKind = <K extends FaultRule['kind']>(customerId: string, kind: K) =>
    rulesOf(customerId)
      .map((fault) => fault.rule)
      .filter((rule): rule is Extract<FaultRule, { kind: K }> => rule.kind === kind);
  return {
    add(customerId, input) {
      const rule = validate(input);
      const id = randomUUID();
      const remaining = 'count' in rule ? rule.count : null;
      store.set(customerId, [...rulesOf(customerId), { id, rule, remaining }]);
      return id;
    },
    list: (customerId) => rulesOf(customerId).map((fault) => ({ ...fault })),
    clear(customerId) {
      store.delete(customerId);
    },
    async before(call) {
      const customerId = call.access?.customerId;
      if (!customerId) return undefined;
      for (const fault of rulesOf(customerId)) {
        const { rule } = fault;
        if (!('method' in rule || ['error-rate', 'status', 'reset', 'hang'].includes(rule.kind)))
          continue;
        if ('method' in rule && rule.method && rule.method !== call.route.id) continue;
        switch (rule.kind) {
          case 'status':
            if (fault.remaining! > 0) {
              fault.remaining!--;
              return statusResponse(call.route.api, rule.status, rule.reason);
            }
            break;
          case 'reset':
            if (fault.remaining! > 0) {
              fault.remaining!--;
              return 'reset';
            }
            break;
          case 'hang':
            if (fault.remaining! > 0) {
              fault.remaining!--;
              await deps.clock.sleep(rule.ms / deps.timing.scale);
            }
            break;
          case 'error-rate':
            if (deps.random.chance(rule.rate)) return transientError(call.route.api);
            break;
        }
      }
      return undefined;
    },
    grant(customerId, clientId, scopes) {
      if (ofKind(customerId, 'revoke-delegation').some((rule) => rule.clientId === clientId))
        return undefined;
      const removed = new Set(
        ofKind(customerId, 'remove-scope')
          .filter((rule) => rule.clientId === clientId)
          .map((rule) => rule.scope),
      );
      return scopes?.filter((scope) => !removed.has(scope));
    },
    isAdmin(customerId, email, isAdmin) {
      return (
        isAdmin &&
        !ofKind(customerId, 'demote-subject').some(
          (rule) => rule.subject.toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase(),
        )
      );
    },
    hidden(customerId) {
      return new Set(ofKind(customerId, 'remove-entity').map((rule) => rule.entityId));
    },
  };
}
```

### Task 18: Control API, entry point, and mockctl

**Files:**
- Create: `src/control/control.ts`, `src/main.ts`, `bin/mockctl.ts`
- Modify: `README.md`
- Test: `src/control/control.test.ts`, `src/control/mockctl.test.ts`, `src/main.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything before.
- Produces: `createControlHandler(mock: Mock): (request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse) => void`.
- Produces: the `src/main.ts` entry point. It reads `MOCK_DATA_DIR` (default `/data`), `MOCK_GOOGLE_PORT` (443), `MOCK_GOOGLE_HOST` (`0.0.0.0`), `MOCK_CONTROL_PORT` (8080), `MOCK_CONTROL_HOST` (`127.0.0.1`), `MOCK_TIME_SCALE` (1), `MOCK_DELAYS` (`on`), and `MOCK_CA_EXPORT` (optional file path). It prints one JSON line `{"event":"started","googlePort","controlPort","dataDir","timing"}`.
- Produces: the `mockctl` CLI. It reads `MOCK_CONTROL_URL` (default `http://127.0.0.1:8080`).

Control routes:

| Method and path | Body | Answer |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `GET /health` | none | `200 {"status":"ok","domains":n}` |
| `GET /ca.pem` | none | `200` PEM text |
| `GET /clock` | none | `200` timing |
| `PUT /clock` | `{scale?, delays?}` | `200` timing. A scale change resets the quota buckets. |
| `GET /domains` | none | `200` list |
| `POST /domains` | seed specification | `201 {customerId, primaryDomain, studentDomain, aliasDomain, districtName, adminEmail, counts, durationMs}` |
| `GET /domains/{id}` | none | `200` domain record |
| `POST /domains/{id}/reset` | none | `200` seed result |
| `DELETE /domains/{id}` | none | `204` |
| `POST /domains/{id}/service-accounts` | `{projectId?}` | `201` service-account file |
| `PUT /domains/{id}/delegation/{clientId}` | `{scopes: string[]}` | `200 {clientId, scopes}` |
| `PUT /domains/{id}/settings` | settings patch | `200` merged settings |
| `GET /domains/{id}/quota` | none | `200` quota report |
| `GET /domains/{id}/faults` | none | `200` fault list |
| `POST /domains/{id}/faults` | fault rule | `201 {id}` |
| `DELETE /domains/{id}/faults` | none | `204` |

Errors answer `{"error": "<message>"}` with 400, 404, 409, or 413.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the control handler**

`src/control/control.ts`:

```ts
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from 'node:http';
import { AccountError, issueServiceAccount } from '../auth/accounts.ts';
import { MAX_SCALE } from '../clock.ts';
import { DomainError } from '../domains.ts';
import { FaultError } from '../faults/faults.ts';
import type { Mock } from '../mock.ts';

const BODY_LIMIT = 1024 * 1024;

class ControlError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  constructor(status: number, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ControlError';
    this.status = status;
  }
}

async function readJson(request: IncomingMessage): Promise<unknown> {
  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
  let size = 0;
  for await (const chunk of request as AsyncIterable<Buffer>) {
    size += chunk.length;
    if (size > BODY_LIMIT) throw new ControlError(413, 'The request body is too large.');
    chunks.push(chunk);
  }
  const text = Buffer.concat(chunks).toString('utf8').trim();
  if (text === '') return {};
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    throw new ControlError(400, 'The request body is not valid JSON.');
  }
}

function send(response: ServerResponse, status: number, body?: unknown, type = 'application/json') {
  if (body === undefined) {
    response.writeHead(status).end();
    return;
  }
  response.writeHead(status, { 'content-type': type });
  response.end(type === 'application/json' ? `${JSON.stringify(body, null, 2)}\n` : String(body));
}

/** The control API. It binds to loopback or the container network only. */
export function createControlHandler(mock: Mock) {
  const domainOrThrow = (id: string) => {
    const domain = mock.domains.get(id);
    if (!domain) throw new ControlError(404, `Domain ${id} does not exist.`);
    return domain;
  };

  async function route(request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): Promise<void> {
    const method = request.method ?? 'GET';
    const path = new URL(request.url ?? '/', 'http://control.invalid').pathname;
    const parts = path.split('/').filter(Boolean).map(decodeURIComponent);

    if (method === 'GET' && path === '/health')
      return send(response, 200, { status: 'ok', domains: mock.domains.list().length });
    if (method === 'GET' && path === '/ca.pem')
      return send(response, 200, mock.certificates.caCert, 'application/x-pem-file');
    if (path === '/clock') {
      if (method === 'PUT') {
        const body = (await readJson(request)) as { scale?: unknown; delays?: unknown };
        if (body.scale !== undefined) {
          if (typeof body.scale !== 'number' || body.scale < 1 || body.scale > MAX_SCALE)
            throw new ControlError(400, `scale must be a number from 1 to ${MAX_SCALE}.`);
          if (body.scale !== mock.timing.scale) mock.limits.reset();
          mock.timing.scale = body.scale;
        }
        if (body.delays !== undefined) {
          if (typeof body.delays !== 'boolean') throw new ControlError(400, 'delays must be true or false.');
          mock.timing.delays = body.delays;
        }
      } else if (method !== 'GET') throw new ControlError(404, 'Not found.');
      return send(response, 200, mock.timing);
    }
    if (parts[0] !== 'domains') throw new ControlError(404, 'Not found.');

    if (parts.length === 1) {
      if (method === 'GET') return send(response, 200, mock.domains.list());
      if (method === 'POST') {
        const { domain, result } = mock.domains.create(await readJson(request), mock.clock.now());
        return send(response, 201, {
          customerId: domain.customerId,
          primaryDomain: domain.spec.primaryDomain,
          studentDomain: domain.spec.studentDomain,
          aliasDomain: domain.spec.aliasDomain,
          districtName: domain.spec.districtName,
          adminEmail: result.adminEmail,
          counts: result.counts,
          durationMs: result.durationMs,
        });
      }
    }
    const id = parts[1];
    if (parts.length === 2) {
      if (method === 'GET') return send(response, 200, domainOrThrow(id).record);
      if (method === 'DELETE') {
        mock.faults.clear(id);
        mock.domains.delete(id);
        return send(response, 204);
      }
    }
    if (parts.length === 3) {
      domainOrThrow(id);
      switch (`${method} ${parts[2]}`) {
        case 'POST reset':
          return send(response, 200, mock.domains.reset(id, mock.clock.now()));
        case 'POST service-accounts': {
          const body = (await readJson(request)) as { projectId?: unknown };
          if (body.projectId !== undefined && typeof body.projectId !== 'string')
            throw new ControlError(400, 'projectId must be a string.');
          return send(response, 201, issueServiceAccount(mock.registry, id, body.projectId));
        }
        case 'PUT settings':
          return send(response, 200, mock.domains.updateSettings(id, await readJson(request)));
        case 'GET quota':
          return send(response, 200, mock.limits.report(id));
        case 'GET faults':
          return send(response, 200, mock.faults.list(id));
        case 'POST faults':
          return send(response, 201, { id: mock.faults.add(id, await readJson(request)) });
        case 'DELETE faults':
          mock.faults.clear(id);
          return send(response, 204);
      }
    }
    if (parts.length === 4 && parts[2] === 'delegation' && method === 'PUT') {
      domainOrThrow(id);
      const body = (await readJson(request)) as { scopes?: unknown };
      if (
        !Array.isArray(body.scopes) ||
        !body.scopes.every(
          (scope) => typeof scope === 'string' && scope.startsWith('https://www.googleapis.com/auth/'),
        )
      )
        throw new ControlError(400, 'scopes must be an array of Google scope URLs.');
      mock.registry.setGrant(id, parts[3], body.scopes as string[]);
      return send(response, 200, { clientId: parts[3], scopes: body.scopes });
    }
    throw new ControlError(404, 'Not found.');
  }

  return (request: IncomingMessage, response: ServerResponse): void => {
    route(request, response).catch((error: unknown) => {
      if (error instanceof ControlError || error instanceof DomainError)
        return send(response, error.status, { error: error.message });
      if (error instanceof AccountError || error instanceof FaultError)
        return send(response, 400, { error: error.message });
      console.error(error);
      send(response, 500, { error: 'Internal error.' });
    });
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the entry point**

`src/main.ts`:

```ts
import { once } from 'node:events';
import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import http from 'node:http';
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';
import { dirname } from 'node:path';
import { parseTiming } from './clock.ts';
import { createControlHandler } from './control/control.ts';
import { createMock } from './mock.ts';

const env = process.env;
const port = (value: string | undefined, fallback: number) => {
  const parsed = value === undefined || value === '' ? fallback : Number(value);
  if (!Number.isInteger(parsed) || parsed < 0 || parsed > 65_535)
    throw new Error(`Invalid port: ${value}`);
  return parsed;
};

const dataDir = env.MOCK_DATA_DIR ?? '/data';
const timing = parseTiming(env.MOCK_TIME_SCALE, env.MOCK_DELAYS);
const mock = createMock({ dataDir, timing });
if (env.MOCK_CA_EXPORT) {
  mkdirSync(dirname(env.MOCK_CA_EXPORT), { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(env.MOCK_CA_EXPORT, mock.certificates.caCert, { mode: 0o644 });
}
const google = await mock.listenGoogle(
  port(env.MOCK_GOOGLE_PORT, 443),
  env.MOCK_GOOGLE_HOST ?? '0.0.0.0',
);
const control = http.createServer(createControlHandler(mock));
control.listen(port(env.MOCK_CONTROL_PORT, 8080), env.MOCK_CONTROL_HOST ?? '127.0.0.1');
await once(control, 'listening');
console.log(
  JSON.stringify({
    event: 'started',
    googlePort: (google.address() as AddressInfo).port,
    controlPort: (control.address() as AddressInfo).port,
    dataDir,
    timing,
  }),
);

const stop = () => {
  control.close();
  mock.close();
  process.exit(0);
};
process.on('SIGTERM', stop);
process.on('SIGINT', stop);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the CLI**

`bin/mockctl.ts`:

```ts
#!/usr/bin/env node
import { writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { parseArgs } from 'node:util';
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../src/auth/scopes.ts';

const BASE = (process.env.MOCK_CONTROL_URL ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8080').replace(/\/$/, '');

const USAGE = `Usage: mockctl <command>

  domain create --tier <small|medium|large|xlarge> --seed <seed> [--name <name>] [--layout <role-first|school-first>] [--counts <json>] [--rates <json>]
  domain list
  domain show <customerId>
  domain reset <customerId>
  domain delete <customerId>
  credential <customerId> [--scopes <a,b>] [--project <id>] [--out <file>]
  settings <customerId> --json <patch>
  quota <customerId>
  fault add <customerId> --json <rule>
  fault list <customerId>
  fault clear <customerId>
  clock [--scale <n>] [--delays <on|off>]
  ca [--out <file>]

MOCK_CONTROL_URL selects the control API. The default is ${BASE}.`;

class CliError extends Error {}

async function call(method: string, path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
  const response = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: body === undefined ? {} : { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const text = await response.text();
  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = text;
    try {
      message = (JSON.parse(text) as { error: string }).error;
    } catch {
      /* The body is not JSON. */
    }
    throw new CliError(`${response.status}: ${message}`);
  }
  if (text === '') return undefined;
  return response.headers.get('content-type')?.includes('json') ? JSON.parse(text) : text;
}

const json = (text: string | undefined, label: string): unknown => {
  if (text === undefined) throw new CliError(`${label} is required.`);
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    throw new CliError(`${label} is not valid JSON.`);
  }
};

const print = (value: unknown) => {
  if (value !== undefined)
    console.log(typeof value === 'string' ? value.trimEnd() : JSON.stringify(value, null, 2));
};

async function main(argv: string[]): Promise<void> {
  const { positionals, values } = parseArgs({
    args: argv,
    allowPositionals: true,
    options: {
      tier: { type: 'string' },
      seed: { type: 'string' },
      name: { type: 'string' },
      layout: { type: 'string' },
      counts: { type: 'string' },
      rates: { type: 'string' },
      scopes: { type: 'string' },
      project: { type: 'string' },
      out: { type: 'string' },
      json: { type: 'string' },
      scale: { type: 'string' },
      delays: { type: 'string' },
    },
  });
  const [command, sub, third] = positionals;
  const id = (value: string | undefined) => {
    if (!value) throw new CliError('A customer ID is required.');
    return encodeURIComponent(value);
  };
  switch (command) {
    case 'domain':
      switch (sub) {
        case 'create':
          return print(
            await call('POST', '/domains', {
              tier: values.tier,
              seed: values.seed,
              ...(values.name ? { name: values.name } : {}),
              ...(values.layout ? { layout: values.layout } : {}),
              ...(values.counts ? { counts: json(values.counts, '--counts') } : {}),
              ...(values.rates ? { rates: json(values.rates, '--rates') } : {}),
            }),
          );
        case 'list':
          return print(await call('GET', '/domains'));
        case 'show':
          return print(await call('GET', `/domains/${id(third)}`));
        case 'reset':
          return print(await call('POST', `/domains/${id(third)}/reset`));
        case 'delete':
          await call('DELETE', `/domains/${id(third)}`);
          return print({ deleted: third });
      }
      break;
    case 'credential': {
      const account = (await call('POST', `/domains/${id(sub)}/service-accounts`, {
        ...(values.project ? { projectId: values.project } : {}),
      })) as { client_id: string };
      const scopes = values.scopes ? values.scopes.split(',').map((scope) => scope.trim()) : READ_SCOPES;
      await call('PUT', `/domains/${id(sub)}/delegation/${account.client_id}`, { scopes });
      if (values.out) {
        writeFileSync(values.out, `${JSON.stringify(account, null, 2)}\n`, { mode: 0o600 });
        return print({ written: values.out, clientId: account.client_id, scopes });
      }
      return print(account);
    }
    case 'settings':
      return print(await call('PUT', `/domains/${id(sub)}/settings`, json(values.json, '--json')));
    case 'quota':
      return print(await call('GET', `/domains/${id(sub)}/quota`));
    case 'fault':
      switch (sub) {
        case 'add':
          return print(await call('POST', `/domains/${id(third)}/faults`, json(values.json, '--json')));
        case 'list':
          return print(await call('GET', `/domains/${id(third)}/faults`));
        case 'clear':
          await call('DELETE', `/domains/${id(third)}/faults`);
          return print({ cleared: third });
      }
      break;
    case 'clock': {
      const body: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      if (values.scale !== undefined) body.scale = Number(values.scale);
      if (values.delays !== undefined) body.delays = values.delays === 'on';
      return print(await call(Object.keys(body).length > 0 ? 'PUT' : 'GET', '/clock', Object.keys(body).length > 0 ? body : undefined));
    }
    case 'ca': {
      const pem = (await call('GET', '/ca.pem')) as string;
      if (values.out) {
        writeFileSync(values.out, pem, { mode: 0o644 });
        return print({ written: values.out });
      }
      return print(pem);
    }
  }
  throw new CliError(USAGE);
}

main(process.argv.slice(2)).catch((error: unknown) => {
  console.error(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
  process.exit(1);
});
```

Run: `chmod +x bin/mockctl.ts`.

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { once } from 'node:events';
import http from 'node:http';
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';
import test from 'node:test';
import { SCOPES } from '../auth/scopes.ts';
import { startHarness } from '../testing/harness.ts';
import { signAssertion, tokenRequestBody } from '../testing/jwt.ts';
import { createControlHandler } from './control.ts';

const SPEC = { tier: 'small', seed: 'control', counts: { schools: 2, students: 60, staff: 10, devices: 80 } };

async function setup(t: import('node:test').TestContext) {
  const harness = await startHarness();
  const server = http.createServer(createControlHandler(harness.mock));
  server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  await once(server, 'listening');
  t.after(async () => {
    server.close();
    await harness.close();
  });
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
  const call = async (method: string, path: string, body?: unknown) => {
    const response = await fetch(`${base}${path}`, {
      method,
      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : typeof body === 'string' ? body : JSON.stringify(body),
    });
    const text = await response.text();
    return { status: response.status, text, json: () => JSON.parse(text) };
  };
  return { harness, call };
}

test('the control API creates a domain that serves real token exchanges', async (t) => {
  const { harness, call } = await setup(t);
  const created = await call('POST', '/domains', SPEC);
  assert.equal(created.status, 201);
  const { customerId, adminEmail, counts } = created.json();
  assert.equal(counts.devices, 80);
  assert.equal((await call('POST', '/domains', SPEC)).status, 409);
  assert.equal((await call('POST', '/domains', { tier: 'huge', seed: 'x' })).status, 400);
  assert.equal((await call('POST', '/domains', '{not json')).status, 400);
  assert.equal((await call('GET', '/domains')).json().length, 1);
  assert.equal((await call('GET', `/domains/${customerId}`)).json().customerId, customerId);

  const account = (await call('POST', `/domains/${customerId}/service-accounts`, {})).json();
  assert.equal(account.type, 'service_account');
  const scope = SCOPES.orgunit[1];
  assert.equal((await call('PUT', `/domains/${customerId}/delegation/${account.client_id}`, { scopes: [scope] })).status, 200);
  assert.equal((await call('PUT', `/domains/${customerId}/delegation/${account.client_id}`, { scopes: ['bad'] })).status, 400);
  const token = await harness.request({
    host: 'oauth2.googleapis.com',
    path: '/token',
    method: 'POST',
    body: tokenRequestBody(signAssertion(account, { scope, sub: adminEmail })),
  });
  assert.equal(token.status, 200);
  const orgunits = await harness.request({
    host: 'admin.googleapis.com',
    path: '/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits',
    token: token.json().access_token,
  });
  assert.equal(orgunits.status, 200);
  const quota = (await call('GET', `/domains/${customerId}/quota`)).json();
  assert.equal(quota.buckets[0].snapshot.count, 1);
});

test('settings, faults, clock, CA, reset, and delete work through the control API', async (t) => {
  const { harness, call } = await setup(t);
  const { customerId } = (await call('POST', '/domains', SPEC)).json();
  assert.equal((await call('PUT', `/domains/${customerId}/settings`, { quota: { perMinute: 7 } })).json().quota.perMinute, 7);
  assert.equal((await call('PUT', `/domains/${customerId}/settings`, { quota: { perMinute: -1 } })).status, 400);
  const fault = await call('POST', `/domains/${customerId}/faults`, { kind: 'reset', count: 1 });
  assert.equal(fault.status, 201);
  assert.equal((await call('GET', `/domains/${customerId}/faults`)).json().length, 1);
  assert.equal((await call('POST', `/domains/${customerId}/faults`, { kind: 'meteor' })).status, 400);
  assert.equal((await call('DELETE', `/domains/${customerId}/faults`)).status, 204);
  const clock = await call('PUT', '/clock', { scale: 60, delays: false });
  assert.deepEqual(clock.json(), { scale: 60, delays: false });
  assert.equal(harness.mock.timing.scale, 60);
  assert.equal((await call('PUT', '/clock', { scale: 0 })).status, 400);
  assert.match((await call('GET', '/ca.pem')).text, /BEGIN CERTIFICATE/);
  assert.equal((await call('POST', `/domains/${customerId}/reset`)).status, 200);
  assert.equal((await call('DELETE', `/domains/${customerId}`)).status, 204);
  assert.equal((await call('GET', `/domains/${customerId}`)).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await call('GET', '/nothing')).status, 404);
  assert.deepEqual((await call('GET', '/health')).json(), { status: 'ok', domains: 0 });
});
```

```ts
test('mockctl creates a domain, issues a credential file, and sets the clock', async (t) => {
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { once } from 'node:events';
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { createInterface } from 'node:readline';
import test from 'node:test';

test('the entry point starts both listeners, exports the CA, and stops on SIGTERM', async () => {
  const dataDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'main-'));
  const caExport = join(dataDir, 'public', 'ca.pem');
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['src/main.ts'], {
    env: {
      ...process.env,
      MOCK_DATA_DIR: dataDir,
      MOCK_GOOGLE_PORT: '0',
      MOCK_GOOGLE_HOST: '127.0.0.1',
      MOCK_CONTROL_PORT: '0',
      MOCK_CA_EXPORT: caExport,
      MOCK_DELAYS: 'off',
    },
    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'inherit'],
  });
  try {
    const [line] = (await once(createInterface({ input: child.stdout! }), 'line')) as [string];
    const started = JSON.parse(line);
    assert.equal(started.event, 'started');
    assert.ok(started.googlePort > 0 && started.controlPort > 0);
    assert.deepEqual(started.timing, { scale: 1, delays: false });
    const health = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${started.controlPort}/health`);
    assert.equal(health.status, 200);
    assert.match(readFileSync(caExport, 'utf8'), /BEGIN CERTIFICATE/);
    child.kill('SIGTERM');
    const [code] = await once(child, 'exit');
    assert.equal(code, 0);
  } finally {
    child.kill();
    rmSync(dataDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
});
```

````markdown
## Run locally

Start the mock on high ports with data in `./data`:

```sh
MOCK_DATA_DIR=./data MOCK_GOOGLE_PORT=8443 MOCK_CONTROL_PORT=8080 npm start
```

Create a domain and a credential:

```sh
node bin/mockctl.ts domain create --tier small --seed demo
node bin/mockctl.ts credential <customerId> --out ./demo-credential.json
```

The credential grants the read-only milestone 1 scopes. Pass `--scopes` to grant a different list.
Delegate to the `adminEmail` that `domain create` prints.

`mockctl clock --scale 60` compresses every quota window, flag, and delay by 60.
`mockctl clock --delays off` removes all delays.
`mockctl quota <customerId>` shows the quota buckets and the flag state.
`mockctl fault add <customerId> --json '{"kind":"status","status":503,"reason":"backendError","count":3}'` injects faults.
````

### Task 19: Discovery contract tests

**Files:**
- Create: `test/contract/discovery/directory_v1.json`, `test/contract/discovery/chromemanagement_v1.json` (downloaded), `test/contract/validate.ts`, `test/contract/validate.test.ts`, `test/contract/contract.test.ts`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the harness and every milestone 1 route.
- Produces: `Schema`, `Discovery`, `validate(doc, schema, value, path?): string[]`, `methodSchema(doc, path: readonly string[]): Schema`.

The validator reports a property that the schema does not declare. That rule catches invented fields. It checks `int64` strings, date-time strings, enums, arrays, and nested `$ref` schemas. Type `any` accepts any value.

```ts
        when: { type: 'string', format: 'date-time' },
        state: { type: 'string', enum: ['ON', 'OFF'] },

      },
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the validator**

`test/contract/validate.ts`:

```ts
export interface Schema {
  type?: string;
  $ref?: string;
  format?: string;
  enum?: string[];
  properties?: Record<string, Schema>;
  additionalProperties?: Schema;
  items?: Schema;
}

interface Resource {
  resources?: Record<string, Resource>;
  methods?: Record<string, { response?: Schema }>;
}

export interface Discovery extends Resource {
  schemas: Record<string, Schema>;
}

/** Validate a response against a discovery schema. Undeclared properties are errors. */
export function validate(
  doc: Discovery,
  schema: Schema,
  value: unknown,
  path = '$',
): string[] {
  if (schema.$ref) {
    const target = doc.schemas[schema.$ref];
    return target ? validate(doc, target, value, path) : [`${path}: unknown schema ${schema.$ref}`];
  }
  switch (schema.type) {
    case 'object': {
      if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value))
        return [`${path}: expected object`];
      const errors: string[] = [];
      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
        const property = schema.properties?.[key] ?? schema.additionalProperties;
        if (!property) errors.push(`${path}.${key}: not in schema`);
        else errors.push(...validate(doc, property, item, `${path}.${key}`));
      }
      return errors;
    }
    case 'array':
      return Array.isArray(value)
        ? value.flatMap((item, index) => validate(doc, schema.items ?? {}, item, `${path}[${index}]`))
        : [`${path}: expected array`];
    case 'string':
      if (typeof value !== 'string') return [`${path}: expected string`];
      if ((schema.format === 'int64' || schema.format === 'uint64') && !/^-?\d+$/.test(value))
        return [`${path}: expected an integer string`];
      if (
        (schema.format === 'date-time' || schema.format === 'google-datetime') &&
        Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value))
      )
        return [`${path}: expected a date-time`];
      if (schema.enum && !schema.enum.includes(value)) return [`${path}: ${value} is not in the enum`];
      return [];
    case 'integer':
      return Number.isInteger(value) ? [] : [`${path}: expected integer`];
    case 'number':
      return typeof value === 'number' ? [] : [`${path}: expected number`];
    case 'boolean':
      return typeof value === 'boolean' ? [] : [`${path}: expected boolean`];
    case 'any':
    case undefined:
      return [];
    default:
      return [`${path}: unknown schema type ${schema.type}`];
  }
}

/** The response schema of a method, for example `['customers', 'telemetry', 'devices', 'list']`. */
export function methodSchema(doc: Discovery, path: readonly string[]): Schema {
  let node: Resource = doc;
  for (const name of path.slice(0, -1)) {
    const next = node.resources?.[name];
    if (!next) throw new Error(`No resource ${name} in ${path.join('.')}.`);
    node = next;
  }
  const response = node.methods?.[path.at(-1)!]?.response;
  if (!response) throw new Error(`No response schema for ${path.join('.')}.`);
  return response;
}
```

Expected: PASS, 3 tests.
