```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import test from 'node:test';
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../../src/auth/scopes.ts';
import { row } from '../../src/store/sqlite.ts';
import { grantToken, seedTestDomain, startHarness } from '../../src/testing/harness.ts';
import { methodSchema, validate, type Discovery } from './validate.ts';

const load = (name: string) =>
  JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL(`./discovery/${name}.json`, import.meta.url), 'utf8')) as Discovery;
const directory = load('directory_v1');
const chrome = load('chromemanagement_v1');
const MASK = 'name,customer,orgUnitId,deviceId,serialNumber,batteryInfo,batteryStatusReport,memoryInfo,storageInfo';
const D = '/admin/directory/v1';

test('every milestone 1 response matches its discovery schema', async (t) => {
  const harness = await startHarness();
  t.after(() => harness.close());
  const domain = seedTestDomain(harness, {
    tier: 'small',
    seed: 'contract',
    counts: { schools: 3, students: 200, staff: 30, devices: 300 },
  });
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const deviceId = row<{ device_id: string }>(
    domain.db.prepare("SELECT device_id FROM devices WHERE status = 'ACTIVE' LIMIT 1").get(),
  )!.device_id;
  const deprovisioned = row<{ device_id: string }>(
    domain.db.prepare("SELECT device_id FROM devices WHERE status = 'DEPROVISIONED' LIMIT 1").get(),
  )?.device_id;
  const ou = row<{ path: string }>(domain.db.prepare("SELECT path FROM orgunits WHERE path != '/' LIMIT 1").get())!.path;
  const admin = `admin@${domain.spec.primaryDomain}`;
  const devices = `${D}/customer/my_customer/devices/chromeos`;
  const cases: {
    name: string;
    doc: Discovery;
    method: string[];
    host?: string;
    path: string;
    query?: Record<string, string>;
  }[] = [
    { name: 'customers.get', doc: directory, method: ['customers', 'get'], path: `${D}/customers/my_customer` },
    { name: 'domains.list', doc: directory, method: ['domains', 'list'], path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/domains` },
    { name: 'orgunits.list', doc: directory, method: ['orgunits', 'list'], path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/orgunits`, query: { type: 'allIncludingParent' } },
    { name: 'orgunits.get', doc: directory, method: ['orgunits', 'get'], path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/orgunits/${encodeURIComponent(ou.slice(1))}` },
    { name: 'chromeosdevices.list FULL', doc: directory, method: ['chromeosdevices', 'list'], path: devices, query: { maxResults: '300' } },
    { name: 'chromeosdevices.list BASIC', doc: directory, method: ['chromeosdevices', 'list'], path: devices, query: { maxResults: '300', projection: 'BASIC' } },
    { name: 'chromeosdevices.get', doc: directory, method: ['chromeosdevices', 'get'], path: `${devices}/${deviceId}` },
    ...(deprovisioned
      ? [{ name: 'chromeosdevices.get deprovisioned', doc: directory, method: ['chromeosdevices', 'get'], path: `${devices}/${deprovisioned}` }]
      : []),
    { name: 'users.list', doc: directory, method: ['users', 'list'], path: `${D}/users`, query: { customer: 'my_customer', maxResults: '500' } },
    { name: 'users.list public', doc: directory, method: ['users', 'list'], path: `${D}/users`, query: { customer: 'my_customer', viewType: 'domain_public' } },
    { name: 'users.get', doc: directory, method: ['users', 'get'], path: `${D}/users/${admin}` },
    {
      name: 'telemetry.devices.list',
      doc: chrome,
      method: ['customers', 'telemetry', 'devices', 'list'],
      host: 'chromemanagement.googleapis.com',
      path: '/v1/customers/my_customer/telemetry/devices',
      query: { readMask: MASK, filter: 'reports_timestamp>=0', pageSize: '1000' },
    },
    {
      name: 'telemetry.devices.get',
      doc: chrome,
      method: ['customers', 'telemetry', 'devices', 'get'],
      host: 'chromemanagement.googleapis.com',
      path: `/v1/customers/my_customer/telemetry/devices/${deviceId}`,
      query: { readMask: MASK },
    },
  ];
  for (const testCase of cases) {
    const response = await harness.request({
      host: testCase.host ?? 'admin.googleapis.com',
      path: testCase.path,
      query: testCase.query,
      token,
    });
    assert.equal(response.status, 200, `${testCase.name}: ${response.text.slice(0, 300)}`);
    assert.deepEqual(
      validate(testCase.doc, methodSchema(testCase.doc, testCase.method), response.json()),
      [],
      testCase.name,
    );
  }
});
```

### Task 20: Google client compatibility

**Files:**
- Create: `test/compat/proxy.ts`, `test/compat/run.ts`, `test/compat/directory.compat.ts`, `test/compat/chrome.compat.ts`
- Modify: `package.json` (dev dependencies), `.github/workflows/ci.yml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createMock`, `issueServiceAccount`, `READ_SCOPES`, `SCOPES`.
- Produces: `startConnectProxy(target: { host: string; port: number }): Promise<{ port: number; close(): Promise<void> }>`.

Google's clients connect to port 443 on the real hostnames. The test redirects them without root access:
- An HTTP CONNECT proxy forwards `*.googleapis.com:443` to the mock's random port. `gaxios` honors `HTTPS_PROXY`.
- TLS still terminates at the mock with the requested server name. The child process trusts the mock CA through `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`.
- The proxy refuses every other host, so no request reaches real Google.

- [ ] **Step 1: Install Google's clients**

Run: `npm install --save-exact --save-dev google-auth-library @googleapis/admin @googleapis/chromemanagement`
Expected: the three packages appear under `devDependencies`. Run `npm ls google-auth-library` and confirm one deduplicated version where possible.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the proxy**

`test/compat/proxy.ts`:

```ts
import { once } from 'node:events';
import http from 'node:http';
import net, { type AddressInfo } from 'node:net';

/** Forward CONNECT requests for *.googleapis.com:443 to the mock. Refuse every other host. */
export async function startConnectProxy(target: {
  host: string;
  port: number;
}): Promise<{ port: number; close(): Promise<void> }> {
  const sockets = new Set<net.Socket>();
  const server = http.createServer((_request, response) => response.writeHead(405).end());
  server.on('connect', (request: http.IncomingMessage, socket: net.Socket, head: Buffer) => {
    const [host, port] = (request.url ?? '').split(':');
    if (!host.endsWith('.googleapis.com') || port !== '443') {
      socket.end('HTTP/1.1 403 Forbidden\r\n\r\n');
      return;
    }
    const upstream = net.connect(target.port, target.host, () => {
      socket.write('HTTP/1.1 200 Connection Established\r\n\r\n');
      if (head.length > 0) upstream.write(head);
      upstream.pipe(socket);
      socket.pipe(upstream);
    });
    for (const side of [socket, upstream]) {
      sockets.add(side);
      side.on('close', () => sockets.delete(side));
      side.on('error', () => {
        socket.destroy();
        upstream.destroy();
      });
    }
  });
  server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
  await once(server, 'listening');
  return {
    port: (server.address() as AddressInfo).port,
    async close() {
      for (const socket of sockets) socket.destroy();
      server.close();
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the runner**

`test/compat/run.ts`:

```ts
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { once } from 'node:events';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { issueServiceAccount } from '../../src/auth/accounts.ts';
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../../src/auth/scopes.ts';
import { createMock } from '../../src/mock.ts';
import { row } from '../../src/store/sqlite.ts';
import { startConnectProxy } from './proxy.ts';

const dataDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'compat-'));
const mock = createMock({ dataDir, timing: { scale: 1, delays: false } });
const google = await mock.listenGoogle(0, '127.0.0.1');
const proxy = await startConnectProxy({
  host: '127.0.0.1',
  port: (google.address() as AddressInfo).port,
});
const { domain } = mock.domains.create(
  { tier: 'small', seed: 'compat', counts: { schools: 3, students: 400, staff: 50, devices: 750 } },
  Date.now(),
);
const account = issueServiceAccount(mock.registry, domain.customerId);
mock.registry.setGrant(domain.customerId, account.client_id, READ_SCOPES);
const caFile = join(dataDir, 'ca-export.pem');
const accountFile = join(dataDir, 'account.json');
writeFileSync(caFile, mock.certificates.caCert);
writeFileSync(accountFile, JSON.stringify(account), { mode: 0o600 });

const count = (sql: string) => Number(row<{ n: number }>(domain.db.prepare(sql).get())?.n);
const expected = {
  customerId: domain.customerId,
  primaryDomain: domain.spec.primaryDomain,
  orgUnits: count("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM orgunits WHERE path != '/'"),
  devices: count('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices'),
  users: count('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM users'),
  telemetry: count("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices WHERE status != 'DEPROVISIONED'"),
  deviceId: row<{ device_id: string }>(domain.db.prepare('SELECT device_id FROM devices LIMIT 1').get())!.device_id,
};

const env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = {
  ...process.env,
  NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS: caFile,
  HTTPS_PROXY: `http://127.0.0.1:${proxy.port}`,
  MOCK_SA_FILE: accountFile,
  MOCK_SUBJECT: `admin@${domain.spec.primaryDomain}`,
  MOCK_EXPECT: JSON.stringify(expected),
};
delete env.NO_PROXY;
delete env.no_proxy;
delete env.https_proxy;

const child = spawn(
  process.execPath,
  ['--test', 'test/compat/directory.compat.ts', 'test/compat/chrome.compat.ts'],
  { stdio: 'inherit', env },
);
const [code] = (await once(child, 'exit')) as [number | null];
await proxy.close();
mock.close();
rmSync(dataDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
process.exit(code ?? 1);
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import test from 'node:test';
import { admin } from '@googleapis/admin';
import { JWT } from 'google-auth-library';
import { READ_SCOPES, SCOPES } from '../../src/auth/scopes.ts';

const key = JSON.parse(readFileSync(process.env.MOCK_SA_FILE!, 'utf8')) as {
  client_email: string;
  private_key: string;
};
const expected = JSON.parse(process.env.MOCK_EXPECT!) as {
  customerId: string;
  primaryDomain: string;
  orgUnits: number;
  devices: number;
  users: number;
  deviceId: string;
};
const auth = (scopes: readonly string[]) =>
  new JWT({
    email: key.client_email,
    key: key.private_key,
    subject: process.env.MOCK_SUBJECT,
    scopes: [...scopes],
  });
const directory = admin({ version: 'directory_v1', auth: auth(READ_SCOPES) });

test('customers.get and domains.list work through the Google client', async () => {
  assert.equal((await directory.customers.get({ customerKey: 'my_customer' })).data.id, expected.customerId);
  const domains = (await directory.domains.list({ customer: 'my_customer' })).data.domains ?? [];
  assert.ok(domains.some((domain) => domain.domainName === expected.primaryDomain && domain.isPrimary));
});

test('orgunits.list returns every OU', async () => {
  const { data } = await directory.orgunits.list({ customerId: 'my_customer', type: 'all' });
  assert.equal(data.organizationUnits?.length, expected.orgUnits);
});

test('chromeosdevices.list pages through every device', async () => {
  let count = 0;
  let pageToken: string | undefined;
  do {
    const { data } = await directory.chromeosdevices.list({ customerId: 'my_customer', maxResults: 300, pageToken });
    count += data.chromeosdevices?.length ?? 0;
    pageToken = data.nextPageToken ?? undefined;
  } while (pageToken);
  assert.equal(count, expected.devices);
  const { data } = await directory.chromeosdevices.get({ customerId: 'my_customer', deviceId: expected.deviceId });
  assert.equal(data.deviceId, expected.deviceId);
});

test('users.list pages through every user', async () => {
  let count = 0;
  let pageToken: string | undefined;
  do {
    const { data } = await directory.users.list({ customer: 'my_customer', maxResults: 500, pageToken });
    count += data.users?.length ?? 0;
    pageToken = data.nextPageToken ?? undefined;
  } while (pageToken);
  assert.equal(count, expected.users);
  const administrator = await directory.users.get({ userKey: process.env.MOCK_SUBJECT! });
  assert.equal(administrator.data.isAdmin, true);
});

test('getTokenInfo reports the exact requested scope', async () => {
  const client = auth([SCOPES.orgunit[1]]);
  const { token } = await client.getAccessToken();
  const info = await client.getTokenInfo(token!);
  assert.deepEqual(info.scopes, [SCOPES.orgunit[1]]);
});

test('an ungranted scope fails the token exchange', async () => {
  await assert.rejects(
    auth(['https://www.googleapis.com/auth/admin.directory.group.readonly']).getAccessToken(),
    /unauthorized_client/,
  );
});

test('a batch request answers each part', async () => {
  const devices = `/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/devices/chromeos`;
  const body = [
    `--compat\r\nContent-Type: application/http\r\nContent-ID: <a>\r\n\r\nGET ${devices}/${expected.deviceId}\r\n\r\n`,
    `--compat\r\nContent-Type: application/http\r\nContent-ID: <b>\r\n\r\nGET ${devices}/missing\r\n\r\n`,
    '--compat--\r\n',
  ].join('');
  const response = await auth(READ_SCOPES).request<string>({
    url: 'https://www.googleapis.com/batch/admin/directory_v1',
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'content-type': 'multipart/mixed; boundary=compat' },
    data: body,
    responseType: 'text',
  });
  assert.match(response.data, /Content-ID: <response-a>\r\n\r\nHTTP\/1\.1 200/);
  assert.match(response.data, /Content-ID: <response-b>\r\n\r\nHTTP\/1\.1 404/);
});
```

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import test from 'node:test';
import { chromemanagement } from '@googleapis/chromemanagement';
import { JWT } from 'google-auth-library';
import { SCOPES } from '../../src/auth/scopes.ts';

const key = JSON.parse(readFileSync(process.env.MOCK_SA_FILE!, 'utf8')) as {
  client_email: string;
  private_key: string;
};
const expected = JSON.parse(process.env.MOCK_EXPECT!) as { telemetry: number };

test('telemetry pages through every reporting device', async () => {
  const management = chromemanagement({
    version: 'v1',
    auth: new JWT({
      email: key.client_email,
      key: key.private_key,
      subject: process.env.MOCK_SUBJECT,
      scopes: [...SCOPES.telemetry],
    }),
  });
  let count = 0;
  let pageToken: string | undefined;
  do {
    const { data } = await management.customers.telemetry.devices.list({
      parent: 'customers/my_customer',
      readMask: 'deviceId,batteryInfo,batteryStatusReport',
      pageSize: 1000,
      pageToken,
    });
    count += data.devices?.length ?? 0;
    pageToken = data.nextPageToken ?? undefined;
  } while (pageToken);
  assert.equal(count, expected.telemetry);
});
```

### Task 21: Docker image and the Campus Commander overlay

**Files:**
- Create: `Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`, `compose/standalone.yml`, `compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml`, `compose/smoke.yml`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `src/main.ts` and its environment variables (Task 18).
- Produces: the image `ghcr.io/campuscommander/admin-sdk-mock`. The `ADMIN_SDK_MOCK_IMAGE` variable overrides it.
- Produces: the overlay. It adds the `admin-sdk-mock` service to the Campus Commander `internal` and `ingress` networks, aliases the four Google hostnames on `internal`, mounts the CA into `api` and `workers`, and sets `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`.

Facts from the Campus Commander all-docker renderer (`deployment/profiles/all-docker/render.mjs`): the services are named `api` and `workers`. Both join the `internal` network, which is `internal: true`. No file in `campus-commander` changes.

The image runs as the `node` user. Port 443 needs `net.ipv4.ip_unprivileged_port_start=0`, which every Compose file sets.

- [ ] **Step 1: Pin the base image digest**

Run: `docker buildx imagetools inspect node:24-alpine --format '{{json .Manifest.Digest}}'`
Expected: a `sha256:` digest. Use it in the Dockerfile in place of `<digest>`. If the Docker daemon is unavailable, run `curl -sSI -H 'Accept: application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json' -H "Authorization: Bearer $(curl -sS 'https://auth.docker.io/token?service=registry.docker.io&scope=repository:library/node:pull' | node -pe 'JSON.parse(require("fs").readFileSync(0)).token')" https://registry-1.docker.io/v2/library/node/manifests/24-alpine | grep -i docker-content-digest` instead.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the image files**

`Dockerfile`:

```dockerfile
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1
FROM node:24-alpine@<digest>
WORKDIR /app
COPY package.json package-lock.json ./
RUN npm ci --omit=dev && npm cache clean --force
COPY src ./src
COPY bin ./bin
ENV NODE_ENV=production \
    MOCK_DATA_DIR=/data \
    MOCK_GOOGLE_PORT=443 \
    MOCK_CONTROL_PORT=8080 \
    MOCK_CONTROL_HOST=0.0.0.0
RUN mkdir -p /data /ca-public && chown node:node /data /ca-public
USER node
VOLUME ["/data"]
EXPOSE 443 8080
HEALTHCHECK --interval=5s --timeout=3s --retries=12 \
  CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:8080/health').then(r => process.exit(r.ok ? 0 : 1), () => process.exit(1))"]
ENTRYPOINT ["node", "src/main.ts"]
```

`.dockerignore`:

```
node_modules
data
.git
.github
test
parity
docs
compose
*.sqlite*
```

`compose/standalone.yml`:

```yaml
name: admin-sdk-mock
services:
  admin-sdk-mock:
    image: ${ADMIN_SDK_MOCK_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/campuscommander/admin-sdk-mock:main}
    build: ..
    sysctls:
      net.ipv4.ip_unprivileged_port_start: 0
    environment:
      MOCK_TIME_SCALE: ${MOCK_TIME_SCALE:-1}
      MOCK_DELAYS: ${MOCK_DELAYS:-on}
      MOCK_CA_EXPORT: /ca-public/ca.pem
    volumes:
      - admin-sdk-mock-data:/data
      - admin-sdk-mock-ca:/ca-public
    ports:
      - '127.0.0.1:18080:8080'
      - '127.0.0.1:18443:443'
volumes:
  admin-sdk-mock-data: {}
  admin-sdk-mock-ca: {}
```

`compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml`:

```yaml
# Combine with a rendered Campus Commander all-docker Compose file:
#   docker compose -f <install>/docker-compose.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml up --detach --wait
# The api and workers containers then reach this mock for every Google hostname.
services:
  admin-sdk-mock:
    image: ${ADMIN_SDK_MOCK_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/campuscommander/admin-sdk-mock:main}
    sysctls:
      net.ipv4.ip_unprivileged_port_start: 0
    environment:
      MOCK_TIME_SCALE: ${MOCK_TIME_SCALE:-1}
      MOCK_DELAYS: ${MOCK_DELAYS:-on}
      MOCK_CA_EXPORT: /ca-public/ca.pem
    volumes:
      - admin-sdk-mock-data:/data
      - admin-sdk-mock-ca:/ca-public
    ports:
      - '127.0.0.1:18080:8080'
    networks:
      ingress: {}
      internal:
        aliases:
          - oauth2.googleapis.com
          - admin.googleapis.com
          - www.googleapis.com
          - chromemanagement.googleapis.com
  api:
    environment:
      NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS: /run/admin-sdk-mock/ca.pem
    volumes:
      - admin-sdk-mock-ca:/run/admin-sdk-mock:ro
    depends_on:
      admin-sdk-mock:
        condition: service_healthy
  workers:
    environment:
      NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS: /run/admin-sdk-mock/ca.pem
    volumes:
      - admin-sdk-mock-ca:/run/admin-sdk-mock:ro
    depends_on:
      admin-sdk-mock:
        condition: service_healthy
volumes:
  admin-sdk-mock-data: {}
  admin-sdk-mock-ca: {}
```

`compose/smoke.yml` stands in for Campus Commander with two containers named `api` and `workers` on an internal network. A real Google request cannot leave that network. A `401` answer therefore proves the alias and the CA trust:

```yaml
name: admin-sdk-mock-smoke
services:
  api:
    image: node:24-alpine@<digest>
    networks: [internal]
    command:
      - node
      - -e
      - "fetch('https://admin.googleapis.com/admin/directory/v1/customers/my_customer').then(r => { console.log('api', r.status); process.exit(r.status === 401 ? 0 : 1) }, e => { console.error(e); process.exit(1) })"
  workers:
    image: node:24-alpine@<digest>
    networks: [internal]
    command:
      - node
      - -e
      - "fetch('https://chromemanagement.googleapis.com/v1/customers/my_customer/telemetry/devices').then(r => { console.log('workers', r.status); process.exit(r.status === 401 ? 0 : 1) }, e => { console.error(e); process.exit(1) })"
networks:
  ingress: {}
  internal:
    internal: true
```

Use the same digest as the Dockerfile in both `image` lines.

- [ ] **Step 3: Build and smoke-test the image**

Run:

```bash
docker build -t admin-sdk-mock:ci .
export ADMIN_SDK_MOCK_IMAGE=admin-sdk-mock:ci
docker compose -f compose/smoke.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml up --detach --wait admin-sdk-mock
docker compose -f compose/smoke.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml run --rm api
docker compose -f compose/smoke.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml run --rm workers
docker compose -f compose/smoke.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml exec admin-sdk-mock node bin/mockctl.ts domain create --tier small --seed smoke
docker compose -f compose/smoke.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml down --volumes
```

Expected: `api 401`, `workers 401`, then the domain JSON. If the local Docker daemon is unavailable, record that in the task report and rely on the CI job in Step 4. Do not push without owner approval.

- [ ] **Step 4: Add the image job to CI**

Append to `.github/workflows/ci.yml` under `jobs:`:

```yaml
  image:
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04
    needs: [check]
    permissions:
      contents: read
      packages: write
    env:
      ADMIN_SDK_MOCK_IMAGE: admin-sdk-mock:ci
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - run: docker build -t admin-sdk-mock:ci .
      - name: Smoke-test the Campus Commander overlay
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail
          files="-f compose/smoke.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml"
          docker compose $files up --detach --wait admin-sdk-mock
          docker compose $files run --rm -T api
          docker compose $files run --rm -T workers
          docker compose $files exec -T admin-sdk-mock node bin/mockctl.ts domain create --tier small --seed smoke
          docker compose $files down --volumes
      - if: github.event_name == 'push' && github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
        uses: docker/login-action@v3
        with:
          registry: ghcr.io
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
      - if: github.event_name == 'push' && github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
        name: Publish the image
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail
          for tag in main "${GITHUB_SHA}"; do
            docker tag admin-sdk-mock:ci "ghcr.io/campuscommander/admin-sdk-mock:${tag}"
            docker push "ghcr.io/campuscommander/admin-sdk-mock:${tag}"
          done
          docker inspect --format '{{index .RepoDigests 0}}' ghcr.io/campuscommander/admin-sdk-mock:main
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Document Docker use**

Append to `README.md`:

````markdown
## Run in Docker

```sh
docker compose -f compose/standalone.yml up --detach --wait --build
docker compose -f compose/standalone.yml exec admin-sdk-mock node bin/mockctl.ts domain create --tier medium --seed demo
```

The control API listens on `127.0.0.1:18080`. The Google listener answers on `127.0.0.1:18443`.

## Use with Campus Commander

Combine the overlay with a rendered Campus Commander all-docker Compose file:

```sh
docker compose -f <install>/docker-compose.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml up --detach --wait
```

The overlay adds no file to Campus Commander. It routes the four Google hostnames to the mock inside the `internal` network.

Outside Docker, run the mock with `MOCK_GOOGLE_PORT=443` as a user that may bind port 443.
Map the four Google hostnames to `127.0.0.1` in `/etc/hosts`.
Start each client with `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS` set to the file that `mockctl ca --out <file>` writes.
The `api` and `workers` containers trust the mock CA through `NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS`.
See [docs/campus-commander.md](docs/campus-commander.md) for the full procedure.
````

### Task 22: Scale test

**Files:**
- Create: `test/scale/scale.ts`
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the harness, `READ_SCOPES`, `grantToken`.
- Produces: `npm run test:scale`. It reads `SCALE_TIER` (default `xlarge`) and `SCALE_TIME` (default 600). It prints one JSON report and exits 1 on a failed check.

The test pages exactly as Campus Commander does: 300 devices per page with the same `fields` list, then users at 500, OUs, and telemetry at 1,000. Delays stay on. The time scale compresses them.

Checks:
1. Each enumerated count equals the database count.
2. Seeding finishes in under 10 minutes.
3. The mean server time per page stays under 250 ms of real time.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the scale test**

`test/scale/scale.ts`:

```ts
import { READ_SCOPES } from '../../src/auth/scopes.ts';
import { row } from '../../src/store/sqlite.ts';
import { grantToken, startHarness } from '../../src/testing/harness.ts';

const tier = process.env.SCALE_TIER ?? 'xlarge';
const scale = Number(process.env.SCALE_TIME ?? 600);
const DEVICE_FIELDS =
  'nextPageToken,chromeosdevices(deviceId,serialNumber,model,annotatedAssetId,orgUnitPath,lastSync,annotatedLocation,notes,status)';

const harness = await startHarness({ timing: { scale, delays: true } });
const failures: string[] = [];
try {
  const seedStarted = performance.now();
  const { domain, result } = harness.mock.domains.create({ tier, seed: 'scale' }, Date.now());
  const seedMs = Math.round(performance.now() - seedStarted);
  if (seedMs > 600_000) failures.push(`Seeding took ${seedMs} ms.`);
  harness.mock.domains.updateSettings(domain.customerId, {
    quota: { perMinute: 1_000_000 },
    chromeQuota: { perMinute: 1_000_000 },
  });
  const token = grantToken(harness, domain, READ_SCOPES);
  const count = (sql: string) => Number(row<{ n: number }>(domain.db.prepare(sql).get())?.n);

  async function enumerate(host: string, path: string, key: string, query: Record<string, string | number>) {
    const started = performance.now();
    let items = 0;
    let pages = 0;
    let pageToken: string | undefined;
    do {
      const response = await harness.request({
        host,
        path,
        token,
        query: { ...query, ...(pageToken ? { pageToken } : {}) },
      });
      if (response.status !== 200) throw new Error(`${path} answered ${response.status}: ${response.text.slice(0, 200)}`);
      const body = response.json();
      items += body[key]?.length ?? 0;
      pageToken = body.nextPageToken;
      pages++;
    } while (pageToken);
    const ms = Math.round(performance.now() - started);
    return { items, pages, ms, meanPageMs: Math.round(ms / pages) };
  }

  const devices = await enumerate(
    'admin.googleapis.com',
    '/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/devices/chromeos',
    'chromeosdevices',
    { maxResults: 300, fields: DEVICE_FIELDS },
  );
  const users = await enumerate('admin.googleapis.com', '/admin/directory/v1/users', 'users', {
    customer: 'my_customer',
    maxResults: 500,
  });
  const orgUnits = await enumerate(
    'admin.googleapis.com',
    '/admin/directory/v1/customer/my_customer/orgunits',
    'organizationUnits',
    { type: 'all' },
  );
  const telemetry = await enumerate(
    'chromemanagement.googleapis.com',
    '/v1/customers/my_customer/telemetry/devices',
    'devices',
    { readMask: 'deviceId,batteryInfo,batteryStatusReport', pageSize: 1000 },
  );

  const expected = {
    devices: count('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices'),
    users: count('SELECT count(*) AS n FROM users'),
    orgUnits: count("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM orgunits WHERE path != '/'"),
    telemetry: count("SELECT count(*) AS n FROM devices WHERE status != 'DEPROVISIONED'"),
  };
  const measured = { devices, users, orgUnits, telemetry };
  for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(measured)) {
    if (value.items !== expected[name as keyof typeof expected])
      failures.push(`${name}: ${value.items} of ${expected[name as keyof typeof expected]}.`);
    if (value.meanPageMs - 2000 / scale > 250)
      failures.push(`${name}: mean server time per page ${value.meanPageMs} ms.`);
  }
  console.log(
    JSON.stringify(
      {
        tier,
        scale,
        seed: { ...result, durationMs: seedMs },
        measured,
        rssMb: Math.round(process.memoryUsage().rss / 1_048_576),
        failures,
      },
      null,
      2,
    ),
  );
} finally {
  await harness.close();
}
process.exit(failures.length > 0 ? 1 : 0);
```

The test raises the quota to keep the enumeration inside one bucket. The quota rules have their own tests in Task 15.

Expected: exit 0. Record the seed duration, the page timings, and the memory in the commit message.

### Task 23: Live parity check

**Files:**
- Create: `parity/compare.ts`, `parity/compare.test.ts`, `parity/probes.ts`, `parity/live.ts`, `parity/evidence/` (output)
- Modify: mock modules that a mismatch identifies
- Test: `parity/compare.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the harness, `issueServiceAccount`, `signAssertion`, `SCOPES`.
- Produces: `Shape`, `shapeOf(value)`, `missingPaths(expected, actual)`, `errorSignature(body)`, `redact(value, patterns)`, `Observation { status: number; body: unknown }`, `ProbeResult`, `compareObservations(probe, google, mock): ProbeResult`.
- Produces: `Transport`, `PROBES`, and the `npm run parity` entry point. It reads `PARITY_CREDENTIAL` (a file path) and `PARITY_SUBJECT`.

Authorization: the standing owner authorization covers read-only API checks against `owner-test-domain.example` with the approved DWD credential. Every probe reads data or exchanges tokens. No probe writes to Google.

Credential rules:
- The script reads the credential file and never prints it, logs it, or copies it.
- Evidence holds response shapes, statuses, and error signatures only.
- Redaction replaces customer IDs, email addresses, and the delegated domain before comparison and before writing.

The Easton grant holds three scopes: customer, domain, and OU read-only. The mock side grants the same three. Probes for devices, users, and telemetry therefore compare the `403` scope errors. Their success shapes wait until the owner adds those scopes in Google.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing comparison tests**

`parity/compare.test.ts`:

```ts
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { compareObservations, errorSignature, missingPaths, redact, shapeOf } from './compare.ts';

test('shapes merge array elements and record types', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(shapeOf({ a: 1, b: [{ x: 's' }, { y: true }], c: null }), {
    a: 'number',
    b: [{ x: 'string', y: 'boolean' }],
    c: 'null',
  });
});

test('missingPaths lists what the mock lacks', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    missingPaths(shapeOf({ a: 1, b: { c: 1, d: 2 } }), shapeOf({ a: 1, b: { c: 1 }, e: 3 })),
    ['$.b.d'],
  );
});

test('error signatures cover both envelopes and OAuth errors', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    errorSignature({ error: { code: 403, message: 'm', status: 'S', errors: [{ reason: 'r', domain: 'global', message: 'n' }] } }),
    { code: 403, message: 'm', status: 'S', reason: 'r', domain: 'global', itemMessage: 'n', keys: 'code,errors,message,status' },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(errorSignature({ error: 'unauthorized_client', error_description: 'x' }), {
    error: 'unauthorized_client',
    description: 'x',
  });
});

test('redaction replaces identifiers inside strings', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    redact({ m: 'Customer C01abcdef owns a@b.test at b.test' }, [/b\.test/g]),
    { m: 'Customer <customer> owns <email> at <redacted>' },
  );
});

test('a success comparison flags missing fields and notes extras', () => {
  const result = compareObservations('p', { status: 200, body: { a: 1, b: 2 } }, { status: 200, body: { a: 1, c: 3 } });
  assert.deepEqual(result.mismatches, ['missing $.b']);
  assert.deepEqual(result.notes, ['extra $.c']);
});

test('an error comparison flags every differing signature field', () => {
  const result = compareObservations(
    'p',
    { status: 403, body: { error: { code: 403, message: 'x', errors: [{ reason: 'forbidden', domain: 'global', message: 'x' }] } } },
    { status: 404, body: { error: { code: 404, message: 'y', errors: [{ reason: 'notFound', domain: 'global', message: 'y' }] } } },
  );
  assert.deepEqual(result.mismatches, [
    'status 403 != 404',
    'code 403 != 404',
    'message "x" != "y"',
    'reason "forbidden" != "notFound"',
    'itemMessage "x" != "y"',
  ]);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the comparison**

`parity/compare.ts`:

```ts
export type Shape = string | Shape[] | { [key: string]: Shape };

function merge(a: Shape | undefined, b: Shape): Shape {
  if (a === undefined) return b;
  if (Array.isArray(a) && Array.isArray(b)) return a.length === 0 ? b : b.length === 0 ? a : [merge(a[0], b[0])];
  if (typeof a === 'object' && typeof b === 'object' && !Array.isArray(a) && !Array.isArray(b)) {
    const out: Record<string, Shape> = { ...a };
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(b)) out[key] = merge(out[key], value);
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(out).sort(([x], [y]) => (x < y ? -1 : 1)));
  }
  return a === b ? a : `${String(a)}|${String(b)}`;
}

/** The keys and value types of a JSON value, without the values. */
export function shapeOf(value: unknown): Shape {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    const merged = value.map(shapeOf).reduce<Shape | undefined>((all, item) => merge(all, item), undefined);
    return merged === undefined ? [] : [merged];
  }
  if (value === null) return 'null';
  if (typeof value === 'object')
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(value)
        .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : 1))
        .map(([key, item]) => [key, shapeOf(item)]),
    );
  return typeof value;
}

/** Paths in `expected` that `actual` lacks. */
export function missingPaths(expected: Shape, actual: Shape, path = '$'): string[] {
  if (Array.isArray(expected))
    return Array.isArray(actual) && expected.length > 0 && actual.length > 0
      ? missingPaths(expected[0], actual[0], `${path}[]`)
      : [];
  if (typeof expected !== 'object') return [];
  if (typeof actual !== 'object' || Array.isArray(actual)) return [path];
  return Object.entries(expected).flatMap(([key, value]) =>
    key in actual ? missingPaths(value, actual[key], `${path}.${key}`) : [`${path}.${key}`],
  );
}

/** The fields of an error body that the mock must reproduce. */
export function errorSignature(body: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (body === null || typeof body !== 'object') return { raw: typeof body };
  const value = body as { error?: unknown; error_description?: unknown };
  if (typeof value.error === 'string')
    return { error: value.error, description: value.error_description };
  const error = (value.error ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
  const first = (Array.isArray(error.errors) ? error.errors[0] : undefined) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  const signature: Record<string, unknown> = {
    code: error.code,
    message: error.message,
    status: error.status,
    reason: first?.reason,
    domain: first?.domain,
    itemMessage: first?.message,
    location: first?.location,
    locationType: first?.locationType,
    keys: Object.keys(error).sort().join(','),
  };
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(signature).filter(([, item]) => item !== undefined));
}

const CUSTOMER = /\bC[0-9A-Za-z]{8}\b/g;
const EMAIL = /[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Za-z]{2,}/g;

/** Replace customer IDs, email addresses, and the given patterns in every string. */
export function redact(value: unknown, patterns: readonly RegExp[]): unknown {
  if (typeof value === 'string') {
    let text = value.replace(CUSTOMER, '<customer>').replace(EMAIL, '<email>');
    for (const pattern of patterns) text = text.replace(pattern, '<redacted>');
    return text;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => redact(item, patterns));
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object')
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(value).map(([key, item]) => [key, redact(item, patterns)]));
  return value;
}

export interface Observation {
  status: number;
  body: unknown;
}

export interface ProbeResult {
  probe: string;
  google: { status: number; shape?: Shape; error?: Record<string, unknown> };
  mock: { status: number; shape?: Shape; error?: Record<string, unknown> };
  mismatches: string[];
  notes: string[];
}

const ok = (status: number) => status >= 200 && status < 300;

export function compareObservations(probe: string, google: Observation, mock: Observation): ProbeResult {
  const mismatches: string[] = [];
  const notes: string[] = [];
  if (google.status !== mock.status) mismatches.push(`status ${google.status} != ${mock.status}`);
  const side = (observation: Observation) =>
    ok(observation.status)
      ? { status: observation.status, shape: shapeOf(observation.body) }
      : { status: observation.status, error: errorSignature(observation.body) };
  const g = side(google);
  const m = side(mock);
  if (g.shape && m.shape) {
    mismatches.push(...missingPaths(g.shape, m.shape).map((path) => `missing ${path}`));
    notes.push(...missingPaths(m.shape, g.shape).map((path) => `extra ${path}`));
  } else if (g.error && m.error) {
    for (const key of new Set([...Object.keys(g.error), ...Object.keys(m.error)]))
      if (key !== 'keys' && JSON.stringify(g.error[key]) !== JSON.stringify(m.error[key]))
        mismatches.push(`${key} ${JSON.stringify(g.error[key])} != ${JSON.stringify(m.error[key])}`);
    if (g.error.keys !== m.error.keys) notes.push(`error keys ${String(g.error.keys)} vs ${String(m.error.keys)}`);
  }
  return { probe, google: g, mock: m, mismatches, notes };
}
```

Run: `node --test parity/compare.test.ts`

`parity/probes.ts`:

```ts
import type { Observation } from './compare.ts';

const P = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/admin.directory.';
export const PARITY_SCOPES = {
  customer: `${P}customer.readonly`,
  domain: `${P}domain.readonly`,
  orgunit: `${P}orgunit.readonly`,
  ungranted: `${P}group.readonly`,
};

export interface RequestInput {
  host: string;
  path: string;
  method?: string;
  query?: Record<string, string>;
  token?: string;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  body?: string;
}

/** One side of the comparison: real Google or the mock. */
export interface Transport {
  token(scope: string): Promise<Observation>;
  request(input: RequestInput): Promise<Observation>;
}

const D = '/admin/directory/v1';
const ADMIN = 'admin.googleapis.com';

async function bearer(transport: Transport, scope: string): Promise<string> {
  const response = await transport.token(scope);
  const token = (response.body as { access_token?: string }).access_token;
  if (!token) throw new Error(`The token exchange for ${scope} answered ${response.status}.`);
  return token;
}

export interface Probe {
  name: string;
  run(transport: Transport): Promise<Observation>;
}

export const PROBES: readonly Probe[] = [
  { name: 'token.granted', run: (t) => t.token(PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit) },
  { name: 'token.ungranted', run: (t) => t.token(PARITY_SCOPES.ungranted) },
  {
    name: 'tokeninfo',
    run: async (t) =>
      t.request({
        host: 'oauth2.googleapis.com',
        path: '/tokeninfo',
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit)}` },
      }),
  },
  {
    name: 'customers.get',
    run: async (t) => t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customers/my_customer`, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.customer) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'domains.list',
    run: async (t) => t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/domains`, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.domain) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'domains.list.badFields',
    run: async (t) =>
      t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/domains`, query: { fields: 'bogusField' }, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.domain) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'orgunits.list.allIncludingParent',
    run: async (t) =>
      t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/orgunits`, query: { type: 'all_including_parent' }, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'orgunits.list.children',
    run: async (t) => t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/orgunits`, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'orgunits.get.missing',
    run: async (t) =>
      t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/orgunits/Parity%20Missing%20Unit`, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'orgunits.list.otherCustomer',
    run: async (t) => t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/C00000000/orgunits`, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'orgunits.list.noToken',
    run: (t) => t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/orgunits` }),
  },
  {
    name: 'orgunits.list.badToken',
    run: (t) => t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/orgunits`, token: 'ya29.parity-invalid-token' }),
  },
  {
    name: 'customers.get.wrongScope',
    run: async (t) => t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customers/my_customer`, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'chromeosdevices.list.wrongScope',
    run: async (t) =>
      t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/devices/chromeos`, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'users.list.wrongScope',
    run: async (t) => t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/users`, query: { customer: 'my_customer' }, token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit) }),
  },
  {
    name: 'telemetry.list.wrongScope',
    run: async (t) =>
      t.request({
        host: 'chromemanagement.googleapis.com',
        path: '/v1/customers/my_customer/telemetry/devices',
        query: { readMask: 'deviceId' },
        token: await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit),
      }),
  },
  {
    name: 'batch.orgunits',
    run: async (t) => {
      const token = await bearer(t, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit);
      const list = await t.request({ host: ADMIN, path: `${D}/customer/my_customer/orgunits`, query: { type: 'all' }, token });
      const unit = ((list.body as { organizationUnits?: { orgUnitPath: string }[] }).organizationUnits ?? [])[0];
      const existing = unit ? unit.orgUnitPath.slice(1).split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/') : 'Parity%20Missing%20Unit';
      const part = (id: string, path: string) =>
        `--parity\r\nContent-Type: application/http\r\nContent-ID: <${id}>\r\n\r\nGET ${D}/customer/my_customer/orgunits/${path}\r\n\r\n`;
      const response = await t.request({
        host: 'www.googleapis.com',
        path: '/batch/admin/directory_v1',
        method: 'POST',
        token,
        headers: { 'content-type': 'multipart/mixed; boundary=parity' },
        body: `${part('one', existing)}${part('two', 'Parity%20Missing%20Unit')}--parity--\r\n`,
      });
      const text = String(response.body);
      const PART =
        /Content-ID: <([^>]+)>(?:\r?\n[^\r\n]+)*\r?\n\r?\nHTTP\/1\.1 (\d{3})[^\r\n]*\r?\n(?:[^\r\n]+\r?\n)*\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n--/g;
      const parts = [...text.matchAll(PART)].map((match) => {
        let body: unknown = match[3];
        try {
          body = JSON.parse(match[3]);
        } catch {
          /* The part body is not JSON. */
        }
        return { contentId: match[1], status: Number(match[2]), body };
      });
      return { status: response.status, body: { parts } };
    },
  },
];
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the entry point**

`parity/live.ts`:

```ts
import { sign } from 'node:crypto';
import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { issueServiceAccount } from '../src/auth/accounts.ts';
import { startHarness } from '../src/testing/harness.ts';
import { signAssertion, tokenRequestBody } from '../src/testing/jwt.ts';
import { compareObservations, redact, type Observation } from './compare.ts';
import { PARITY_SCOPES, PROBES, type RequestInput, type Transport } from './probes.ts';

const credentialPath = process.env.PARITY_CREDENTIAL;
const subject = process.env.PARITY_SUBJECT;
if (!credentialPath || !subject) {
  console.error('Set PARITY_CREDENTIAL to the credential file path and PARITY_SUBJECT to the delegated administrator.');
  process.exit(2);
}
const key = JSON.parse(readFileSync(credentialPath, 'utf8')) as {
  client_email: string;
  private_key: string;
  private_key_id: string;
  client_id: string;
  project_id: string;
};

const parse = (text: string): unknown => {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text);
  } catch {
    return text;
  }
};

async function fetchObservation(input: RequestInput, base: (host: string) => string): Promise<Observation> {
  const query = new URLSearchParams(input.query ?? {}).toString();
  const response = await fetch(`${base(input.host)}${input.path}${query ? `?${query}` : ''}`, {
    method: input.method ?? 'GET',
    headers: {
      ...(input.token ? { authorization: `Bearer ${input.token}` } : {}),
      ...input.headers,
    },
    body: input.body,
  });
  return { status: response.status, body: parse(await response.text()) };
}

const google: Transport = {
  async token(scope) {
    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const encode = (value: unknown) => Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(value)).toString('base64url');
    const unsigned = `${encode({ alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT', kid: key.private_key_id })}.${encode({
      iss: key.client_email,
      scope,
      aud: 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',
      iat: now,
      exp: now + 3600,
      sub: subject,
    })}`;
    const assertion = `${unsigned}.${sign('sha256', Buffer.from(unsigned), key.private_key).toString('base64url')}`;
    return fetchObservation(
      {
        host: 'oauth2.googleapis.com',
        path: '/token',
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
        body: tokenRequestBody(assertion),
      },
      (host) => `https://${host}`,
    );
  },
  request: (input) => fetchObservation(input, (host) => `https://${host}`),
};

const harness = await startHarness();
const domain = harness.mock.domains.create({ tier: 'small', seed: 'parity' }, Date.now()).domain;
const account = issueServiceAccount(harness.mock.registry, domain.customerId);
harness.mock.registry.setGrant(domain.customerId, account.client_id, [
  PARITY_SCOPES.customer,
  PARITY_SCOPES.domain,
  PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit,
]);
const mockSubject = `admin@${domain.spec.primaryDomain}`;
const mock: Transport = {
  async token(scope) {
    const response = await harness.request({
      host: 'oauth2.googleapis.com',
      path: '/token',
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
      body: tokenRequestBody(signAssertion(account, { scope, sub: mockSubject })),
    });
    return { status: response.status, body: parse(response.text) };
  },
  async request(input) {
    const response = await harness.request(input);
    return { status: response.status, body: parse(response.text) };
  },
};

const escape = (text: string) => new RegExp(text.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'), 'g');
const googleSecrets = [key.client_id, key.project_id, key.private_key_id, subject.split('@')[1]].map(escape);
const mockSecrets = [account.client_id, account.project_id, account.private_key_id, domain.spec.primaryDomain].map(escape);

const results = [];
try {
  for (const probe of PROBES) {
    const real = redact(await probe.run(google), googleSecrets) as Observation;
    const simulated = redact(await probe.run(mock), mockSecrets) as Observation;
    results.push(compareObservations(probe.name, real, simulated));
  }
} finally {
  await harness.close();
}

const date = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const evidence = {
  recordedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
  fixture: 'approved Easton read-only fixture',
  scopes: [PARITY_SCOPES.customer, PARITY_SCOPES.domain, PARITY_SCOPES.orgunit],
  limitations: [
    'Device, user, and telemetry success shapes need their scopes in the Easton delegation grant.',
    'The Easton tenant holds few entities. Shapes cover the fields Google returned for its data.',
  ],
  results,
};
mkdirSync('parity/evidence', { recursive: true });
writeFileSync(`parity/evidence/live-${date}.json`, `${JSON.stringify(evidence, null, 2)}\n`);
for (const result of results)
  console.log(`${result.mismatches.length === 0 ? 'match' : 'DIFF '} ${result.probe}${result.mismatches.length ? `: ${result.mismatches.join('; ')}` : ''}`);
process.exit(results.some((result) => result.mismatches.length > 0) ? 1 : 0);
```

```markdown
# Calibration record

Updated: <date>.

| Value | Default | Evidence | Status |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Round trip median and 99th percentile | 80 ms, 400 ms | <parity run or measurement> | <verified or open> |
| Batch part cost | 40 ms per wave of 10 | <Easton batch timing> | <verified or open> |
| Concurrency caps | 40 per method, 120 per customer | Google publishes no value | open |
| Chrome Management quota | 2,400 per minute | Google publishes no value | open |
| Chrome device page maximum | 300, larger values clamp | Needs the device scope in the Easton grant | open |
| Unsupported query operator | 400 invalid | Needs the device or user scope in the Easton grant | open |

## Parity results

<one line per probe from parity/evidence/live-<date>.json, with the calibration change that each mismatch caused>
```

````markdown
# Run Campus Commander against the mock

This procedure changes no file in Campus Commander.

## Prepare

1. Start the Docker daemon. `docker version` must print a server version.
2. Build the mock image: `docker build -t admin-sdk-mock:local .`
3. In Campus Commander, follow `deployment/profiles/all-docker/README.md` to build the release, prepare an installation directory, and render `<install>/docker-compose.yml`.

## Start

```sh
export ADMIN_SDK_MOCK_IMAGE=admin-sdk-mock:local
docker compose -f <install>/docker-compose.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml up --detach --wait
```

## Seed and connect

```sh
mock() { docker compose -f <install>/docker-compose.yml -f compose/campus-commander.overlay.yml exec -T admin-sdk-mock node bin/mockctl.ts "$@"; }
mock domain create --tier large --seed acceptance
mock credential <customerId> > mock-credential.json
```

1. Sign in to Campus Commander.
2. Open **Google connection**. Import `mock-credential.json`.
3. Enter the `adminEmail` from `domain create` as the delegated email. Confirm the customer.
4. Open **Devices** and select **Refresh inventory**. Wait for the sync to finish.

## Record

| Check | Result |
| --- | --- |
| Device count in the grid status bar | |
| Full sync duration | |
| `mock quota <customerId>` after the sync | |
| `git -C ~/projects/campus-commander status --short` is empty | |
| Owner observation | |
````
